March 05, 2026

IROSA: Interactive Robot Skill Adaptation using Natural Language

简要说明：该论文介绍了IROSA，一个利用大型语言模型通过自然语言命令进行交互式机器人技能调整的框架，应用于工业环境，侧重于安全性和可解释性，且无需微调。

TLDR: The paper introduces IROSA, a framework leveraging LLMs for interactive robot skill adaptation in industrial settings using natural language commands, focusing on safety and interpretability without fine-tuning.

Abstract: Foundation models have demonstrated impressive capabilities across diverse domains, while imitation learning provides principled methods for robot skill adaptation from limited data. Combining these approaches holds significant promise for direct application to robotics, yet this combination has received limited attention, particularly for industrial deployment. We present a novel framework that enables open-vocabulary skill adaptation through a tool-based architecture, maintaining a protective abstraction layer between the language model and robot hardware. Our approach leverages pre-trained LLMs to select and parameterize specific tools for adapting robot skills without requiring fine-tuning or direct model-to-robot interaction. We demonstrate the framework on a 7-DoF torque-controlled robot performing an industrial bearing ring insertion task, showing successful skill adaptation through natural language commands for speed adjustment, trajectory correction, and obstacle avoidance while maintaining safety, transparency, and interpretability.

摘要（中文）：基础模型已在各个领域展现出令人印象深刻的能力，而模仿学习为机器人技能从有限数据中进行适应提供了有原则的方法。结合这两种方法对于直接应用于机器人技术具有重大希望，但这种结合受到的关注有限，尤其是在工业部署方面。我们提出了一种新颖的框架，该框架通过基于工具的架构实现开放词汇表的技能适应，从而在语言模型和机器人硬件之间维护一个保护性的抽象层。我们的方法利用预训练的LLM来选择和参数化特定工具，以适应机器人技能，而无需进行微调或直接的模型-机器人交互。我们在执行工业轴承环插入任务的7自由度扭矩控制机器人上演示了该框架，展示了通过自然语言命令成功进行的技能适应，包括速度调整、轨迹校正和避障，同时保持安全性、透明性和可解释性。

Authors: Markus Knauer, Samuel Bustamante, Thomas Eiband, Alin Albu-Schäffer, Freek Stulp, João Silvério

Pretrained Vision-Language-Action Models are Surprisingly Resistant to Forgetting in Continual Learning

简要说明：该论文探索了大型预训练视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型中的持续学习，发现与从头开始训练的小型模型相比，它们对遗忘具有惊人的抵抗力，并且从简单的经验回放中获益匪浅。

TLDR: The paper explores continual learning with large pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, finding they are surprisingly resistant to forgetting and benefit significantly from simple experience replay compared to smaller models trained from scratch.

Abstract: Continual learning is a long-standing challenge in robot policy learning, where a policy must acquire new skills over time without catastrophically forgetting previously learned ones. While prior work has extensively studied continual learning in relatively small behavior cloning (BC) policy models trained from scratch, its behavior in modern large-scale pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models remains underexplored. In this work, we found that pretrained VLAs are remarkably resistant to forgetting compared with smaller policy models trained from scratch. Simple Experience Replay (ER) works surprisingly well on VLAs, sometimes achieving zero forgetting even with a small replay data size. Our analysis reveals that pretraining plays a critical role in downstream continual learning performance: large pretrained models mitigate forgetting with a small replay buffer size while maintaining strong forward learning capabilities. Furthermore, we found that VLAs can retain relevant knowledge from prior tasks despite performance degradation during learning new tasks. This knowledge retention enables rapid recovery of seemingly forgotten skills through finetuning. Together, these insights imply that large-scale pretraining fundamentally changes the dynamics of continual learning, enabling models to continually acquire new skills over time with simple replay. Code and more information can be found at https://ut-austin-rpl.github.io/continual-vla

摘要（中文）：持续学习是机器人策略学习中一个长期存在的挑战，策略必须随着时间的推移学习新技能，而不会灾难性地遗忘以前学习过的技能。虽然先前的工作已经广泛研究了在从头开始训练的相对较小的行为克隆（BC）策略模型中的持续学习，但它在现代大规模预训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中的表现仍然未被充分探索。在这项工作中，我们发现与从头开始训练的较小策略模型相比，预训练的VLA模型对遗忘具有显著的抵抗力。简单的经验回放（ER）在VLA模型上表现出乎意料地好，有时甚至可以在较小的回放数据规模下实现零遗忘。我们的分析表明，预训练在下游持续学习性能中起着关键作用：大型预训练模型可以通过较小的回放缓冲区大小来减轻遗忘，同时保持强大的前向学习能力。此外，我们发现VLA模型可以保留来自先前任务的相关知识，即使在新任务学习期间性能下降。这种知识保留使得可以通过微调快速恢复看似被遗忘的技能。综上所述，这些见解表明，大规模预训练从根本上改变了持续学习的动态，使模型能够通过简单的回放随着时间的推移不断学习新技能。代码和更多信息请访问https://ut-austin-rpl.github.io/continual-vla

Authors: Huihan Liu, Changyeon Kim, Bo Liu, Minghuan Liu, Yuke Zhu

Act-Observe-Rewrite: Multimodal Coding Agents as In-Context Policy Learners for Robot Manipulation

简要说明：该论文提出了Act-Observe-Rewrite (AOR)框架，该框架允许LLM通过迭代生成和改进Python控制器代码，并基于视觉观察和结构化的episode结果来改进机器人操作策略，无需演示、奖励工程或梯度更新即可实现高成功率。

TLDR: The paper introduces Act-Observe-Rewrite (AOR), a framework allowing LLMs to improve robot manipulation policies by iteratively generating and refining Python controller code based on visual observations and structured episode outcomes, achieving high success rates without demonstrations, reward engineering, or gradient updates.

Abstract: Can a multimodal language model learn to manipulate physical objects by reasoning about its own failures-without gradient updates, demonstrations, or reward engineering? We argue the answer is yes, under conditions we characterise precisely. We present Act-Observe-Rewrite (AOR), a framework in which an LLM agent improves a robot manipulation policy by synthesising entirely new executable Python controller code between trials, guided by visual observations and structured episode outcomes. Unlike prior work that grounds LLMs in pre-defined skill libraries or uses code generation for one-shot plan synthesis, AOR makes the full low-level motor control implementation the unit of LLM reasoning, enabling the agent to change not just what the robot does, but how it does it. The central claim is that interpretable code as the policy representation creates a qualitatively different kind of in-context learning from opaque neural policies: the agent can diagnose systematic failures and rewrite their causes. We validate this across three robosuite manipulation tasks and report promising results, with the agent achieving high success rates without demonstrations, reward engineering, or gradient updates.

摘要（中文）：多模态语言模型能否通过推理自身失败的原因来学习操纵物体，而无需梯度更新、演示或奖励工程？我们认为答案是肯定的，但需满足我们精确描述的条件。我们提出了一种名为Act-Observe-Rewrite（AOR）的框架，在该框架中，LLM智能体通过在试验之间合成全新的可执行Python控制器代码来改进机器人操纵策略，并以视觉观察和结构化的情节结果作为指导。与以往将LLM植根于预定义技能库或使用代码生成进行一次性计划合成的工作不同，AOR将完整的低级电机控制实现作为LLM推理的单元，使智能体不仅可以改变机器人*做什么*，还可以改变机器人*如何做*。核心论点是，可解释的代码作为策略表示，与不透明的神经策略相比，创建了一种本质上不同的上下文学习：智能体可以诊断系统性失败并重新编写其原因。我们在三个robosuite操纵任务中验证了这一点，并报告了有前景的结果，智能体无需演示、奖励工程或梯度更新即可实现高成功率。

Authors: Vaishak Kumar

PROSPECT: Unified Streaming Vision-Language Navigation via Semantic--Spatial Fusion and Latent Predictive Representation

简要说明：PROSPECT 是一种新颖的 VLN 代理，它融合了来自 SigLIP 和 CUT3R 的语义和空间特征，并结合了潜在的预测表示学习，在 VLN 基准测试和真实机器人部署中实现了最先进的性能。

TLDR: PROSPECT is a novel VLN agent that fuses semantic and spatial features from SigLIP and CUT3R with latent predictive representation learning, achieving state-of-the-art performance on VLN benchmarks and real robot deployment.

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced zero-shot end-to-end Vision-Language Navigation (VLN), yet robust navigation requires not only semantic understanding but also predictive modeling of environment dynamics and spatial structure. We propose PROSPECT, a unified streaming navigation agent that couples a streaming Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policy with latent predictive representation learning. PROSPECT uses CUT3R as a streaming 3D foundation spatial encoder to produce long-context, absolute-scale spatial features, and fuses them with SigLIP semantic features via cross-attention. During training, we introduce learnable stream query tokens that query the streaming context and predict next-step 2D and 3D latent features (rather than pixels or explicit modalities), supervised in the latent spaces of frozen SigLIP and CUT3R teachers. The predictive branch shapes internal representations without inference overhead. Experiments on VLN-CE benchmarks and real-robot deployment demonstrate state-of-the-art performance and improved long-horizon robustness under diverse lighting. We will release code for the community soon.

摘要（中文）：多模态大型语言模型（MLLMs）极大地促进了零样本端到端视觉语言导航（VLN）的发展，然而，稳健的导航不仅需要语义理解，还需要对环境动态和空间结构的预测建模。我们提出了PROSPECT，一个统一的流式导航智能体，它将流式视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略与潜在预测表示学习相结合。PROSPECT使用CUT3R作为流式3D基础空间编码器，生成长上下文、绝对尺度的空间特征，并通过交叉注意力将其与SigLIP语义特征融合。在训练过程中，我们引入可学习的流查询令牌，用于查询流式上下文并预测下一步的2D和3D潜在特征（而非像素或显式模态），并在冻结的SigLIP和CUT3R教师模型的潜在空间中进行监督。预测分支塑造了内部表示，且不增加推理开销。在VLN-CE基准测试和真实机器人部署上的实验表明，该方法具有最先进的性能，并在不同光照条件下提高了长期导航的稳健性。我们将很快向社区发布代码。

Authors: Zehua Fan, Wenqi Lyu, Wenxuan Song, Linge Zhao, Yifei Yang, Xi Wang, Junjie He, Lida Huang, Haiyan Liu, Bingchuan Sun, Guangjun Bao, Xuanyao Mao, Liang Xu, Yan Wang, Feng Gao

Proact-VL: A Proactive VideoLLM for Real-Time AI Companions

简要说明：该论文介绍了Proact-VL，一个使用多模态语言模型创建主动式、实时AI伴侣的框架，并在一个新的Live Gaming Benchmark上对其进行了评估，在延迟、质量和视频理解方面取得了可喜的成果。

TLDR: The paper introduces Proact-VL, a framework using multimodal language models for creating proactive, real-time AI companions, and evaluates it on a new Live Gaming Benchmark with promising results in latency, quality, and video understanding.

Abstract: Proactive and real-time interactive experiences are essential for human-like AI companions, yet face three key challenges: (1) achieving low-latency inference under continuous streaming inputs, (2) autonomously deciding when to respond, and (3) controlling both quality and quantity of generated content to meet real-time constraints. In this work, we instantiate AI companions through two gaming scenarios, commentator and guide, selected for their suitability for automatic evaluation. We introduce the Live Gaming Benchmark, a large-scale dataset with three representative scenarios: solo commentary, co-commentary, and user guidance, and present Proact-VL, a general framework that shapes multimodal language models into proactive, real-time interactive agents capable of human-like environment perception and interaction. Extensive experiments show Proact-VL achieves superior response latency and quality while maintaining strong video understanding capabilities, demonstrating its practicality for real-time interactive applications.

摘要（中文）：主动且实时的互动体验对于类人AI伴侣至关重要，但面临三个关键挑战：（1）在连续流输入下实现低延迟推理，（2）自主决定何时响应，以及（3）控制生成内容的质量和数量以满足实时约束。 在这项工作中，我们通过两种游戏场景（解说员和引导员）实例化AI伴侣，选择它们是因为它们适合自动评估。我们引入了Live Gaming Benchmark，这是一个大规模数据集，包含三个代表性场景：单人解说、合作解说和用户引导，并提出了Proact-VL，这是一个通用框架，将多模态语言模型塑造成主动、实时的交互式代理，能够进行类人环境感知和交互。 大量实验表明，Proact-VL实现了卓越的响应延迟和质量，同时保持了强大的视频理解能力，证明了其在实时交互应用中的实用性。

Authors: Weicai Yan, Yuhong Dai, Qi Ran, Haodong Li, Wang Lin, Hao Liao, Xing Xie, Tao Jin, Jianxun Lian

Beyond Language Modeling: An Exploration of Multimodal Pretraining

简要说明：该论文从零开始探索多模态预训练，重点研究了视觉表示、模态协同作用、世界建模涌现以及基于MoE的有效缩放等关键见解，表明视觉相比语言更需要大量数据。

TLDR: This paper explores multimodal pretraining from scratch, identifying key insights on visual representations, modality synergy, world modeling emergence, and MoE-based efficient scaling, highlighting a significant data-hungry nature of vision compared to language.

Abstract: The visual world offers a critical axis for advancing foundation models beyond language. Despite growing interest in this direction, the design space for native multimodal models remains opaque. We provide empirical clarity through controlled, from-scratch pretraining experiments, isolating the factors that govern multimodal pretraining without interference from language pretraining. We adopt the Transfusion framework, using next-token prediction for language and diffusion for vision, to train on diverse data including text, video, image-text pairs, and even action-conditioned video. Our experiments yield four key insights: (i) Representation Autoencoder (RAE) provides an optimal unified visual representation by excelling at both visual understanding and generation; (ii) visual and language data are complementary and yield synergy for downstream capabilities; (iii) unified multimodal pretraining leads naturally to world modeling, with capabilities emerging from general training; and (iv) Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) enables efficient and effective multimodal scaling while naturally inducing modality specialization. Through IsoFLOP analysis, we compute scaling laws for both modalities and uncover a scaling asymmetry: vision is significantly more data-hungry than language. We demonstrate that the MoE architecture harmonizes this scaling asymmetry by providing the high model capacity required by language while accommodating the data-intensive nature of vision, paving the way for truly unified multimodal models.

摘要（中文）：视觉世界为推动基础模型超越语言提供了一个关键维度。尽管对这一方向的兴趣日益增长，但原生多模态模型的设计空间仍然不明朗。我们通过受控的、从头开始的预训练实验，分离出控制多模态预训练的因素，避免语言预训练的干扰，从而提供经验上的清晰性。我们采用 Transfusion 框架，使用下一词预测进行语言建模，扩散模型进行视觉建模，并在包含文本、视频、图像-文本对，甚至是以动作为条件的视频等多样化数据上进行训练。我们的实验产生了四个关键见解：（i）表征自编码器（RAE）通过在视觉理解和生成方面表现出色，提供了一种最佳的统一视觉表征；（ii）视觉和语言数据相互补充，并为下游能力产生协同效应；（iii）统一的多模态预训练自然而然地导致世界建模，其能力从通用训练中涌现；（iv）混合专家（MoE）实现了高效且有效的多模态扩展，同时自然地引发模态专业化。通过 IsoFLOP 分析，我们计算了两种模态的缩放法则，并发现了一种缩放不对称性：视觉对数据的需求明显高于语言。我们证明了 MoE 架构通过提供语言所需的高模型容量，同时适应视觉的数据密集型特性，从而调和了这种缩放不对称性，为真正统一的多模态模型铺平了道路。

Authors: Shengbang Tong, David Fan, John Nguyen, Ellis Brown, Gaoyue Zhou, Shengyi Qian, Boyang Zheng, Théophane Vallaeys, Junlin Han, Rob Fergus, Naila Murray, Marjan Ghazvininejad, Mike Lewis, Nicolas Ballas, Amir Bar, Michael Rabbat, Jakob Verbeek, Luke Zettlemoyer, Koustuv Sinha, Yann LeCun, Saining Xie

OmniPlanner: Universal Exploration and Inspection Path Planning across Robot Morphologies

简要说明：OmniPlanner是一个统一的路径规划框架，用于在空中、地面和水下机器人之间进行自主探索和检查，在各种环境中都表现出强大的性能并提高了效率。

TLDR: OmniPlanner is a unified path planning framework for autonomous exploration and inspection across aerial, ground, and underwater robots, demonstrating robust performance and improved efficiency in diverse environments.

Abstract: Autonomous robotic systems are increasingly deployed for mapping, monitoring, and inspection in complex and unstructured environments. However, most existing path planning approaches remain domain-specific (i.e., either on air, land, or sea), limiting their scalability and cross-platform applicability. This article presents OmniPlanner, a unified planning framework for autonomous exploration and inspection across aerial, ground, and underwater robots. The method integrates volumetric exploration and viewpoint-based inspection, alongside target reach behaviors within a single modular architecture, complemented by a platform abstraction layer that captures morphology-specific sensing, traversability and motion constraints. This enables the same planning strategy to generalize across distinct mobility domains with minimal retuning. The framework is validated through extensive simulation studies and field deployments in underground mines, industrial facilities, forests, submarine bunkers, and structured outdoor environments. Across these diverse scenarios, OmniPlanner demonstrates robust performance, consistent cross-domain generalization, and improved exploration and inspection efficiency compared to representative state-of-the-art baselines.

摘要（中文）：自主机器人系统正越来越多地部署于复杂且非结构化环境中的地图绘制、监控和检查任务。然而，当前大多数路径规划方法仍然是领域特定的（即，空中、陆地或水下），限制了其可扩展性和跨平台适用性。本文提出了一种统一的规划框架OmniPlanner，用于空中、地面和水下机器人的自主探索和检查。该方法在一个模块化架构中集成了体积探索和基于视点的检查，并结合了目标到达行为，同时辅以平台抽象层，该抽象层捕获了形态特定的传感、 traversability 和运动约束。这使得相同的规划策略能够以最小的调整推广到不同的移动领域。通过大量的模拟研究和在地下矿井、工业设施、森林、潜艇掩体和结构化户外环境中的现场部署验证了该框架。在这些不同的场景中，OmniPlanner展现了稳健的性能、一致的跨领域泛化能力，以及相较于代表性的最先进基线而言的更优的探索和检查效率。

Authors: Angelos Zacharia, Mihir Dharmadhikari, Mohit Singh, Kostas Alexis

VANGUARD: Vehicle-Anchored Ground Sample Distance Estimation for UAVs in GPS-Denied Environments

简要说明：该论文介绍了 VANGUARD，一种用于 GPS 受限环境中无人机的几何感知技能，通过车辆检测估计地面采样距离 (GSD)，帮助基于 LLM/VLM 的智能体提高空间推理能力，减少尺度幻觉。

TLDR: The paper introduces VANGUARD, a geometric perception skill for UAVs in GPS-denied environments that estimates Ground Sample Distance (GSD) using vehicle detection, helping LLM/VLM-based agents improve spatial reasoning and reducing scale hallucinations.

Abstract: Autonomous aerial robots operating in GPS-denied or communication-degraded environments frequently lose access to camera metadata and telemetry, leaving onboard perception systems unable to recover the absolute metric scale of the scene. As LLM/VLM-based planners are increasingly adopted as high-level agents for embodied systems, their ability to reason about physical dimensions becomes safety-critical -- yet our experiments show that five state-of-the-art VLMs suffer from spatial scale hallucinations, with median area estimation errors exceeding 50%. We propose VANGUARD, a lightweight, deterministic Geometric Perception Skill designed as a callable tool that any LLM-based agent can invoke to recover Ground Sample Distance (GSD) from ubiquitous environmental anchors: small vehicles detected via oriented bounding boxes, whose modal pixel length is robustly estimated through kernel density estimation and converted to GSD using a pre-calibrated reference length. The tool returns both a GSD estimate and a composite confidence score, enabling the calling agent to autonomously decide whether to trust the measurement or fall back to alternative strategies. On the DOTA~v1.5 benchmark, VANGUARD achieves 6.87% median GSD error on 306~images. Integrated with SAM-based segmentation for downstream area measurement, the pipeline yields 19.7% median error on a 100-entry benchmark -- with 2.6x lower category dependence and 4x fewer catastrophic failures than the best VLM baseline -- demonstrating that equipping agents with deterministic geometric tools is essential for safe autonomous spatial reasoning.

摘要（中文）：在GPS拒止或通信降级环境中运行的自主飞行机器人，经常会失去对相机元数据和遥测信息的访问，导致机载感知系统无法恢复场景的绝对尺度。由于基于LLM/VLM的规划器正日益被采纳为具身系统的高级代理，它们对物理维度的推理能力变得至关重要，然而我们的实验表明，五种最先进的VLM存在空间尺度幻觉，面积估计的中位数误差超过50%。我们提出了VANGUARD，一种轻量级、确定性的几何感知技能，设计成可调用的工具，任何基于LLM的代理都可以调用它，以便从普遍存在的环境锚点（通过有向边界框检测到的小型车辆）恢复地面采样距离(GSD)。这些车辆的模态像素长度通过核密度估计得到稳健估计，并使用预先校准的参考长度转换为GSD。该工具返回GSD估计值和一个综合置信度评分，使调用代理能够自主决定是否信任该测量值或回退到替代策略。在DOTA~v1.5基准测试中，VANGUARD在306张图像上实现了6.87%的中位数GSD误差。与基于SAM的分割集成用于下游面积测量后，该流程在100个条目的基准测试中产生19.7%的中位数误差，其类别依赖性降低了2.6倍，灾难性故障减少了4倍，优于最佳VLM基线，证明了为代理配备确定性几何工具对于安全的自主空间推理至关重要。

Authors: Yifei Chen, Xupeng Chen, Feng Wang, Niangang Jiao, Jiayin Liu

RoboLight: A Dataset with Linearly Composable Illumination for Robotic Manipulation

简要说明：该论文介绍了RoboLight，这是一个新的真实和合成数据集，用于在系统变化的光照条件下进行机器人操作，其特点是定制的Light Cube和HDR图像捕获。

TLDR: The paper introduces RoboLight, a new real-world and synthetic dataset for robotic manipulation under systematically varied lighting conditions, featuring a custom-built Light Cube and HDR image capture.

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce RoboLight, the first real-world robotic manipulation dataset capturing synchronized episodes under systematically varied lighting conditions. RoboLight consists of two components. (a) RoboLight-Real contains 2,800 real-world episodes collected in our custom Light Cube setup, a calibrated system equipped with eight programmable RGB LED lights. It includes structured illumination variation along three independently controlled dimensions: color, direction, and intensity. Each dimension is paired with a dedicated task featuring objects of diverse geometries and materials to induce perceptual challenges. All image data are recorded in high-dynamic-range (HDR) format to preserve radiometric accuracy. Leveraging the linearity of light transport, we introduce (b) RoboLight-Synthetic, comprising 196,000 episodes synthesized through interpolation in the HDR image space of RoboLight-Real. In principle, RoboLight-Synthetic can be arbitrarily expanded by refining the interpolation granularity. We further verify the dataset quality through qualitative analysis and real-world policy roll-outs, analyzing task difficulty, distributional diversity, and the effectiveness of synthesized data. We additionally demonstrate three representative use cases of the proposed dataset. The full dataset, along with the system software and hardware design, will be released as open-source to support continued research.

摘要（中文）：本文介绍了RoboLight，这是首个真实世界的机器人操作数据集，它捕捉了在系统性变化的照明条件下同步的 episodes。RoboLight 由两部分组成。(a) RoboLight-Real 包含 2,800 个真实世界 episodes，这些 episodes 在我们定制的 Light Cube 设置中收集，该设置是一个配备八个可编程 RGB LED 灯的校准系统。它包括沿着三个独立控制的维度（颜色、方向和强度）的结构化照明变化。每个维度都与一个专门的任务配对，该任务以具有不同几何形状和材料的物体为特征，以引发感知挑战。所有图像数据均以高动态范围 (HDR) 格式记录，以保持辐射测量的准确性。利用光传输的线性特性，我们引入了 (b) RoboLight-Synthetic，它包含通过在 RoboLight-Real 的 HDR 图像空间中进行插值而合成的 196,000 个 episodes。原则上，可以通过细化插值粒度来任意扩展 RoboLight-Synthetic。我们通过定性分析和真实世界策略部署进一步验证数据集质量，分析任务难度、分布多样性和合成数据的有效性。此外，我们还展示了所提出的数据集的三个代表性用例。完整的数据集，连同系统软件和硬件设计，将以开源形式发布，以支持持续的研究。

Authors: Shutong Jin, Jin Yang, Muhammad Zahid, Florian T. Pokorny

PRAM-R: A Perception-Reasoning-Action-Memory Framework with LLM-Guided Modality Routing for Adaptive Autonomous Driving

简要说明：本文介绍了PRAM-R，一个由LLM引导的自适应自动驾驶框架，它能智能地路由和权衡多模态传感器数据，通过分层记忆系统在减少计算的同时保持性能。

TLDR: This paper introduces PRAM-R, an LLM-guided framework for adaptive autonomous driving that intelligently routes and weights multimodal sensor data, reducing computation while maintaining performance through a hierarchical memory system.

Abstract: Multimodal perception enables robust autonomous driving but incurs unnecessary computational cost when all sensors remain active. This paper presents PRAM-R, a unified Perception-Reasoning-Action-Memory framework with LLM-Guided Modality Routing for adaptive autonomous driving. PRAM-R adopts an asynchronous dual-loop design: a fast reactive loop for perception and control, and a slow deliberative loop for reasoning-driven modality selection and memory updates. An LLM router selects and weights modalities using environmental context and sensor diagnostics, while a hierarchical memory module preserves temporal consistency and supports long-term adaptation. We conduct a two-stage evaluation: (1) synthetic stress tests for stability analysis and (2) real-world validation on the nuScenes dataset. Synthetic stress tests confirm 87.2% reduction in routing oscillations via hysteresis-based stabilization. Real-world validation on nuScenes shows 6.22% modality reduction with 20% memory recall while maintaining comparable trajectory accuracy to full-modality baselines in complex urban scenarios. Our work demonstrates that LLM-augmented architectures with hierarchical memory achieve efficient, adaptive multimodal perception in autonomous driving.

摘要（中文）：多模态感知技术能够实现稳健的自动驾驶，但当所有传感器保持激活状态时，会产生不必要的计算成本。 本文提出了一种统一的感知-推理-行动-记忆框架PRAM-R，利用LLM引导的模态路由实现自适应自动驾驶。PRAM-R采用异步双环设计：一个用于感知和控制的快速反应环，以及一个用于推理驱动的模态选择和记忆更新的慢速深思熟虑环。一个LLM路由器使用环境上下文和传感器诊断来选择和加权模态，而一个分层记忆模块则保持时间一致性并支持长期适应。 我们进行了两阶段评估：（1）用于稳定性分析的合成压力测试；（2）在nuScenes数据集上的真实世界验证。合成压力测试证实，通过基于滞后的稳定化，路由振荡减少了87.2%。在nuScenes上的真实世界验证表明，在复杂的城市环境中，在保持与全模态基线相当的轨迹精度情况下，模态减少了6.22%，同时记忆召回率为20%。 我们的工作证明，具有分层记忆的LLM增强架构能够在自动驾驶中实现高效、自适应的多模态感知。

GarmentPile++: Affordance-Driven Cluttered Garments Retrieval with Vision-Language Reasoning

简要说明：本文介绍了一种用于从衣物堆中进行机器人衣物检索的视觉-语言推理流程，使用分割、可供性感知和双臂协作来处理复杂场景。

TLDR: This paper introduces a vision-language reasoning pipeline for robotic garment retrieval from a pile, using segmentation, affordance perception, and dual-arm cooperation to handle complex scenarios.

Abstract: Garment manipulation has attracted increasing attention due to its critical role in home-assistant robotics. However, the majority of existing garment manipulation works assume an initial state consisting of only one garment, while piled garments are far more common in real-world settings. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel garment retrieval pipeline that can not only follow language instruction to execute safe and clean retrieval but also guarantee exactly one garment is retrieved per attempt, establishing a robust foundation for the execution of downstream tasks (e.g., folding, hanging, wearing). Our pipeline seamlessly integrates vision-language reasoning with visual affordance perception, fully leveraging the high-level reasoning and planning capabilities of VLMs alongside the generalization power of visual affordance for low-level actions. To enhance the VLM's comprehensive awareness of each garment's state within a garment pile, we employ visual segmentation model (SAM2) to execute object segmentation on the garment pile for aiding VLM-based reasoning with sufficient visual cues. A mask fine-tuning mechanism is further integrated to address scenarios where the initial segmentation results are suboptimal. In addition, a dual-arm cooperation framework is deployed to address cases involving large or long garments, as well as excessive garment sagging caused by incorrect grasping point determination, both of which are strenuous for a single arm to handle. The effectiveness of our pipeline are consistently demonstrated across diverse tasks and varying scenarios in both real-world and simulation environments. Project page: https://garmentpile2.github.io/.

摘要（中文）：服装操控由于其在家庭助手机器人中的关键作用而日益受到关注。然而，现有的大多数服装操控工作都假设一个初始状态，其中仅包含一件服装，而在现实世界中，堆叠的服装更为常见。为了弥合这一差距，我们提出了一种新颖的服装检索流程，该流程不仅可以遵循语言指令执行安全且干净的检索，而且可以保证每次尝试仅检索一件服装，为下游任务（例如，折叠、悬挂、穿着）的执行建立稳固的基础。我们的流程无缝地集成了视觉-语言推理与视觉可供性感知，充分利用了视觉语言模型（VLM）的高级推理和规划能力，以及视觉可供性在低级动作上的泛化能力。为了增强VLM对服装堆中每件服装状态的全面感知，我们采用视觉分割模型（SAM2）对服装堆执行目标分割，从而为基于VLM的推理提供充分的视觉线索。进一步集成了一种掩码微调机制，以解决初始分割结果次优的情况。此外，还部署了一个双臂协作框架来处理涉及大型或长型服装，以及因抓取点确定不正确而导致服装过度下垂的情况，这些情况对于单臂来说都非常吃力。我们的流程的有效性在真实世界和仿真环境中的各种任务和不同场景中都得到了持续的证明。项目主页：https://garmentpile2.github.io/。

SaFeR: Safety-Critical Scenario Generation for Autonomous Driving Test via Feasibility-Constrained Token Resampling

简要说明：该论文介绍了一种名为SaFeR的新型自动驾驶测试场景生成方法，该方法通过可行性约束的令牌重采样策略，平衡了对抗性临界性、物理可行性和行为真实性。

TLDR: The paper introduces SaFeR, a novel scenario generation method for autonomous driving testing that balances adversarial criticality, physical feasibility, and behavioral realism through a feasibility-constrained token resampling strategy.

Abstract: Safety-critical scenario generation is crucial for evaluating autonomous driving systems. However, existing approaches often struggle to balance three conflicting objectives: adversarial criticality, physical feasibility, and behavioral realism. To bridge this gap, we propose SaFeR: safety-critical scenario generation for autonomous driving test via feasibility-constrained token resampling. We first formulate traffic generation as a discrete next token prediction problem, employing a Transformer-based model as a realism prior to capture naturalistic driving distributions. To capture complex interactions while effectively mitigating attention noise, we propose a novel differential attention mechanism within the realism prior. Building on this prior, SaFeR implements a novel resampling strategy that induces adversarial behaviors within a high-probability trust region to maintain naturalism, while enforcing a feasibility constraint derived from the Largest Feasible Region (LFR). By approximating the LFR via offline reinforcement learning, SaFeR effectively prevents the generation of theoretically inevitable collisions. Closed-loop experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset and nuPlan demonstrate that SaFeR significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a higher solution rate and superior kinematic realism while maintaining strong adversarial effectiveness.

摘要（中文）：安全关键场景生成对于评估自动驾驶系统至关重要。然而，现有方法通常难以平衡三个相互冲突的目标：对抗性临界性、物理可行性以及行为真实性。为了弥合这一差距，我们提出了 SaFeR：通过可行性约束的令牌重采样实现的自动驾驶测试安全关键场景生成。我们首先将交通生成形式化为一种离散的下一个令牌预测问题，采用基于Transformer的模型作为真实性先验，以捕捉自然驾驶分布。为了捕捉复杂的交互，同时有效缓解注意力噪声，我们在真实性先验中提出了一种新颖的差分注意力机制。在此先验的基础上，SaFeR实施了一种新颖的重采样策略，该策略在高概率信任区域内诱导对抗行为以保持自然性，同时强制执行源自最大可行区域 (LFR) 的可行性约束。通过离线强化学习逼近 LFR，SaFeR 有效地阻止了理论上不可避免的碰撞的生成。在 Waymo Open Motion Dataset 和 nuPlan 上的闭环实验表明，SaFeR 显著优于最先进的基线，在保持强大的对抗有效性的同时，实现了更高的求解率和卓越的运动学真实性。

Authors: Jinlong Cui, Fenghua Liang, Guo Yang, Chengcheng Tang, Jianxun Cui

Force-Aware Residual DAgger via Trajectory Editing for Precision Insertion with Impedance Control

简要说明：该论文介绍了TER-DAgger，一种力感知的模仿学习框架，结合轨迹编辑、力感知的故障预测和阻抗控制，以提高精密插入任务的成功率并减少人工监控。

TLDR: The paper introduces TER-DAgger, a force-aware imitation learning framework that combines trajectory editing, force-aware failure anticipation, and impedance control for improving the success rate and reducing human monitoring in precision insertion tasks.

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) has shown strong potential for contact-rich precision insertion tasks. However, its practical deployment is often hindered by covariate shift and the need for continuous expert monitoring to recover from failures during execution. In this paper, we propose Trajectory Editing Residual Dataset Aggregation (TER-DAgger), a scalable and force-aware human-in-the-loop imitation learning framework that mitigates covariate shift by learning residual policies through optimization-based trajectory editing. This approach smoothly fuses policy rollouts with human corrective trajectories, providing consistent and stable supervision. Second, we introduce a force-aware failure anticipation mechanism that triggers human intervention only when discrepancies arise between predicted and measured end-effector forces, significantly reducing the requirement for continuous expert monitoring. Third, all learned policies are executed within a Cartesian impedance control framework, ensuring compliant and safe behavior during contact-rich interactions. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world precision insertion tasks show that TER-DAgger improves the average success rate by over 37\% compared to behavior cloning, human-guided correction, retraining, and fine-tuning baselines, demonstrating its effectiveness in mitigating covariate shift and enabling scalable deployment in contact-rich manipulation.

摘要（中文）：模仿学习（IL）已展现出在富接触精密插入任务中的强大潜力。然而，其实际部署常因协变量偏移问题以及在执行过程中需要专家持续监控以从失败中恢复而受阻。在本文中，我们提出了一种可扩展且具有力感知的闭环模仿学习框架——轨迹编辑残差数据集聚合（TER-DAgger），该框架通过基于优化的轨迹编辑学习残差策略来缓解协变量偏移问题。该方法平滑地融合了策略展开与人工校正轨迹，提供了持续且稳定的监督。其次，我们引入了一种力感知型故障预测机制，该机制仅在预测和测量的末端执行器力之间出现差异时才触发人工干预，从而显著降低了对专家持续监控的需求。第三，所有学习到的策略都在笛卡尔阻抗控制框架内执行，从而确保在富接触交互过程中的顺从性和安全性。在仿真和真实世界精密插入任务中的大量实验表明，TER-DAgger相较于行为克隆、人工引导校正、重训练和微调等基线方法，平均成功率提高了37\%以上，证明了其在缓解协变量偏移和实现富接触操作中可扩展部署方面的有效性。

Authors: Yiou Huang, Ma Ning, Weichu Zhao, Zinuo Liu, Jun Sun, Qiufeng Wang, Yaran Chen

Self-adapting Robotic Agents through Online Continual Reinforcement Learning with World Model Feedback

简要说明：本文介绍了一个持续强化学习框架，该框架允许机器人使用世界模型预测残差来触发微调，从而在线适应，并在模拟和现实世界的机器人平台上进行了演示。

TLDR: This paper introduces a continual reinforcement learning framework that allows robots to adapt online using world model prediction residuals to trigger finetuning, demonstrating it on simulated and real-world robotic platforms.

Abstract: As learning-based robotic controllers are typically trained offline and deployed with fixed parameters, their ability to cope with unforeseen changes during operation is limited. Biologically inspired, this work presents a framework for online Continual Reinforcement Learning that enables automated adaptation during deployment. Building on DreamerV3, a model-based Reinforcement Learning algorithm, the proposed method leverages world model prediction residuals to detect out-of-distribution events and automatically trigger finetuning. Adaptation progress is monitored using both task-level performance signals and internal training metrics, allowing convergence to be assessed without external supervision and domain knowledge. The approach is validated on a variety of contemporary continuous control problems, including a quadruped robot in high-fidelity simulation, and a real-world model vehicle. Relevant metrics and their interpretation are presented and discussed, as well as resulting trade-offs described. The results sketch out how autonomous robotic agents could once move beyond static training regimes toward adaptive systems capable of self-reflection and -improvement during operation, just like their biological counterparts.

摘要（中文）：基于学习的机器人控制器通常离线训练并以固定参数部署，因此应对运行期间意外变化的能力有限。受生物学启发，本文提出了一种在线持续强化学习框架，能够在部署期间实现自动适应。该方法基于DreamerV3（一种基于模型的强化学习算法），利用世界模型预测残差来检测分布外事件，并自动触发微调。适应过程的进展通过任务级性能信号和内部训练指标进行监控，从而可以在没有外部监督和领域知识的情况下评估收敛性。该方法在一系列当代连续控制问题上进行了验证，包括高保真仿真中的四足机器人和真实世界的模型车辆。文中呈现并讨论了相关的指标及其解释，以及由此产生的权衡。结果表明，自主机器人代理如何能摆脱静态训练模式，转变为能够在运行期间进行自我反思和自我改进的自适应系统，就像它们的生物学对应物一样。

Authors: Fabian Domberg, Georg Schildbach

Structural Action Transformer for 3D Dexterous Manipulation

简要说明：本文提出了结构化动作Transformer (SAT)，一种新颖的结构中心视角方法，通过将动作重新定义为无序的关节轨迹序列并使用具身关节代码本，解决了3D灵巧操作中跨具身技能迁移的问题，实现了卓越的样本效率和有效的迁移。

TLDR: The paper introduces Structural Action Transformer (SAT), a novel approach using a structural-centric perspective to address cross-embodiment skill transfer in 3D dexterous manipulation, achieving superior sample efficiency and effective transfer by reframing actions as unordered joint-wise trajectories and using an Embodied Joint Codebook.

Abstract: Achieving human-level dexterity in robots via imitation learning from heterogeneous datasets is hindered by the challenge of cross-embodiment skill transfer, particularly for high-DoF robotic hands. Existing methods, often relying on 2D observations and temporal-centric action representation, struggle to capture 3D spatial relations and fail to handle embodiment heterogeneity. This paper proposes the Structural Action Transformer (SAT), a new 3D dexterous manipulation policy that challenges this paradigm by introducing a structural-centric perspective. We reframe each action chunk not as a temporal sequence, but as a variable-length, unordered sequence of joint-wise trajectories. This structural formulation allows a Transformer to natively handle heterogeneous embodiments, treating the joint count as a variable sequence length. To encode structural priors and resolve ambiguity, we introduce an Embodied Joint Codebook that embeds each joint's functional role and kinematic properties. Our model learns to generate these trajectories from 3D point clouds via a continuous-time flow matching objective. We validate our approach by pre-training on large-scale heterogeneous datasets and fine-tuning on simulation and real-world dexterous manipulation tasks. Our method consistently outperforms all baselines, demonstrating superior sample efficiency and effective cross-embodiment skill transfer. This structural-centric representation offers a new path toward scaling policies for high-DoF, heterogeneous manipulators.

摘要（中文）：通过模仿学习从异构数据集中实现机器人的人类水平灵巧性面临跨机体技能迁移的挑战，尤其对于高自由度机器人手而言。现有的方法通常依赖于2D观测和以时间为中心的动作表示，难以捕捉 3D 空间关系，并且无法处理机体异构性。本文提出了一种结构化动作Transformer（SAT），一种新的 3D 灵巧操作策略，通过引入以结构为中心的视角来挑战这一范式。我们重新将每个动作块定义为关节方向轨迹的可变长度、无序序列，而不是时间序列。这种结构化表示允许Transformer原生处理异构机体，将关节数量视为可变序列长度。为了编码结构先验并消除歧义，我们引入了一种具身关节代码本，该代码本嵌入了每个关节的功能角色和运动学属性。我们的模型学习通过连续时间流匹配目标从 3D 点云生成这些轨迹。我们通过在大规模异构数据集上进行预训练，并在模拟和真实世界的灵巧操作任务上进行微调，来验证我们的方法。我们的方法始终优于所有基线方法，表明其具有卓越的样本效率和有效的跨机体技能迁移能力。这种以结构为中心的表示为扩展高自由度异构机械臂的策略提供了一条新途径。

Authors: Xiaohan Lei, Min Wang, Bohong Weng, Wengang Zhou, Houqiang Li

ArthroCut: Autonomous Policy Learning for Robotic Bone Resection in Knee Arthroplasty

简要说明：ArthroCut是一个用于机器人膝关节置换的自主策略学习框架，它利用微调的Qwen--VL主干和多模态数据，在台式骨切除实验中实现了高成功率。

TLDR: ArthroCut is an autonomous policy learning framework for robotic knee arthroplasty that leverages a fine-tuned Qwen--VL backbone and multimodal data to achieve high success rates in bench-top bone resection experiments.

Abstract: Despite rapid commercialization of surgical robots, their autonomy and real-time decision-making remain limited in practice. To address this gap, we propose ArthroCut, an autonomous policy learning framework that upgrades knee arthroplasty robots from assistive execution to context-aware action generation. ArthroCut fine-tunes a Qwen--VL backbone on a self-built, time-synchronized multimodal dataset from 21 complete cases (23,205 RGB--D pairs), integrating preoperative CT/MR, intraoperative NDI tracking of bones and end effector, RGB--D surgical video, robot state, and textual intent. The method operates on two complementary token families -- Preoperative Imaging Tokens (PIT) to encode patient-specific anatomy and planned resection planes, and Time-Aligned Surgical Tokens (TAST) to fuse real-time visual, geometric, and kinematic evidence -- and emits an interpretable action grammar under grammar/safety-constrained decoding. In bench-top experiments on a knee prosthesis across seven trials, ArthroCut achieves an average success rate of 86% over the six standard resections, significantly outperforming strong baselines trained under the same protocol. Ablations show that TAST is the principal driver of reliability while PIT provides essential anatomical grounding, and their combination yields the most stable multi-plane execution. These results indicate that aligning preoperative geometry with time-aligned intraoperative perception and translating that alignment into tokenized, constrained actions is an effective path toward robust, interpretable autonomy in orthopedic robotic surgery.

摘要（中文）：尽管手术机器人商业化进展迅速，但其实际自主性和实时决策能力仍然有限。为了弥补这一差距，我们提出了 ArthroCut，一个自主策略学习框架，旨在将膝关节置换机器人从辅助执行升级到上下文感知的动作生成。ArthroCut 在自建的时间同步多模态数据集上对 Qwen--VL 主干模型进行微调，该数据集来自 21 个完整病例（23,205 个 RGB--D 对），集成了术前 CT/MR、术中 NDI 跟踪的骨骼和末端执行器、RGB--D 手术视频、机器人状态和文本意图。该方法基于两个互补的标记系列运作——用于编码患者特定解剖结构和计划切除平面的术前影像标记（PIT），以及用于融合实时视觉、几何和运动学证据的时间对齐手术标记（TAST）——并在语法/安全约束解码下，输出一个可解释的动作语法。在七次试验中对膝关节假体进行基准测试实验，ArthroCut 在六个标准切除面上实现了 86% 的平均成功率，显著优于在相同协议下训练的强基线模型。消融实验表明，TAST 是可靠性的主要驱动因素，而 PIT 提供了必要的解剖学基础，两者的结合产生了最稳定的多平面执行。这些结果表明，将术前几何与时间对齐的术中感知对齐，并将该对齐转换为标记化的、受约束的动作，是在骨科机器人手术中实现稳健、可解释自主性的有效途径。

Authors: Xu Lu, Yiling Zhang, Wenquan Cheng, Longfei Ma, Fang Chen, Hongen Liao

RVN-Bench: A Benchmark for Reactive Visual Navigation

简要说明：RVN-Bench是一个新的碰撞感知室内视觉导航基准，使用Habitat 2.0和HM3D场景构建，旨在解决现有基准在拥挤室内环境中安全机器人导航方面的局限性。

TLDR: RVN-Bench is introduced as a new collision-aware benchmark for indoor visual navigation using Habitat 2.0 and HM3D scenes, addressing the limitations of existing benchmarks for safe robot navigation in cluttered indoor environments.

Abstract: Safe visual navigation is critical for indoor mobile robots operating in cluttered environments. Existing benchmarks, however, often neglect collisions or are designed for outdoor scenarios, making them unsuitable for indoor visual navigation. To address this limitation, we introduce the reactive visual navigation benchmark (RVN-Bench), a collision-aware benchmark for indoor mobile robots. In RVN-Bench, an agent must reach sequential goal positions in previously unseen environments using only visual observations and no prior map, while avoiding collisions. Built on the Habitat 2.0 simulator and leveraging high-fidelity HM3D scenes, RVN-Bench provides large-scale, diverse indoor environments, defines a collision-aware navigation task and evaluation metrics, and offers tools for standardized training and benchmarking. RVN-Bench supports both online and offline learning by offering an environment for online reinforcement learning, a trajectory image dataset generator, and tools for producing negative trajectory image datasets that capture collision events. Experiments show that policies trained on RVN-Bench generalize effectively to unseen environments, demonstrating its value as a standardized benchmark for safe and robust visual navigation. Code and additional materials are available at: https://rvn-bench.github.io/.

摘要（中文）：安全视觉导航对于在杂乱环境中运行的室内移动机器人至关重要。然而，现有的基准测试通常忽略碰撞或者专为室外场景设计，使其不适用于室内视觉导航。为了解决这一局限性，我们推出了反应式视觉导航基准测试（RVN-Bench），这是一个针对室内移动机器人的碰撞感知基准测试。在RVN-Bench中，智能体必须仅使用视觉观察，在没有先验地图的情况下，到达之前未见环境中的连续目标位置，同时避免碰撞。RVN-Bench建立在Habitat 2.0模拟器之上，并利用了高保真HM3D场景，提供了大规模、多样化的室内环境，定义了碰撞感知导航任务和评估指标，并为标准化训练和基准测试提供了工具。RVN-Bench通过提供在线强化学习环境、轨迹图像数据集生成器以及用于生成捕捉碰撞事件的负轨迹图像数据集的工具，支持在线和离线学习。实验表明，在RVN-Bench上训练的策略能够有效地泛化到未见环境，证明了其作为安全可靠的视觉导航标准化基准测试的价值。代码和其他材料可在https://rvn-bench.github.io/获取。

Authors: Jaewon Lee, Jaeseok Heo, Gunmin Lee, Howoong Jun, Jeongwoo Oh, Songhwai Oh

Lightweight Visual Reasoning for Socially-Aware Robots

简要说明：本文介绍了一种轻量级的语言到视觉反馈模块，旨在提升VLMs在机器人任务中的性能，结果显示在导航、场景描述和人类意图识别方面取得了性能提升，且参数开销很小。

TLDR: This paper introduces a lightweight language-to-vision feedback module to improve VLMs for robotics tasks, showing performance gains with minimal parameter overhead in navigation, scene description, and human-intention recognition.

Abstract: Robots operating in shared human environments must not only navigate, interact, and detect their surroundings, they must also interpret and respond to dynamic, and often unpredictable, human behaviours. Although recent advances have shown promise in enhancing robotic perception and instruction-following using Vision-Language Models (VLMs), they remain limited in addressing the complexities of multimodal human-robot interactions (HRI). Motivated by this challenge, we introduce a lightweight language-to-vision feedback module that closes the loop between an LLM and the vision encoder in VLMs. The module projects image-token hidden states through a gated Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) back into the encoder input, prompting a second pass that reinterprets the scene under text context. We evaluate this approach on three robotics-centred tasks: navigation in a simulated environment (Habitat), sequential scene description (Mementos-Robotics), and human-intention recognition (our HRI dataset). Results show that our method improves Qwen 2.5 (7B) by $3.3\%$ (less distance), $+0.057$ description score, and $+2.93\%$ accuracy, with less than $3\%$ extra parameters; Gemma 3 (4B) and LLaVA OV 1.5 (4B) show mixed navigation results but gains $+0.111,+0.055$ and $+10.81\%,+4.79\%$ on the latter two tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/alessioGalatolo/VLM-Reasoning-for-Robotics

摘要（中文）：在共享的人类环境中运行的机器人不仅必须导航、交互和探测周围环境，还必须解释和响应动态且常常不可预测的人类行为。尽管最近的进展表明，使用视觉-语言模型（VLMs）增强机器人感知和指令跟随具有前景，但在解决多模态人机交互（HRI）的复杂性方面仍然存在局限性。为了应对这一挑战，我们引入了一种轻量级的语言-视觉反馈模块，该模块可以闭合VLMs中LLM和视觉编码器之间的环路。该模块通过门控多层感知机（MLP）将图像token的隐藏状态投影回编码器的输入，从而提示进行第二次传递，以便在文本上下文中重新解释场景。我们在三个以机器人为中心的任务上评估了这种方法：模拟环境中的导航（Habitat）、连续场景描述（Mementos-Robotics）以及人类意图识别（我们的人机交互数据集）。结果表明，我们的方法在额外参数少于$3\%$的情况下，使Qwen 2.5（7B）的性能提高了$3.3\%$（缩短了距离）、$+0.057$的描述分数和$+2.93\%$的准确率；Gemma 3（4B）和LLaVA OV 1.5（4B）显示出混合的导航结果，但在后两个任务上分别获得了$+0.111,+0.055$和$+10.81\%,+4.79\%$的提升。代码可在https://github.com/alessioGalatolo/VLM-Reasoning-for-Robotics 获取。

Authors: Alessio Galatolo, Ronald Cumbal, Alexandros Rouchitsas, Katie Winkle, Didem Gürdür Broo, Ginevra Castellano

DISC: Dense Integrated Semantic Context for Large-Scale Open-Set Semantic Mapping

简要说明：该论文介绍了DISC，一种用于开放集语义地图构建的新型单次、距离加权提取机制，它利用视觉Transformer的中间层进行高效准确的特征提取，并在语义精度和查询检索方面优于最先进的方法。

TLDR: The paper introduces DISC, a novel single-pass, distance-weighted extraction mechanism for open-set semantic mapping, leveraging vision transformer intermediate layers for efficient and accurate feature extraction, outperforming state-of-the-art methods in semantic accuracy and query retrieval.

Abstract: Open-set semantic mapping enables language-driven robotic perception, but current instance-centric approaches are bottlenecked by context-depriving and computationally expensive crop-based feature extraction. To overcome this fundamental limitation, we introduce DISC (Dense Integrated Semantic Context), featuring a novel single-pass, distance-weighted extraction mechanism. By deriving high-fidelity CLIP embeddings directly from the vision transformer's intermediate layers, our approach eliminates the latency and domain-shift artifacts of traditional image cropping, yielding pure, mask-aligned semantic representations. To fully leverage these features in large-scale continuous mapping, DISC is built upon a fully GPU-accelerated architecture that replaces periodic offline processing with precise, on-the-fly voxel-level instance refinement. We evaluate our approach on standard benchmarks (Replica, ScanNet) and a newly generated large-scale-mapping dataset based on Habitat-Matterport 3D (HM3DSEM) to assess scalability across complex scenes in multi-story buildings. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that DISC significantly surpasses current state-of-the-art zero-shot methods in both semantic accuracy and query retrieval, providing a robust, real-time capable framework for robotic deployment. The full source code, data generation and evaluation pipelines will be made available at https://github.com/DFKI-NI/DISC.

摘要（中文）：开放集语义地图构建能够实现语言驱动的机器人感知，但目前以实例为中心的方法受到缺乏上下文信息和计算成本高的基于裁剪的特征提取的限制。为了克服这一根本性限制，我们提出了DISC (密集集成语义上下文)， 具有一种新型单次、距离加权的提取机制。通过直接从视觉转换器的中间层推导出高保真CLIP嵌入，我们的方法消除了传统图像裁剪的延迟和域偏移伪影，从而产生纯粹的、与掩码对齐的语义表示。为了在大型连续地图构建中充分利用这些特征，DISC建立在一个完全GPU加速的架构之上，该架构用精确的、即时体素级实例细化取代了周期性的离线处理。我们在标准基准测试(Replica, ScanNet)和一个基于Habitat-Matterport 3D (HM3DSEM) 新生成的大规模地图构建数据集上评估了我们的方法，以评估其在多层建筑复杂场景中的可扩展性。广泛的评估表明，DISC在语义准确性和查询检索方面均显著超越了当前的最新零样本方法，为机器人部署提供了一个稳健的、具有实时能力的框架。完整的源代码、数据生成和评估流程将在https://github.com/DFKI-NI/DISC上提供。

Authors: Felix Igelbrink, Lennart Niecksch, Martin Atzmueller, Joachim Hertzberg

SkillVLA: Tackling Combinatorial Diversity in Dual-Arm Manipulation via Skill Reuse

简要说明：该论文介绍了SkillVLA，一种新的视觉-语言-动作框架，旨在实现双臂操作中的技能复用，通过显式支持已学习的单臂技能的重新组合，解决了组合多样性的挑战。

TLDR: The paper introduces SkillVLA, a novel vision-language-action framework designed to enable skill reuse in dual-arm manipulation, addressing the challenge of combinatorial diversity by explicitly supporting the recombination of learned single-arm skills.

Abstract: Recent progress in vision-language-action (VLA) models has demonstrated strong potential for dual-arm manipulation, enabling complex behaviors and generalization to unseen environments. However, mainstream bimanual VLA formulations largely overlook the critical challenge of combinatorial diversity. Different pairings of single-arm behaviors can induce qualitatively distinct task behaviors, yet existing models do not explicitly account for this structure. We argue that effective bimanual VLAs should support skill reuse - the ability to recombine previously learned single-arm skills across novel left-right pairings - thereby avoiding the need to separately learn every possible combination. Current VLA designs entangle skills across arms, preventing such recomposition and limiting scalability. To address this limitation, we propose SkillVLA, a framework explicitly designed to enable skill reuse in dual-arm manipulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SkillVLA substantially improves skill composition, increasing overall success rate from 0% to 51%, and achieves strong performance on cooperative and long-horizon tasks.

摘要（中文）：视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型近期的进展展现出在双臂操作方面的巨大潜力，能够实现复杂的行为并泛化到未见环境中。然而，主流的双手动VLA结构在很大程度上忽略了组合多样性的关键挑战。单臂行为的不同配对可以产生性质上截然不同的任务行为，但现有模型并未明确考虑这种结构。我们认为，有效的双手动VLA应当支持技能复用——即能够在新的左右配对中重组先前学习的单臂技能——从而避免分别学习每种可能组合的需求。当前VLA设计将技能与手臂纠缠在一起，阻碍了这种重组并限制了可扩展性。为了解决这一局限性，我们提出了SkillVLA，一个专门为在双臂操作中实现技能复用而设计的框架。大量实验表明，SkillVLA显著提升了技能组合能力，将整体成功率从0%提高到51%，并在协作和长时程任务上取得了优异的性能。

Authors: Xuanran Zhai, Zekai Huang, Longyan Wu, Qianyou Zhao, Qiaojun Yu, Jieji Ren, Ce Hao, Harold Soh

Learning Surgical Robotic Manipulation with 3D Spatial Priors

简要说明：该论文介绍了Spatial Surgical Transformer (SST)，一种端到端的视觉运动策略，利用内窥镜图像中的3D空间线索进行手术机器人操作。他们还引入了一个大型数据集Surgical3D，并在真实机器人手术任务上展示了最先进的性能。

TLDR: The paper introduces Spatial Surgical Transformer (SST), an end-to-end visuomotor policy that leverages 3D spatial cues from endoscopic images for surgical robotic manipulation. They also introduce a large-scale dataset Surgical3D, and demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on real-robot surgical tasks.

Abstract: Achieving 3D spatial awareness is crucial for surgical robotic manipulation, where precise and delicate operations are required. Existing methods either explicitly reconstruct the surgical scene prior to manipulation, or enhance multi-view features by adding wrist-mounted cameras to supplement the default stereo endoscopes. However, both paradigms suffer from notable limitations: the former easily leads to error accumulation and prevents end-to-end optimization due to its multi-stage nature, while the latter is rarely adopted in clinical practice since wrist-mounted cameras can interfere with the motion of surgical robot arms. In this work, we introduce the Spatial Surgical Transformer (SST), an end-to-end visuomotor policy that empowers surgical robots with 3D spatial awareness by directly exploring 3D spatial cues embedded in endoscopic images. First, we build Surgical3D, a large-scale photorealistic dataset containing 30K stereo endoscopic image pairs with accurate 3D geometry, addressing the scarcity of 3D data in surgical scenes. Based on Surgical3D, we finetune a powerful geometric transformer to extract robust 3D latent representations from stereo endoscopes images. These representations are then seamlessly aligned with the robot's action space via a lightweight multi-level spatial feature connector (MSFC), all within an endoscope-centric coordinate frame. Extensive real-robot experiments demonstrate that SST achieves state-of-the-art performance and strong spatial generalization on complex surgical tasks such as knot tying and ex-vivo organ dissection, representing a significant step toward practical clinical deployment. The dataset and code will be released.

摘要（中文）：实现三维空间感知对于需要精准和精细操作的外科机器人操控至关重要。现有方法要么在操控前明确地重建手术场景，要么通过增加腕部摄像头来增强多视角特征，以补充默认的立体内窥镜。然而，这两种范式都存在显著局限性：前者由于其多阶段性质，容易导致误差累积并阻碍端到端优化；而后者在临床实践中很少采用，因为腕部摄像头可能会干扰外科机器人手臂的运动。本文中，我们介绍了空间外科Transformer (SST)，一种端到端视觉运动策略，它通过直接探索内窥镜图像中嵌入的三维空间线索，赋予外科机器人三维空间感知能力。首先，我们构建了Surgical3D，一个包含3万个带有精确三维几何信息的立体内窥镜图像对的大规模照片级真实感数据集，以解决手术场景中三维数据的稀缺问题。基于Surgical3D，我们微调了一个强大的几何Transformer，以从立体内窥镜图像中提取稳健的三维潜在表示。然后，通过一个轻量级的多层空间特征连接器(MSFC)，将这些表示无缝地与机器人的动作空间对齐，所有操作都在以内窥镜为中心的坐标系中进行。大量的真实机器人实验表明，SST在诸如打结和离体器官解剖等复杂的外科任务上实现了最先进的性能和强大的空间泛化能力，代表着向实际临床部署迈出的重要一步。数据集和代码将会发布。

Authors: Yu Sheng, Lidian Wang, Xiaomeng Chu, Jiajun Deng, Min Cheng, Yanyong Zhang, Bei Hua, Houqiang Li, Jianmin Ji

Cognition to Control - Multi-Agent Learning for Human-Humanoid Collaborative Transport

简要说明：该论文介绍了一种用于人机协作的认知到控制（C2C）框架，该框架采用分层方法，包括一个基于视觉语言模型的 grounding 层、一个深思熟虑的 MARL 层和一个全身控制层，并在协作操作任务中表现出更高的性能。

TLDR: The paper introduces a cognition-to-control (C2C) framework for human-robot collaboration, using a hierarchical approach with a VLM-based grounding layer, a deliberative MARL layer, and a whole-body control layer, demonstrating improved performance in collaborative manipulation tasks.

Abstract: Effective human-robot collaboration (HRC) requires translating high-level intent into contact-stable whole-body motion while continuously adapting to a human partner. Many vision-language-action (VLA) systems learn end-to-end mappings from observations and instructions to actions, but they often emphasize reactive (System 1-like) behavior and leave under-specified how sustained System 2-style deliberation can be integrated with reliable, low-latency continuous control. This gap is acute in multi-agent HRC, where long-horizon coordination decisions and physical execution must co-evolve under contact, feasibility, and safety constraints. We address this limitation with cognition-to-control (C2C), a three-layer hierarchy that makes the deliberation-to-control pathway explicit: (i) a VLM-based grounding layer that maintains persistent scene referents and infers embodiment-aware affordances/constraints; (ii) a deliberative skill/coordination layer-the System 2 core-that optimizes long-horizon skill choices and sequences under human-robot coupling via decentralized MARL cast as a Markov potential game with a shared potential encoding task progress; and (iii) a whole-body control layer that executes the selected skills at high frequency while enforcing kinematic/dynamic feasibility and contact stability. The deliberative layer is realized as a residual policy relative to a nominal controller, internalizing partner dynamics without explicit role assignment. Experiments on collaborative manipulation tasks show higher success and robustness than single-agent and end-to-end baselines, with stable coordination and emergent leader-follower behaviors.

摘要（中文）：有效的人机协作（HRC）需要将高层意图转化为具有接触稳定性的全身运动，同时持续适应人类伙伴。许多视觉-语言-动作（VLA）系统学习从观测和指令到行动的端到端映射，但它们通常强调反应性（类似系统1）行为，并且对如何将持续的系统2式深思熟虑与可靠、低延迟的连续控制相结合考虑不足。这种差距在多智能体HRC中尤为明显，在其中，长期协调决策和物理执行必须在接触、可行性和安全约束下共同演进。我们通过认知到控制（C2C）的方法来解决这一局限性，C2C是一种三层架构，明确了从深思熟虑到控制的路径：（i）一个基于VLM的 grounding 层，维护持久的场景参照物，并推断出具有具身意识的 affordance/约束；（ii）一个深思熟虑的技能/协调层——系统2的核心——它通过去中心化MARL优化长期技能选择和序列，该MARL被视为具有共享势能编码任务进程的马尔科夫势博弈；以及（iii）一个全身控制层，以高频率执行选定的技能，同时强制执行运动学/动力学可行性和接触稳定性。深思熟虑层实现为一个相对于标称控制器的残差策略，在没有明确角色分配的情况下内在化伙伴动态。在协作操纵任务上的实验表明，与单智能体和端到端基线相比，具有更高的成功率和鲁棒性，以及稳定的协调和涌现的领导者-追随者行为。

Authors: Hao Zhang, Ding Zhao, H. Eric Tseng

Interaction-Aware Whole-Body Control for Compliant Object Transport

简要说明：本文提出了一种面向交互的全身控制（IO-WBC）方法，用于在非结构化环境中进行协作对象运输，结合轨迹优化和强化学习，以实现重载交互下的鲁棒性和顺应性行为。

TLDR: This paper presents an interaction-oriented whole-body control (IO-WBC) method for cooperative object transport in unstructured environments, combining trajectory optimization and reinforcement learning for robust and compliant behavior under heavy load interactions.

Abstract: Cooperative object transport in unstructured environments remains challenging for assistive humanoids because strong, time-varying interaction forces can make tracking-centric whole-body control unreliable, especially in close-contact support tasks. This paper proposes a bio-inspired, interaction-oriented whole-body control (IO-WBC) that functions as an artificial cerebellum - an adaptive motor agent that translates upstream (skill-level) commands into stable, physically consistent whole-body behavior under contact. This work structurally separates upper-body interaction execution from lower-body support control, enabling the robot to maintain balance while shaping force exchange in a tightly coupled robot-object system. A trajectory-optimized reference generator (RG) provides a kinematic prior, while a reinforcement learning (RL) policy governs body responses under heavy-load interactions and disturbances. The policy is trained in simulation with randomized payload mass/inertia and external perturbations, and deployed via asymmetric teacher-student distillation so that the student relies only on proprioceptive histories at runtime. Extensive experiments demonstrate that IO-WBC maintains stable whole-body behavior and physical interaction even when precise velocity tracking becomes infeasible, enabling compliant object transport across a wide range of scenarios.

摘要（中文）：在非结构化环境中进行协作式物体搬运对于辅助人形机器人来说仍然具有挑战性，因为强烈的、随时间变化的交互力会使以跟踪为中心的全身控制变得不可靠，尤其是在近距离接触支持任务中。本文提出了一种受生物启发的、面向交互的全身控制（IO-WBC），它作为一个人工小脑发挥作用——一个自适应运动智能体，将上游（技能级别）指令转化为在接触下稳定的、物理一致的全身行为。这项工作在结构上将上半身的交互执行与下半身的支持控制分离，使机器人能够在紧密耦合的机器人-物体系统中保持平衡，同时塑造力的交换。一个轨迹优化的参考生成器（RG）提供了一个运动学先验，而一个强化学习（RL）策略控制身体在高负载交互和扰动下的响应。该策略在模拟环境中进行训练，负载的质量/惯性和外部扰动是随机化的，并通过非对称教师-学生蒸馏进行部署，以便学生在运行时仅依赖于本体感觉历史。大量实验表明，即使精确的速度跟踪变得不可行，IO-WBC 也能保持稳定的全身行为和物理交互，从而能够在各种场景中实现顺从的物体搬运。

Authors: Hao Zhang, Yves Tseng, Ding Zhao, H. Eric Tseng

RAGNav: A Retrieval-Augmented Topological Reasoning Framework for Multi-Goal Visual-Language Navigation

简要说明：本文介绍了一种名为RAGNav的检索增强框架，该框架使用双基内存系统（拓扑地图和语义森林）来改进多目标视觉语言导航，并实现了SOTA结果。

TLDR: The paper introduces RAGNav, a retrieval-augmented framework using a dual-basis memory system (topological map and semantic forest) for improved multi-goal Vision-Language Navigation, achieving SOTA results.

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) is evolving from single-point pathfinding toward the more challenging Multi-Goal VLN. This task requires agents to accurately identify multiple entities while collaboratively reasoning over their spatial-physical constraints and sequential execution order. However, generic Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) paradigms often suffer from spatial hallucinations and planning drift when handling multi-object associations due to the lack of explicit spatial modeling.To address these challenges, we propose RAGNav, a framework that bridges the gap between semantic reasoning and physical structure. The core of RAGNav is a Dual-Basis Memory system, which integrates a low-level topological map for maintaining physical connectivity with a high-level semantic forest for hierarchical environment abstraction. Building on this representation, the framework introduces an anchor-guided conditional retrieval and a topological neighbor score propagation mechanism. This approach facilitates the rapid screening of candidate targets and the elimination of semantic noise, while performing semantic calibration by leveraging the physical associations inherent in the topological neighborhood.This mechanism significantly enhances the capability of inter-target reachability reasoning and the efficiency of sequential planning. Experimental results demonstrate that RAGNav achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance in complex multi-goal navigation tasks.

摘要（中文）：视觉-语言导航(VLN)正从单点寻径发展到更具挑战性的多目标VLN。该任务要求智能体准确识别多个实体，同时协同推理它们的空间-物理约束和顺序执行顺序。然而，通用的检索增强生成(RAG)范式在处理多对象关联时，由于缺乏显式的空间建模，经常遭受空间幻觉和规划漂移。为了应对这些挑战，我们提出了RAGNav，一个弥合语义推理和物理结构之间差距的框架。RAGNav的核心是一个双基记忆系统，它集成了用于维护物理连通性的底层拓扑地图和用于分层环境抽象的高层语义森林。基于这种表示，该框架引入了一种锚点引导的条件检索和一种拓扑邻居分数传播机制。该方法有助于快速筛选候选目标并消除语义噪声，同时通过利用拓扑邻域中固有的物理关联执行语义校准。这种机制显著增强了目标间可达性推理的能力和顺序规划的效率。实验结果表明，RAGNav在复杂的多目标导航任务中实现了最先进(SOTA)的性能。

Authors: Ling Luo, Qiangian Bai

HALyPO: Heterogeneous-Agent Lyapunov Policy Optimization for Human-Robot Collaboration

简要说明：该论文介绍了HALyPO，一种基于李雅普诺夫的策略优化方法，用于稳定人机协作中的多智能体强化学习，解决了异构智能体之间的理性差距。

TLDR: The paper introduces HALyPO, a Lyapunov-based policy optimization method for stable multi-agent reinforcement learning in human-robot collaboration, addressing the rationality gap between heterogeneous agents.

Abstract: To improve generalization and resilience in human-robot collaboration (HRC), robots must handle the combinatorial diversity of human behaviors and contexts, motivating multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). However, inherent heterogeneity between robots and humans creates a rationality gap (RG) in the learning process-a variational mismatch between decentralized best-response dynamics and centralized cooperative ascent. The resulting learning problem is a general-sum differentiable game, so independent policy-gradient updates can oscillate or diverge without added structure. We propose heterogeneous-agent Lyapunov policy optimization (HALyPO), which establishes formal stability directly in the policy-parameter space by enforcing a per-step Lyapunov decrease condition on a parameter-space disagreement metric. Unlike Lyapunov-based safe RL, which targets state/trajectory constraints in constrained Markov decision processes, HALyPO uses Lyapunov certification to stabilize decentralized policy learning. HALyPO rectifies decentralized gradients via optimal quadratic projections, ensuring monotonic contraction of RG and enabling effective exploration of open-ended interaction spaces. Extensive simulations and real-world humanoid-robot experiments show that this certified stability improves generalization and robustness in collaborative corner cases.

摘要（中文）：为了提高人机协作 (HRC) 的泛化性和鲁棒性，机器人必须能够处理人类行为和环境的组合多样性，这推动了多智能体强化学习 (MARL) 的发展。然而，机器人和人类之间固有的异质性在学习过程中产生了一个理性差距 (RG)——去中心化最佳响应动态与中心化协作提升之间的变分不匹配。由此产生的学习问题是一个一般和可微游戏，因此，如果没有额外的结构，独立的策略梯度更新可能会振荡或发散。我们提出了异质智能体Lyapunov策略优化 (HALyPO)，它通过在参数空间的差异度量上强制执行每一步的Lyapunov递减条件，直接在策略参数空间中建立形式稳定性。与基于Lyapunov的安全强化学习（针对约束马尔可夫决策过程中的状态/轨迹约束）不同，HALyPO使用Lyapunov认证来稳定去中心化的策略学习。HALyPO通过最优二次投影来修正去中心化的梯度，确保 RG 的单调收缩，并能够有效地探索开放式的交互空间。大量的仿真和真实的人形机器人实验表明，这种经过认证的稳定性提高了协作边缘情况下的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Authors: Hao Zhang, Yaru Niu, Yikai Wang, Ding Zhao, H. Eric Tseng

X-Loco: Towards Generalist Humanoid Locomotion Control via Synergetic Policy Distillation

简要说明：该论文介绍了 X-Loco，一个策略蒸馏框架，用于训练基于视觉的通用人形机器人运动策略，通过从专家策略进行协同策略蒸馏，结合了直立行走、全身协调和跌倒恢复。

TLDR: The paper introduces X-Loco, a policy distillation framework for training a vision-based generalist humanoid locomotion policy that combines upright locomotion, whole-body coordination, and fall recovery via synergetic policy distillation from specialist policies.

Abstract: While recent advances have demonstrated strong performance in individual humanoid skills such as upright locomotion, fall recovery and whole-body coordination, learning a single policy that masters all these skills remains challenging due to the diverse dynamics and conflicting control objectives involved. To address this, we introduce X-Loco, a framework for training a vision-based generalist humanoid locomotion policy. X-Loco trains multiple oracle specialist policies and adopts a synergetic policy distillation with a case-adaptive specialist selection mechanism, which dynamically leverages multiple specialist policies to guide a vision-based student policy. This design enables the student to acquire a broad spectrum of locomotion skills, ranging from fall recovery to terrain traversal and whole-body coordination skills. To the best of our knowledge, X-Loco is the first framework to demonstrate vision-based humanoid locomotion that jointly integrates upright locomotion, whole-body coordination and fall recovery, while operating solely under velocity commands without relying on reference motions. Experimental results show that X-Loco achieves superior performance, demonstrated by tasks such as fall recovery and terrain traversal. Ablation studies further highlight that our framework effectively leverages specialist expertise and enhances learning efficiency.

摘要（中文）：虽然最近的进展已经证明了在个体人形机器人技能（如直立行走、跌倒恢复和全身协调）方面的强大性能，但由于涉及的各式各样的动力学特性和相互冲突的控制目标，学习一个掌握所有这些技能的单一策略仍然具有挑战性。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了 X-Loco，一个用于训练基于视觉的通用人形机器人运动策略的框架。X-Loco 训练多个预言机专家策略，并采用协同策略蒸馏和案例自适应专家选择机制，动态地利用多个专家策略来指导一个基于视觉的学生策略。这种设计使学生能够获得广泛的运动技能，从跌倒恢复到地形穿越和全身协调技能。据我们所知，X-Loco 是第一个展示基于视觉的人形机器人运动的框架，它联合集成了直立行走、全身协调和跌倒恢复，同时仅在速度指令下运行，而不依赖于参考运动。实验结果表明，X-Loco 实现了卓越的性能，这体现在诸如跌倒恢复和地形穿越等任务中。消融研究进一步强调了我们的框架有效地利用了专家知识，并提高了学习效率。

Authors: Dewei Wang, Xinmiao Wang, Chenyun Zhang, Jiyuan Shi, Yingnan Zhao, Chenjia Bai, Xuelong Li

Large-Language-Model-Guided State Estimation for Partially Observable Task and Motion Planning

简要说明：该论文提出了CoCo-TAMP框架，该框架利用LLM引导的常识知识来改进机器人部分可观测环境中的状态估计和规划，并在模拟和真实世界实验中展示了显著的时间缩减。

TLDR: This paper introduces CoCo-TAMP, a framework that uses LLM-guided common-sense knowledge to improve state estimation and planning for robots in partially observable environments, demonstrating significant time reductions in simulation and real-world experiments.

Abstract: Robot planning in partially observable environments, where not all objects are known or visible, is a challenging problem, as it requires reasoning under uncertainty through partially observable Markov decision processes. During the execution of a computed plan, a robot may unexpectedly observe task-irrelevant objects, which are typically ignored by naive planners. In this work, we propose incorporating two types of common-sense knowledge: (1) certain objects are more likely to be found in specific locations; and (2) similar objects are likely to be co-located, while dissimilar objects are less likely to be found together. Manually engineering such knowledge is complex, so we explore leveraging the powerful common-sense reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). Our planning and execution framework, CoCo-TAMP, introduces a hierarchical state estimation that uses LLM-guided information to shape the belief over task-relevant objects, enabling efficient solutions to long-horizon task and motion planning problems. In experiments, CoCo-TAMP achieves an average reduction of 62.7 in planning and execution time in simulation, and 72.6 in real-world demonstrations, compared to a baseline that does not incorporate either type of common-sense knowledge.

摘要（中文）：在部分可观测环境中进行机器人规划是一个具有挑战性的问题，在这种环境中，并非所有物体都是已知或可见的，因此需要通过部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程进行不确定性推理。在已计算规划的执行过程中，机器人可能会意外观察到与任务无关的物体，这些物体通常会被简单的规划器忽略。在这项工作中，我们提出整合两种类型的常识知识：（1）某些物体更可能在特定位置被发现；（2）相似的物体更可能共存于同一地点，而不同的物体不太可能在一起发现。手动设计此类知识是复杂的，因此我们探索利用大型语言模型（LLM）强大的常识推理能力。我们的规划和执行框架CoCo-TAMP引入了一种分层状态估计，该估计使用LLM引导的信息来塑造对任务相关物体的置信度，从而能够有效解决长时程任务与运动规划问题。在实验中，与不包含任何一种常识知识的基线相比，CoCo-TAMP在模拟中平均减少了62.7%的规划和执行时间，在真实世界演示中平均减少了72.6%。

MistyPilot: An Agentic Fast-Slow Thinking LLM Framework for Misty Social Robots

简要说明：本文介绍了一种名为 MistyPilot 的 LLM 驱动社交机器人框架，该框架集成了快慢思考模式，旨在解决基于用户指令的工具选择、编排和执行方面的挑战。该框架包含一个物理交互代理 (PIA) 和一个社交智能代理 (SIA)，并在自定义基准数据集上进行了评估。

TLDR: The paper introduces MistyPilot, an LLM-driven framework for social robots that integrates fast-slow thinking and addresses challenges in tool selection, orchestration, and execution based on user instructions. The framework includes a physically interactive agent (PIA) and a socially intelligent agent (SIA), evaluated on custom benchmark datasets.

Abstract: With the availability of open APIs in social robots, it has become easier to customize general-purpose tools to meet users' needs. However, interpreting high-level user instructions, selecting and configuring appropriate tools, and executing them reliably remain challenging for users without programming experience. To address these challenges, we introduce MistyPilot, an agentic LLM-driven framework for autonomous tool selection, orchestration, and parameter configuration. MistyPilot comprises two core components: a Physically Interactive Agent (PIA) and a Socially Intelligent Agent (SIA). The PIA enables robust sensor-triggered and tool-driven task execution, while the SIA generates socially intelligent and emotionally aligned dialogue. MistyPilot further integrates a fast-slow thinking paradigm to capture user preferences, reduce latency, and improve task efficiency. To comprehensively evaluate MistyPilot, we contribute five benchmark datasets. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework in routing correctness, task completeness, fast-slow thinking retrieval efficiency, tool scalability,and emotion alignment. All code, datasets, and experimental videos will be made publicly available on the project webpage.

摘要（中文）：随着社交机器人中开放 API 的普及，定制通用工具以满足用户需求变得更加容易。然而，对于缺乏编程经验的用户来说，解释高级用户指令、选择和配置合适的工具以及可靠地执行它们仍然具有挑战性。为了应对这些挑战，我们引入了 MistyPilot，一个基于代理的、由 LLM 驱动的框架，用于自主工具选择、编排和参数配置。MistyPilot 由两个核心组件组成：物理交互代理 (PIA) 和社交智能代理 (SIA)。PIA 支持稳健的传感器触发和工具驱动的任务执行，而 SIA 则生成具有社交智能和情感对齐的对话。MistyPilot 进一步集成了一种快-慢思考范式，以捕捉用户偏好、减少延迟并提高任务效率。为了全面评估 MistyPilot，我们贡献了五个基准数据集。广泛的实验证明了我们的框架在路由正确性、任务完整性、快-慢思考检索效率、工具可扩展性以及情感对齐方面的有效性。所有代码、数据集和实验视频将在项目网页上公开提供。

Authors: Xiao Wang, Lu Dong, Jingchen Sun, Ifeoma Nwogu, Srirangaraj Setlur, Venu Govindaraju

MEM: Multi-Scale Embodied Memory for Vision Language Action Models

简要说明：该论文介绍了多尺度具身记忆（MEM），一种新颖的机器人策略记忆架构，它结合了基于视频的短时记忆和基于文本的长时记忆，从而实现长时任务。

TLDR: The paper introduces Multi-Scale Embodied Memory (MEM), a novel memory architecture for robotic policies that combines video-based short-horizon memory and text-based long-horizon memory to enable long-horizon tasks.

Abstract: Conventionally, memory in end-to-end robotic learning involves inputting a sequence of past observations into the learned policy. However, in complex multi-stage real-world tasks, the robot's memory must represent past events at multiple levels of granularity: from long-term memory that captures abstracted semantic concepts (e.g., a robot cooking dinner should remember which stages of the recipe are already done) to short-term memory that captures recent events and compensates for occlusions (e.g., a robot remembering the object it wants to pick up once its arm occludes it). In this work, our main insight is that an effective memory architecture for long-horizon robotic control should combine multiple modalities to capture these different levels of abstraction. We introduce Multi-Scale Embodied Memory (MEM), an approach for mixed-modal long-horizon memory in robot policies. MEM combines video-based short-horizon memory, compressed via a video encoder, with text-based long-horizon memory. Together, they enable robot policies to perform tasks that span up to fifteen minutes, like cleaning up a kitchen, or preparing a grilled cheese sandwich. Additionally, we find that memory enables MEM policies to intelligently adapt manipulation strategies in-context.

摘要（中文）：传统上，端到端机器人学习中的记忆涉及将过去的观测序列输入到学习到的策略中。然而，在复杂的多阶段真实世界任务中，机器人的记忆必须以多个粒度级别表示过去的事件：从捕获抽象语义概念的长期记忆（例如，烹饪晚餐的机器人应该记住菜谱的哪些阶段已经完成）到捕获最近事件并补偿遮挡的短期记忆（例如，机器人记住它想要拾取的物体，即使它的手臂遮挡了它）。在这项工作中，我们的主要见解是，用于长时程机器人控制的有效记忆架构应结合多种模态来捕获这些不同层次的抽象。我们引入了多尺度具身记忆（MEM），这是一种用于机器人策略中混合模态长时程记忆的方法。 MEM结合了基于视频的短时程记忆（通过视频编码器压缩）和基于文本的长时程记忆。 它们共同使机器人策略能够执行长达十五分钟的任务，例如清理厨房或准备烤奶酪三明治。 此外，我们发现记忆使MEM策略能够在情境中智能地调整操作策略。

Authors: Marcel Torne, Karl Pertsch, Homer Walke, Kyle Vedder, Suraj Nair, Brian Ichter, Allen Z. Ren, Haohuan Wang, Jiaming Tang, Kyle Stachowicz, Karan Dhabalia, Michael Equi, Quan Vuong, Jost Tobias Springenberg, Sergey Levine, Chelsea Finn, Danny Driess

From Local Matches to Global Masks: Novel Instance Detection in Open-World Scenes

简要说明：该论文介绍了一种名为L2G-Det的新型实例检测框架，该框架通过局部patch匹配和SAM提示绕过显式对象提议，从而提高了在开放世界环境中的性能。

TLDR: L2G-Det is introduced, a novel instance detection framework bypassing explicit object proposals via local patch matching and SAM prompting for improved performance in open-world environments.

Abstract: Detecting and segmenting novel object instances in open-world environments is a fundamental problem in robotic perception. Given only a small set of template images, a robot must locate and segment a specific object instance in a cluttered, previously unseen scene. Existing proposal-based approaches are highly sensitive to proposal quality and often fail under occlusion and background clutter. We propose L2G-Det, a local-to-global instance detection framework that bypasses explicit object proposals by leveraging dense patch-level matching between templates and the query image. Locally matched patches generate candidate points, which are refined through a candidate selection module to suppress false positives. The filtered points are then used to prompt an augmented Segment Anything Model (SAM) with instance-specific object tokens, enabling reliable reconstruction of complete instance masks. Experiments demonstrate improved performance over proposal-based methods in challenging open-world settings.

摘要（中文）：在开放世界环境中检测和分割新颖物体实例是机器人感知中的一个基本问题。仅给定一小组模板图像，机器人必须在杂乱的、先前未见过的场景中定位并分割特定的物体实例。现有的基于候选区域的方法对候选区域质量高度敏感，并且在遮挡和背景杂乱的情况下经常失效。我们提出了L2G-Det，一个局部到全局的实例检测框架，它通过利用模板图像和查询图像之间的密集patch级匹配来绕过显式的物体候选区域。局部匹配的patch生成候选点，这些候选点通过候选点选择模块进行优化以抑制假阳性。然后，过滤后的点被用于提示一个增强的Segment Anything Model (SAM)，该模型具有实例特定的对象tokens，从而实现完整实例掩模的可靠重建。实验表明，在具有挑战性的开放世界环境中，我们的方法优于基于候选区域的方法。

Authors: Qifan Zhang, Sai Haneesh Allu, Jikai Wang, Yangxiao Lu, Yu Xiang

Phys4D: Fine-Grained Physics-Consistent 4D Modeling from Video Diffusion

简要说明：本文介绍了Phys4D，一个三阶段流程，旨在通过预训练、使用仿真数据进行监督微调和强化学习，来提高从视频扩散模型中学习的4D世界表征的物理一致性。 他们还引入了新的指标来评估4D一致性。

TLDR: The paper introduces Phys4D, a three-stage pipeline to improve the physical consistency of 4D world representations learned from video diffusion models using pretraining, supervised fine-tuning with simulation data, and reinforcement learning. They also introduce new metrics for evaluating 4D consistency.

Abstract: Recent video diffusion models have achieved impressive capabilities as large-scale generative world models. However, these models often struggle with fine-grained physical consistency, exhibiting physically implausible dynamics over time. In this work, we present \textbf{Phys4D}, a pipeline for learning physics-consistent 4D world representations from video diffusion models. Phys4D adopts \textbf{a three-stage training paradigm} that progressively lifts appearance-driven video diffusion models into physics-consistent 4D world representations. We first bootstrap robust geometry and motion representations through large-scale pseudo-supervised pretraining, establishing a foundation for 4D scene modeling. We then perform physics-grounded supervised fine-tuning using simulation-generated data, enforcing temporally consistent 4D dynamics. Finally, we apply simulation-grounded reinforcement learning to correct residual physical violations that are difficult to capture through explicit supervision. To evaluate fine-grained physical consistency beyond appearance-based metrics, we introduce a set of \textbf{4D world consistency evaluation} that probe geometric coherence, motion stability, and long-horizon physical plausibility. Experimental results demonstrate that Phys4D substantially improves fine-grained spatiotemporal and physical consistency compared to appearance-driven baselines, while maintaining strong generative performance. Our project page is available at https://sensational-brioche-7657e7.netlify.app/

摘要（中文）：近期的视频扩散模型作为大规模生成世界模型，已经取得了令人印象深刻的能力。然而，这些模型常常难以处理细粒度的物理一致性，表现出在时间上不符合物理规律的动态。 在这项工作中，我们提出了 \textbf{Phys4D}，一个从视频扩散模型中学习物理一致性 4D 世界表征的流程。Phys4D 采用\textbf{三阶段训练范式}，逐步将外观驱动的视频扩散模型提升为物理一致的 4D 世界表征。我们首先通过大规模伪监督预训练引导出稳健的几何和运动表征，为 4D 场景建模奠定基础。然后，我们使用仿真生成的数据进行基于物理原理的监督微调，从而保证时间上一致的 4D 动态。最后，我们应用基于仿真的强化学习来纠正难以通过显式监督捕获的残余物理违反。为了评估超越外观指标的细粒度物理一致性，我们引入了一组 \textbf{4D 世界一致性评估}，用于探测几何相干性、运动稳定性和长期物理合理性。实验结果表明，与外观驱动的基线相比，Phys4D 在保持强大生成性能的同时，显著提高了细粒度的时空和物理一致性。 我们的项目主页可访问 https://sensational-brioche-7657e7.netlify.app/

Authors: Haoran Lu, Shang Wu, Jianshu Zhang, Maojiang Su, Guo Ye, Chenwei Xu, Lie Lu, Pranav Maneriker, Fan Du, Manling Li, Zhaoran Wang, Han Liu

How to Peel with a Knife: Aligning Fine-Grained Manipulation with Human Preference

简要说明：本文提出了一种两阶段学习框架，用于处理具有隐式成功标准的机器人操作任务，以削皮刀为例，通过模仿学习和基于偏好的微调实现了较高的成功率和泛化能力。

TLDR: This paper presents a two-stage learning framework for robotic manipulation tasks with implicit success criteria, using peeling with a knife as an example, achieving high success rates and generalization through imitation learning and preference-based finetuning.

Abstract: Many essential manipulation tasks - such as food preparation, surgery, and craftsmanship - remain intractable for autonomous robots. These tasks are characterized not only by contact-rich, force-sensitive dynamics, but also by their "implicit" success criteria: unlike pick-and-place, task quality in these domains is continuous and subjective (e.g. how well a potato is peeled), making quantitative evaluation and reward engineering difficult. We present a learning framework for such tasks, using peeling with a knife as a representative example. Our approach follows a two-stage pipeline: first, we learn a robust initial policy via force-aware data collection and imitation learning, enabling generalization across object variations; second, we refine the policy through preference-based finetuning using a learned reward model that combines quantitative task metrics with qualitative human feedback, aligning policy behavior with human notions of task quality. Using only 50-200 peeling trajectories, our system achieves over 90% average success rates on challenging produce including cucumbers, apples, and potatoes, with performance improving by up to 40% through preference-based finetuning. Remarkably, policies trained on a single produce category exhibit strong zero-shot generalization to unseen in-category instances and to out-of-distribution produce from different categories while maintaining over 90% success rates.

摘要（中文）：许多重要的操作任务——例如食物准备、外科手术和工艺制作——对于自主机器人来说仍然难以处理。这些任务不仅以其富含接触、对力敏感的动力学为特征，还以其“隐式”成功标准为特征：与抓取放置不同，这些领域中的任务质量是连续且主观的（例如，土豆去皮的好坏），使得定量评估和奖励工程变得困难。我们提出了一个针对此类任务的学习框架，以使用刀具进行去皮作为代表性例子。我们的方法遵循一个两阶段流程：首先，我们通过力感知数据收集和模仿学习来学习一个鲁棒的初始策略，从而实现跨越对象变化的泛化；其次，我们使用一个结合了定量任务指标和定性人类反馈的学习奖励模型，通过基于偏好的微调来完善该策略，从而使策略行为与人类对任务质量的概念对齐。仅使用50-200条去皮轨迹，我们的系统在包括黄瓜、苹果和土豆在内的具有挑战性的农产品上实现了超过90%的平均成功率，并且通过基于偏好的微调，性能提高了高达40%。值得注意的是，在单个农产品类别上训练的策略对未见过的同类别实例以及来自不同类别的分布外农产品表现出强大的零样本泛化能力，同时保持超过90%的成功率。

Authors: Toru Lin, Shuying Deng, Zhao-Heng Yin, Pieter Abbeel, Jitendra Malik

ULTRA: Unified Multimodal Control for Autonomous Humanoid Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation

简要说明：该论文介绍了ULTRA，一个用于自主人形机器人全身运动和操作的统一框架。该框架结合了物理驱动的神经重定向和统一的多模态控制器，该控制器支持密集参考和稀疏任务规范，并在真实人形机器人上展示了从自我中心感知到目标条件行为的泛化能力。

TLDR: The paper introduces ULTRA, a unified framework for autonomous humanoid whole-body loco-manipulation that combines physics-driven neural retargeting and a unified multimodal controller which supports both dense references and sparse task specifications, demonstrating generalization to goal-conditioned behavior from egocentric perception on a real humanoid robot.

Abstract: Achieving autonomous and versatile whole-body loco-manipulation remains a central barrier to making humanoids practically useful. Yet existing approaches are fundamentally constrained: retargeted data are often scarce or low-quality; methods struggle to scale to large skill repertoires; and, most importantly, they rely on tracking predefined motion references rather than generating behavior from perception and high-level task specifications. To address these limitations, we propose ULTRA, a unified framework with two key components. First, we introduce a physics-driven neural retargeting algorithm that translates large-scale motion capture to humanoid embodiments while preserving physical plausibility for contact-rich interactions. Second, we learn a unified multimodal controller that supports both dense references and sparse task specifications, under sensing ranging from accurate motion-capture state to noisy egocentric visual inputs. We distill a universal tracking policy into this controller, compress motor skills into a compact latent space, and apply reinforcement learning finetuning to expand coverage and improve robustness under out-of-distribution scenarios. This enables coordinated whole-body behavior from sparse intent without test-time reference motions. We evaluate ULTRA in simulation and on a real Unitree G1 humanoid. Results show that ULTRA generalizes to autonomous, goal-conditioned whole-body loco-manipulation from egocentric perception, consistently outperforming tracking-only baselines with limited skills.

摘要（中文）：实现自主且通用的全身运动操作仍然是使人形机器人具有实际用途的核心障碍。然而，现有的方法从根本上受到限制：重定向数据通常稀缺或质量低下；这些方法难以扩展到大型技能库；最重要的是，它们依赖于跟踪预定义的运动参考，而不是从感知和高级任务规范中生成行为。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了ULTRA，这是一个具有两个关键组成部分的统一框架。首先，我们引入了一种物理驱动的神经重定向算法，该算法将大规模运动捕捉平移到人形机器人，同时保持富于接触的交互的物理合理性。其次，我们学习了一个统一的多模态控制器，该控制器支持密集参考和稀疏任务规范，且传感范围从精确的运动捕捉状态到嘈杂的自我中心视觉输入。我们将通用的跟踪策略提炼到该控制器中，将运动技能压缩到紧凑的潜在空间中，并应用强化学习微调来扩大覆盖范围并提高在分布外场景下的鲁棒性。这使得无需测试时参考运动即可从稀疏的意图实现协调的全身行为。我们在模拟和真实的宇树G1人形机器人上评估了ULTRA。结果表明，ULTRA能够从自我中心感知泛化到自主的、目标条件下的全身运动操作，持续优于具有有限技能的仅跟踪基线。
