Authors: Xialin He, Sirui Xu, Xinyao Li, Runpei Dong, Liuyu Bian, Yu-Xiong Wang, Liang-Yan Gui

Tether: Autonomous Functional Play with Correspondence-Driven Trajectory Warping

简要说明：本文介绍了一种名为Tether的自主机器人功能性玩耍方法，该方法利用对应驱动的轨迹扭曲和视觉语言模型进行持续改进，经过数小时的真实世界多任务玩耍后，能够产生高质量的数据集和专家级策略。

TLDR: The paper introduces Tether, a method for autonomous robotic functional play using correspondence-driven trajectory warping and vision-language models for continuous improvement, resulting in high-quality datasets and expert-level policies after hours of real-world multi-task play.

Abstract: The ability to conduct and learn from interaction and experience is a central challenge in robotics, offering a scalable alternative to labor-intensive human demonstrations. However, realizing such "play" requires (1) a policy robust to diverse, potentially out-of-distribution environment states, and (2) a procedure that continuously produces useful robot experience. To address these challenges, we introduce Tether, a method for autonomous functional play involving structured, task-directed interactions. First, we design a novel open-loop policy that warps actions from a small set of source demonstrations (<=10) by anchoring them to semantic keypoint correspondences in the target scene. We show that this design is extremely data-efficient and robust even under significant spatial and semantic variations. Second, we deploy this policy for autonomous functional play in the real world via a continuous cycle of task selection, execution, evaluation, and improvement, guided by the visual understanding capabilities of vision-language models. This procedure generates diverse, high-quality datasets with minimal human intervention. In a household-like multi-object setup, our method is the first to perform many hours of autonomous multi-task play in the real world starting from only a handful of demonstrations. This produces a stream of data that consistently improves the performance of closed-loop imitation policies over time, ultimately yielding over 1000 expert-level trajectories and training policies competitive with those learned from human-collected demonstrations.

摘要（中文）：在机器人领域，进行交互并从中学习的能力是一项核心挑战，它为劳动密集型的人工示教提供了一种可扩展的替代方案。然而，实现这种“玩耍”（play）需要：（1）一种对多样化的、潜在的分布外环境状态具有鲁棒性的策略，以及（2）一个能够持续产生有用的机器人经验的过程。为了应对这些挑战，我们引入了Tether，一种涉及结构化、任务导向型交互的自主功能性玩耍方法。首先，我们设计了一种新颖的开环策略，该策略通过将动作锚定到目标场景中的语义关键点对应关系，从而扭曲来自少量源演示 (<=10) 的动作。我们证明了这种设计即使在显著的空间和语义变化下也极其数据高效且鲁棒。其次，我们利用视觉语言模型的视觉理解能力，通过任务选择、执行、评估和改进的连续循环，在现实世界中部署该策略进行自主功能性玩耍。这个过程以最少的人工干预生成多样化的、高质量的数据集。在一个类似家庭的多物体设置中，我们的方法是第一个仅从少量演示开始就在现实世界中进行数小时自主多任务玩耍的方法。这产生了一个数据流，随着时间的推移，该数据流不断提高闭环模仿策略的性能，最终产生超过1000条专家级轨迹，并训练出具有与从人工收集的演示中学到的策略相媲美的性能。

HoMMI: Learning Whole-Body Mobile Manipulation from Human Demonstrations

简要说明：本文介绍了一种名为HoMMI的框架，该框架使用第一人称视角感知和跨具身的手眼策略，从无机器人的演示中学习全身移动操作，从而弥合人与机器人之间的差距。

TLDR: This paper introduces HoMMI, a framework for learning whole-body mobile manipulation from robot-free human demonstrations using egocentric sensing and a cross-embodiment hand-eye policy to bridge the human-to-robot gap.

Abstract: We present Whole-Body Mobile Manipulation Interface (HoMMI), a data collection and policy learning framework that learns whole-body mobile manipulation directly from robot-free human demonstrations. We augment UMI interfaces with egocentric sensing to capture the global context required for mobile manipulation, enabling portable, robot-free, and scalable data collection. However, naively incorporating egocentric sensing introduces a larger human-to-robot embodiment gap in both observation and action spaces, making policy transfer difficult. We explicitly bridge this gap with a cross-embodiment hand-eye policy design, including an embodiment agnostic visual representation; a relaxed head action representation; and a whole-body controller that realizes hand-eye trajectories through coordinated whole-body motion under robot-specific physical constraints. Together, these enable long-horizon mobile manipulation tasks requiring bimanual and whole-body coordination, navigation, and active perception. Results are best viewed on: https://hommi-robot.github.io

摘要（中文）：我们提出了全身移动操作界面(HoMMI)，一个直接从无机器人人类演示中学习全身移动操作的数据采集和策略学习框架。我们通过以自我为中心的感知增强UMI界面，以捕获移动操作所需的全局环境，从而实现便携式、无机器人且可扩展的数据采集。然而，简单地整合以自我为中心的感知会在观察和行动空间中引入更大的人机具身差异，从而使策略迁移变得困难。我们通过一种跨具身手眼策略设计，显式地弥合了这一差距，该设计包括一种与具身无关的视觉表示；一种宽松的头部行动表示；以及一个全身控制器，该控制器通过在机器人特定的物理约束下协调全身运动来实现手眼轨迹。总而言之，这些实现了需要双手和全身协调、导航和主动感知的长程移动操作任务。结果可在以下网址获得最佳观看体验：https://hommi-robot.github.io

Authors: Xiaomeng Xu, Jisang Park, Han Zhang, Eric Cousineau, Aditya Bhat, Jose Barreiros, Dian Wang, Shuran Song

What Does Flow Matching Bring To TD Learning?

简要说明：该论文研究了流匹配如何改进强化学习中的Q值函数估计，发现其原因是测试时的误差恢复和特征可塑性的增加，而不是分布强化学习。他们展示了显著的性能提升，尤其是在具有挑战性的高更新数据比率环境中。

TLDR: The paper investigates why flow matching improves Q-value function estimation in RL, finding that it's due to test-time error recovery and increased feature plasticity, not distributional RL. They demonstrate significant performance gains, especially in challenging high-update-to-data ratio environments.

Abstract: Recent work shows that flow matching can be effective for scalar Q-value function estimation in reinforcement learning (RL), but it remains unclear why or how this approach differs from standard critics. Contrary to conventional belief, we show that their success is not explained by distributional RL, as explicitly modeling return distributions can reduce performance. Instead, we argue that the use of integration for reading out values and dense velocity supervision at each step of this integration process for training improves TD learning via two mechanisms. First, it enables robust value prediction through \emph{test-time recovery}, whereby iterative computation through integration dampens errors in early value estimates as more integration steps are performed. This recovery mechanism is absent in monolithic critics. Second, supervising the velocity field at multiple interpolant values induces more \emph{plastic} feature learning within the network, allowing critics to represent non-stationary TD targets without discarding previously learned features or overfitting to individual TD targets encountered during training. We formalize these effects and validate them empirically, showing that flow-matching critics substantially outperform monolithic critics (2$\times$ in final performance and around 5$\times$ in sample efficiency) in settings where loss of plasticity poses a challenge e.g., in high-UTD online RL problems, while remaining stable during learning.

摘要（中文）：最近的研究表明，流匹配法在强化学习（RL）中的标量Q值函数估计方面是有效的，但这种方法与标准评论家（critic）的区别之处和原因尚不明确。与传统观点相反，我们证明了它们的成功并非源于分布强化学习，因为显式地对回报分布进行建模反而会降低性能。相反，我们认为，使用积分进行值读取，以及在该积分过程的每一步进行密集的速度监督训练，通过两种机制改进了时序差分（TD）学习。首先，它通过\emph{测试时恢复}实现稳健的值预测，即通过积分的迭代计算可以抑制早期值估计中的误差，随着积分步骤的增加，误差逐渐减小。这种恢复机制在单体评论家中是不存在的。其次，在多个插值点值上监督速度场，会在网络内部诱导出更具\emph{可塑性}的特征学习，允许评论家在不丢弃先前学习的特征或过度拟合训练期间遇到的单个TD目标的情况下，表示非平稳TD目标。 我们将这些影响形式化并进行实证验证，结果表明，在可塑性丧失构成挑战的环境中，例如高更新步数（UTD）在线RL问题中，流匹配评论家的性能明显优于单体评论家（最终性能提升2倍，样本效率提升约5倍），并且在学习过程中保持稳定。

Authors: Bhavya Agrawalla, Michal Nauman, Aviral Kumar

IPD: Boosting Sequential Policy with Imaginary Planning Distillation in Offline Reinforcement Learning

简要说明：该论文提出了想象规划蒸馏(IPD)，一种新的离线强化学习框架，通过使用MPC和学习的世界模型生成的想象最优轨迹来增强次优轨迹，从而提升决策转换器策略。 这种方法通过将准最优价值函数提炼到策略中，提高了在D4RL基准上的性能。

TLDR: The paper introduces Imaginary Planning Distillation (IPD), a novel offline RL framework that enhances decision transformer policies by augmenting suboptimal trajectories with imagined optimal rollouts generated using MPC and a learned world model. This approach improves performance on D4RL benchmarks by distilling a quasi-optimal value function into the policy.

Abstract: Decision transformer based sequential policies have emerged as a powerful paradigm in offline reinforcement learning (RL), yet their efficacy remains constrained by the quality of static datasets and inherent architectural limitations. Specifically, these models often struggle to effectively integrate suboptimal experiences and fail to explicitly plan for an optimal policy. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{Imaginary Planning Distillation (IPD)}, a novel framework that seamlessly incorporates offline planning into data generation, supervised training, and online inference. Our framework first learns a world model equipped with uncertainty measures and a quasi-optimal value function from the offline data. These components are utilized to identify suboptimal trajectories and augment them with reliable, imagined optimal rollouts generated via Model Predictive Control (MPC). A Transformer-based sequential policy is then trained on this enriched dataset, complemented by a value-guided objective that promotes the distillation of the optimal policy. By replacing the conventional, manually-tuned return-to-go with the learned quasi-optimal value function, IPD improves both decision-making stability and performance during inference. Empirical evaluations on the D4RL benchmark demonstrate that IPD significantly outperforms several state-of-the-art value-based and transformer-based offline RL methods across diverse tasks.

摘要（中文）：基于决策转换器的序列策略已成为离线强化学习 (RL) 中一种强大的范式，但其效力仍然受到静态数据集的质量和固有架构限制的约束。 具体而言，这些模型通常难以有效整合次优经验，并且无法显式地规划最优策略。 为了弥合这一差距，我们提出了 \textbf{虚构规划蒸馏 (IPD)}，这是一种新颖的框架，可将离线规划无缝地整合到数据生成、监督训练和在线推理中。 我们的框架首先从离线数据中学习一个配备了不确定性度量和准最优价值函数的世界模型。 这些组件用于识别次优轨迹，并通过模型预测控制 (MPC) 生成的可靠的、虚构的最优轨迹来增强它们。 然后，在此丰富的增强数据集上训练基于 Transformer 的序列策略，并辅以价值引导的目标，以促进最优策略的蒸馏。 通过用学习到的准最优价值函数替换传统的、手动调整的剩余回报 (return-to-go)，IPD 提高了推理期间决策的稳定性和性能。 在 D4RL 基准测试上的实证评估表明，在各种任务中，IPD 显著优于几种最先进的基于价值的和基于 Transformer 的离线 RL 方法。

Authors: Yihao Qin, Yuanfei Wang, Hang Zhou, Peiran Liu, Hao Dong, Yiding Ji

Unbiased Dynamic Pruning for Efficient Group-Based Policy Optimization

简要说明：本文介绍了动态剪枝策略优化（DPPO），通过动态剪枝和使用重要性抽样进行无偏梯度估计来加速LLM推理的分组相对策略优化（GRPO），同时采用密集提示打包以提高数据密度和硬件利用率，实验表明该方法显著提高了速度和准确性。

TLDR: This paper introduces Dynamic Pruning Policy Optimization (DPPO) to accelerate Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for LLM reasoning by dynamically pruning and using importance sampling for unbiased gradient estimation, alongside Dense Prompt Packing for improved data density and hardware utilization, demonstrating significant speedups and accuracy gains.

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) effectively scales LLM reasoning but incurs prohibitive computational costs due to its extensive group-based sampling requirement. While recent selective data utilization methods can mitigate this overhead, they could induce estimation bias by altering the underlying sampling distribution, compromising theoretical rigor and convergence behavior. To address this limitation, we propose Dynamic Pruning Policy Optimization (DPPO), a framework that enables dynamic pruning while preserving unbiased gradient estimation through importance sampling-based correction. By incorporating mathematically derived rescaling factors, DPPO significantly accelerates GRPO training without altering the optimization objective of the full-batch baseline. Furthermore, to mitigate the data sparsity induced by pruning, we introduce Dense Prompt Packing, a window-based greedy strategy that maximizes valid token density and hardware utilization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DPPO consistently accelerates training across diverse models and benchmarks. For instance, on Qwen3-4B trained on MATH, DPPO achieves 2.37$\times$ training speedup and outperforms GRPO by 3.36% in average accuracy across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks.

摘要（中文）：群组相对策略优化 (GRPO) 可以有效地扩展大型语言模型 (LLM) 的推理能力，但由于其广泛的基于群组的采样需求，会产生令人望而却步的计算成本。虽然最近的选择性数据利用方法可以减轻此开销，但它们可能通过改变底层采样分布来引入估计偏差，从而损害理论严谨性和收敛行为。为了解决这一局限性，我们提出了动态剪枝策略优化 (DPPO) 框架，该框架能够在动态剪枝的同时，通过基于重要性采样的校正，保持无偏梯度估计。通过引入数学推导的重缩放因子，DPPO 显著加速了 GRPO 的训练，而没有改变全批量基线的优化目标。此外，为了缓解剪枝引起的数据稀疏性，我们引入了密集提示打包 (Dense Prompt Packing)，这是一种基于窗口的贪婪策略，旨在最大化有效令牌密度和硬件利用率。广泛的实验表明，DPPO 可以持续加速各种模型和基准测试的训练。例如，在训练于 MATH 数据集上的 Qwen3-4B 模型上，DPPO 实现了 2.37 倍的训练加速，并在六个数学推理基准测试中比 GRPO 提高了 3.36% 的平均准确率。

Authors: Haodong Zhu, Yangyang Ren, Yanjing Li, Mingbao Lin, Linlin Yang, Xuhui Liu, Xiantong Zhen, Haiguang Liu, Baochang Zhang

Discriminative Perception via Anchored Description for Reasoning Segmentation

简要说明：该论文介绍了DPAD，一种通过加入判别感知来改进推理分割的方法，它使用描述性标题来使模型专注于独特的目标属性，并提高推理链的效率。在推理分割基准测试中显示出改进的性能和可解释性。

TLDR: The paper introduces DPAD, a method for improving reasoning segmentation by incorporating discriminative perception, using descriptive captions to focus the model on unique target attributes and improve reasoning chain efficiency. It shows improved performance and interpretability on reasoning segmentation benchmarks.

Abstract: Reasoning segmentation increasingly employs reinforcement learning to generate explanatory reasoning chains that guide Multimodal Large Language Models. While these geometric rewards are primarily confined to guiding the final localization, they are incapable of discriminating whether the reasoning process remains anchored on the referred region or strays into irrelevant context. Lacking this discriminative guidance, the model's reasoning often devolves into unfocused and verbose chains that ultimately fail to disambiguate and perceive the target in complex scenes. This suggests a need to complement the RL objective with Discriminative Perception, an ability to actively distinguish a target from its context. To realize this, we propose DPAD to compel the model to generate a descriptive caption of the referred object, which is then used to explicitly discriminate by contrasting the caption's semantic relevance to the referred object against the wider context. By optimizing for this discriminative capability, the model is forced to focus on the unique attributes of the target, leading to a more converged and efficient reasoning chain. The descriptive caption also serves as an interpretability rationale that aligns with the segmentation. Experiments on the benchmarks confirm the validity of our approach, delivering substantial performance gains, with the cIoU on ReasonSeg increasing by 3.09% and the reasoning chain length decreasing by approximately 42%. Code is available at https://github.com/mrazhou/DPAD

摘要（中文）：推理分割越来越多地采用强化学习来生成解释性推理链，从而指导多模态大型语言模型。 虽然这些几何奖励主要局限于引导最终的定位，但它们无法区分推理过程是否仍然锚定在所指区域，或是偏离到不相关的上下文中。 缺乏这种判别性引导，模型的推理常常退化为漫无目的且冗长的链条，最终无法在复杂场景中消除歧义并感知目标。 这表明需要以判别性感知来补充强化学习目标，判别性感知是一种主动将目标与其上下文区分开的能力。 为实现这一点，我们提出了DPAD，它迫使模型生成所指对象的描述性字幕，然后通过对比字幕的语义相关性与所指对象和更广泛的上下文，来显式地进行区分。 通过优化这种判别能力，模型被迫专注于目标的独特属性，从而产生更收敛和高效的推理链。 描述性字幕也充当了与分割对齐的可解释性依据。 在基准测试上的实验证实了我们方法的有效性，提供了显著的性能提升，ReasonSeg上的cIoU提高了3.09%，推理链长度减少了约42%。 代码可在https://github.com/mrazhou/DPAD获取。

Authors: Tao Yang, Qing Zhou, Yanliang Li, Qi Wang

SWE-CI: Evaluating Agent Capabilities in Maintaining Codebases via Continuous Integration

简要说明：该论文介绍了SWE-CI，一个新的基准，用于评估LLM驱动的Agent在持续集成工作流程中长期代码可维护性的能力，解决了现有静态代码修复基准的局限性。

TLDR: The paper introduces SWE-CI, a new benchmark for evaluating the long-term code maintainability capabilities of LLM-powered agents within a continuous integration workflow, addressing the limitations of existing static code repair benchmarks.

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in automating software engineering tasks such as static bug fixing, as evidenced by benchmarks like SWE-bench. However, in the real world, the development of mature software is typically predicated on complex requirement changes and long-term feature iterations -- a process that static, one-shot repair paradigms fail to capture. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{SWE-CI}, the first repository-level benchmark built upon the Continuous Integration loop, aiming to shift the evaluation paradigm for code generation from static, short-term \textit{functional correctness} toward dynamic, long-term \textit{maintainability}. The benchmark comprises 100 tasks, each corresponding on average to an evolution history spanning 233 days and 71 consecutive commits in a real-world code repository. SWE-CI requires agents to systematically resolve these tasks through dozens of rounds of analysis and coding iterations. SWE-CI provides valuable insights into how well agents can sustain code quality throughout long-term evolution.

摘要（中文）：大型语言模型 (LLM) 驱动的智能体已在自动化软件工程任务（如静态缺陷修复）中展现出强大的能力，SWE-bench等基准测试证明了这一点。然而，在现实世界中，成熟软件的开发通常基于复杂的需求变更和长期的特性迭代——而静态的、一次性修复范式无法捕捉到这一过程。为了弥合这一差距，我们提出了 \textbf{SWE-CI}，这是第一个基于持续集成循环的仓库级别基准测试，旨在将代码生成的评估范式从静态的、短期的 \textit{功能正确性} 转向动态的、长期的 \textit{可维护性}。该基准测试包含 100 个任务，每个任务平均对应于真实代码仓库中跨越 233 天和 71 个连续提交的演化历史。SWE-CI 要求智能体通过数十轮的分析和编码迭代来系统地解决这些任务。SWE-CI 提供了关于智能体在长期演化过程中维持代码质量的能力的重要洞察。

Authors: Jialong Chen, Xander Xu, Hu Wei, Chuan Chen, Bing Zhao

Specification-Driven Generation and Evaluation of Discrete-Event World Models via the DEVS Formalism

简要说明：本文提出了一种使用LLM和DEVS形式化方法，从自然语言规范自动生成和评估离散事件世界模型的方法，旨在弥合手动设计的模拟器和隐式神经模型之间的差距。

TLDR: This paper proposes a method for automatically generating and evaluating discrete-event world models from natural language specifications using LLMs and the DEVS formalism, aiming to bridge the gap between hand-engineered simulators and implicit neural models.

Abstract: World models are essential for planning and evaluation in agentic systems, yet existing approaches lie at two extremes: hand-engineered simulators that offer consistency and reproducibility but are costly to adapt, and implicit neural models that are flexible but difficult to constrain, verify, and debug over long horizons. We seek a principled middle ground that combines the reliability of explicit simulators with the flexibility of learned models, allowing world models to be adapted during online execution. By targeting a broad class of environments whose dynamics are governed by the ordering, timing, and causality of discrete events, such as queueing and service operations, embodied task planning, and message-mediated multi-agent coordination, we advocate explicit, executable discrete-event world models synthesized directly from natural-language specifications. Our approach adopts the DEVS formalism and introduces a staged LLM-based generation pipeline that separates structural inference of component interactions from component-level event and timing logic. To evaluate generated models without a unique ground truth, simulators emit structured event traces that are validated against specification-derived temporal and semantic constraints, enabling reproducible verification and localized diagnostics. Together, these contributions produce world models that are consistent over long-horizon rollouts, verifiable from observable behavior, and efficient to synthesize on demand during online execution.

摘要（中文）：世界模型对于智能体系统中的规划和评估至关重要，但现有方法却走向了两个极端：手工设计的模拟器具有一致性和可复现性，但适应成本高昂；隐式神经模型则具有灵活性，但难以约束、验证和调试长时程行为。我们寻求一种有原则的中间路线，将显式模拟器的可靠性与学习模型的灵活性相结合，从而允许世界模型在在线执行期间进行调整。针对一类广泛的环境，这些环境的动力学由离散事件的排序、时间和因果关系所决定，例如排队和服务操作、具身任务规划以及消息介导的多智能体协调，我们提倡直接从自然语言规范合成显式的、可执行的离散事件世界模型。我们的方法采用了DEVS形式化方法，并引入了一个基于分阶段LLM的生成流水线，该流水线将组件交互的结构推断与组件级别的事件和时序逻辑分离开来。为了在没有唯一真实标签的情况下评估生成的模型，模拟器会发出结构化的事件轨迹，这些轨迹会根据规范衍生的时序和语义约束进行验证，从而实现可复现的验证和局部诊断。总而言之，这些贡献产生了在长时程展开中保持一致、可从可观察行为验证，以及可以在在线执行期间按需高效合成的世界模型。

Authors: Zheyu Chen, Zhuohuan Li, Chuanhao Li

MAGE: Meta-Reinforcement Learning for Language Agents toward Strategic Exploration and Exploitation

简要说明：MAGE是一个用于LLM代理的元强化学习框架，通过多轮训练和基于群体的技术，实现在多智能体环境中的战略探索和利用，并表现出对未见过的对手的强大泛化能力。

TLDR: MAGE is a meta-RL framework for LLM agents that enables strategic exploration and exploitation in multi-agent environments through multi-episode training and population-based techniques, demonstrating strong generalization to unseen opponents.

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in learned tasks, yet they often struggle to adapt to non-stationary environments with feedback. While In-Context Learning and external memory offer some flexibility, they fail to internalize the adaptive ability required for long-term improvement. Meta-Reinforcement Learning (meta-RL) provides an alternative by embedding the learning process directly within the model. However, existing meta-RL approaches for LLMs focus primarily on exploration in single-agent settings, neglecting the strategic exploitation necessary for multi-agent environments. We propose MAGE, a meta-RL framework that empowers LLM agents for strategic exploration and exploitation. MAGE utilizes a multi-episode training regime where interaction histories and reflections are integrated into the context window. By using the final episode reward as the objective, MAGE incentivizes the agent to refine its strategy based on past experiences. We further combine population-based training with an agent-specific advantage normalization technique to enrich agent diversity and ensure stable learning. Experiment results show that MAGE outperforms existing baselines in both exploration and exploitation tasks. Furthermore, MAGE exhibits strong generalization to unseen opponents, suggesting it has internalized the ability for strategic exploration and exploitation. Code is available at https://github.com/Lu-Yang666/MAGE.

摘要（中文）：大型语言模型 (LLM) 智能体在已学习的任务中表现出卓越的能力，但它们通常难以适应具有反馈的非平稳环境。虽然上下文学习和外部记忆提供了一定的灵活性，但它们无法内化长期改进所需的适应能力。元强化学习 (meta-RL) 提供了一种替代方案，通过将学习过程直接嵌入到模型中。然而，现有的 LLM 元强化学习方法主要侧重于单智能体环境中的探索，忽略了多智能体环境所需的战略性利用。我们提出了 MAGE，一个赋予 LLM 智能体战略性探索和利用能力的元强化学习框架。MAGE 利用多轮训练机制，将交互历史和反思整合到上下文窗口中。通过使用最终轮次奖励作为目标，MAGE 激励智能体根据过去的经验改进其策略。我们进一步将基于种群的训练与智能体特定优势归一化技术相结合，以丰富智能体的多样性并确保稳定的学习。实验结果表明，MAGE 在探索和利用任务中均优于现有基线。此外，MAGE 表现出对未知对手的强大泛化能力，表明它已经内化了战略性探索和利用的能力。代码可在 https://github.com/Lu-Yang666/MAGE 获取。

Authors: Lu Yang, Zelai Xu, Minyang Xie, Jiaxuan Gao, Zhao Shok, Yu Wang, Yi Wu

Social Norm Reasoning in Multimodal Language Models: An Evaluation

简要说明：本文评估了五个多模态大型语言模型 (MLLM) 在文本和图像故事中进行规范推理的能力，发现 GPT-4o 表现最佳，但模型在处理复杂规范方面存在困难，尤其是在视觉环境中。

TLDR: This paper evaluates the norm reasoning capabilities of five Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) using text-based and image-based stories, finding that GPT-4o performs best but models struggle with complex norms, especially in visual contexts.

Abstract: In Multi-Agent Systems (MAS), agents are designed with social capabilities, allowing them to understand and reason about social concepts such as norms when interacting with others (e.g., inter-robot interactions). In Normative MAS (NorMAS), researchers study how norms develop, and how violations are detected and sanctioned. However, existing research in NorMAS use symbolic approaches (e.g., formal logic) for norm representation and reasoning whose application is limited to simplified environments. In contrast, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) present promising possibilities to develop software used by robots to identify and reason about norms in a wide variety of complex social situations embodied in text and images. However, prior work on norm reasoning have been limited to text-based scenarios. This paper investigates the norm reasoning competence of five MLLMs by evaluating their ability to answer norm-related questions based on thirty text-based and thirty image-based stories, and comparing their responses against humans. Our results show that MLLMs demonstrate superior performance in norm reasoning in text than in images. GPT-4o performs the best in both modalities offering the most promise for integration with MAS, followed by the free model Qwen-2.5VL. Additionally, all models find reasoning about complex norms challenging.

摘要（中文）：在多智能体系统（MAS）中，智能体被设计具有社会能力，使其能够在与其他智能体交互时（例如，机器人间交互）理解和推理规范等社会概念。在规范多智能体系统（NorMAS）中，研究人员研究规范如何发展，以及如何检测和惩罚违规行为。然而，现有的NorMAS研究采用符号方法（例如，形式逻辑）进行规范表示和推理，其应用受限于简化的环境。相比之下，多模态大型语言模型（MLLM）在开发机器人使用的软件方面展现出令人期待的可能性，以识别文本和图像中蕴含的各种复杂社会情景中的规范并进行推理。然而，先前关于规范推理的研究仅限于基于文本的情景。本文通过评估五种MLLM基于三十个文本故事和三十个图像故事回答规范相关问题的能力，并将它们的回答与人类进行比较，来研究它们的规范推理能力。我们的结果表明，MLLM在文本中的规范推理表现优于图像。GPT-4o在这两种模态中表现最佳，为与MAS集成提供了最大的希望，其次是免费模型Qwen-2.5VL。此外，所有模型都发现推理复杂的规范具有挑战性。

Authors: Oishik Chowdhury, Anushka Debnath, Bastin Tony Roy Savarimuthu

Molt Dynamics: Emergent Social Phenomena in Autonomous AI Agent Populations

简要说明：本文介绍了Molt Dynamics，观察了LLM代理的大规模多智能体环境中涌现的协调行为。他们分析了角色专业化、信息传播和协作任务解决，发现协作行为尚处于萌芽阶段。

TLDR: This paper introduces Molt Dynamics, observing emergent coordination behaviors in a large-scale multi-agent environment of LLM agents. They analyze role specialization, information dissemination, and cooperative task resolution, finding nascent cooperative behavior.

Abstract: MoltBook is a large-scale multi-agent coordination environment where over 770,000 autonomous LLM agents interact without human participation, offering the first opportunity we are aware of to observe emergent multi-agent coordination dynamics at this population scale. We introduce \textit{Molt Dynamics}: the emergent agent coordination behaviors, inter-agent communication dynamics, and role specialization patterns arising when autonomous agents operate as decentralized decision-makers in an unconstrained multi-agent environment. Through longitudinal observation of 90,704 active agents over three weeks, we characterize three aspects. First, spontaneous role specialization: network-based clustering reveals six structural roles (silhouette 0.91), though the result primarily reflects core-periphery organization -- 93.5\% of agents occupy a homogeneous peripheral cluster, with meaningful differentiation confined to the active minority. Second, decentralized information dissemination: cascade analysis of 10,323 inter-agent propagation events reveals power-law distributed cascade sizes ($α= 2.57 \pm 0.02$) and saturating adoption dynamics where adoption probability shows diminishing returns with repeated exposures (Cox hazard ratio 0.53, concordance 0.78). Third, distributed cooperative task resolution: 164 multi-agent collaborative events show detectable coordination patterns, but success rates are low (6.7\%, $p = 0.057$) and cooperative outcomes are significantly worse than a matched single-agent baseline (Cohen's $d = -0.88$), indicating emergent cooperative behavior is nascent. These findings establish an empirical baseline for coordination dynamics in decentralized autonomous agent systems, with implications for multi-agent system design, agent communication protocol engineering, and AI safety.

摘要（中文）：MoltBook是一个大规模的多智能体协作环境，其中超过77万个自主的LLM智能体在没有人类参与的情况下进行交互，为我们提供了首个机会来观察这种人口规模下涌现的多智能体协作动态。我们引入了*Molt动态*：即当自主智能体在不受约束的多智能体环境中作为去中心化的决策者运行时所涌现的智能体协作行为、智能体间通信动态以及角色专业化模式。通过对90,704个活跃智能体进行为期三周的纵向观察，我们刻画了三个方面。首先，自发角色专业化：基于网络的聚类揭示了六种结构性角色（轮廓系数0.91），尽管结果主要反映了核心-外围组织——93.5%的智能体占据一个同质的外围集群，有意义的差异化仅限于活跃的少数群体。其次，去中心化的信息传播：对10,323个智能体间传播事件的级联分析揭示了服从幂律分布的级联规模（$α= 2.57 \pm 0.02$）以及饱和的采纳动态，其中采纳概率随着重复暴露呈现递减的回报（Cox风险比0.53，一致性0.78）。第三，分布式合作任务解决：164个多智能体协作事件显示出可检测到的协作模式，但成功率较低（6.7%，$p = 0.057$），并且合作结果显著差于匹配的单智能体基线（Cohen's $d = -0.88$），表明涌现出的合作行为尚处于萌芽阶段。这些发现为去中心化自主智能体系统中的协作动态建立了一个经验基线，对多智能体系统设计、智能体通信协议工程和人工智能安全具有重要意义。

Authors: Brandon Yee, Krishna Sharma

PhyPrompt: RL-based Prompt Refinement for Physically Plausible Text-to-Video Generation

简要说明：该论文介绍了PhyPrompt，一个强化学习框架，通过优化prompt来提高文本到视频生成的物理合理性。该框架通过组合课程和零样本迁移学习实现了最先进的结果。

TLDR: The paper introduces PhyPrompt, a reinforcement learning framework that refines prompts to improve the physical plausibility of text-to-video generation, achieving state-of-the-art results with a compositional curriculum and zero-shot transferability.

Abstract: State-of-the-art text-to-video (T2V) generators frequently violate physical laws despite high visual quality. We show this stems from insufficient physical constraints in prompts rather than model limitations: manually adding physics details reliably produces physically plausible videos, but requires expertise and does not scale. We present PhyPrompt, a two-stage reinforcement learning framework that automatically refines prompts for physically realistic generation. First, we fine-tune a large language model on a physics-focused Chain-of-Thought dataset to integrate principles like object motion and force interactions while preserving user intent. Second, we apply Group Relative Policy Optimization with a dynamic reward curriculum that initially prioritizes semantic fidelity, then progressively shifts toward physical commonsense. This curriculum achieves synergistic optimization: PhyPrompt-7B reaches 40.8\% joint success on VideoPhy2 (8.6pp gain), improving physical commonsense by 11pp (55.8\% to 66.8\%) while simultaneously increasing semantic adherence by 4.4pp (43.4\% to 47.8\%). Remarkably, our curriculum exceeds single-objective training on both metrics, demonstrating compositional prompt discovery beyond conventional multi-objective trade-offs. PhyPrompt outperforms GPT-4o (+3.8\% joint) and DeepSeek-V3 (+2.2\%, 100$\times$ larger) using only 7B parameters. The approach transfers zero-shot across diverse T2V architectures (Lavie, VideoCrafter2, CogVideoX-5B) with up to 16.8\% improvement, establishing that domain-specialized reinforcement learning with compositional curricula surpasses general-purpose scaling for physics-aware generation.

摘要（中文）：最先进的文本到视频 (T2V) 生成器经常在具有高视觉质量的同时违反物理定律。我们表明，这源于提示中物理约束的不足，而非模型本身的局限性：手动添加物理细节可以可靠地生成符合物理规律的视频，但需要专业知识且不具备可扩展性。我们提出了 PhyPrompt，一个两阶段强化学习框架，可以自动优化提示，以实现物理上逼真的生成。首先，我们在一个以物理为中心的思维链数据集上微调一个大型语言模型，以整合诸如物体运动和力相互作用等原则，同时保留用户意图。其次，我们应用具有动态奖励课程的分组相对策略优化，该课程最初优先考虑语义保真度，然后逐步转向物理常识。该课程实现了协同优化：PhyPrompt-7B 在 VideoPhy2 上达到了 40.8% 的联合成功率（提高 8.6 个百分点），将物理常识提高了 11 个百分点（从 55.8% 提升到 66.8%），同时语义一致性也提高了 4.4 个百分点（从 43.4% 提升到 47.8%）。值得注意的是，我们的课程在两项指标上都超越了单目标训练，展示了超越传统多目标权衡的组合提示发现。PhyPrompt 仅使用 7B 参数就超过了 GPT-4o（+3.8% 联合）和 DeepSeek-V3（+2.2%，规模大 100 倍）。该方法在各种 T2V 架构（Lavie、VideoCrafter2、CogVideoX-5B）上实现了零样本迁移，并具有高达 16.8% 的改进，这表明，对于具有物理感知能力的生成，具有组合课程的领域专业强化学习超越了通用模型规模扩展。

Authors: Shang Wu, Chenwei Xu, Zhuofan Xia, Weijian Li, Lie Lu, Pranav Maneriker, Fan Du, Manling Li, Han Liu

Beyond Pixel Histories: World Models with Persistent 3D State

简要说明：该论文介绍了 PERSIST，一种通过模拟潜在 3D 场景来提高交互式视频生成中的空间记忆、3D 一致性和长时程稳定性的世界模型，从而实现新颖的 3D 环境合成和几何感知控制。

TLDR: The paper introduces PERSIST, a world model that simulates a latent 3D scene to improve spatial memory, 3D consistency, and long-horizon stability in interactive video generation, enabling novel 3D environment synthesis and geometry-aware control.

Abstract: Interactive world models continually generate video by responding to a user's actions, enabling open-ended generation capabilities. However, existing models typically lack a 3D representation of the environment, meaning 3D consistency must be implicitly learned from data, and spatial memory is restricted to limited temporal context windows. This results in an unrealistic user experience and presents significant obstacles to down-stream tasks such as training agents. To address this, we present PERSIST, a new paradigm of world model which simulates the evolution of a latent 3D scene: environment, camera, and renderer. This allows us to synthesize new frames with persistent spatial memory and consistent geometry. Both quantitative metrics and a qualitative user study show substantial improvements in spatial memory, 3D consistency, and long-horizon stability over existing methods, enabling coherent, evolving 3D worlds. We further demonstrate novel capabilities, including synthesising diverse 3D environments from a single image, as well as enabling fine-grained, geometry-aware control over generated experiences by supporting environment editing and specification directly in 3D space. Project page: https://francelico.github.io/persist.github.io

摘要（中文）：交互式世界模型通过响应用户的动作来持续生成视频，从而实现开放式的生成能力。然而，现有的模型通常缺乏环境的3D表示，这意味着3D一致性必须从数据中隐式学习，并且空间记忆被限制在有限的时间上下文窗口内。这导致不真实的用户体验，并为训练代理等下游任务带来重大障碍。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了PERSIST，一种新的世界模型范式，它模拟了潜在3D场景的演变：环境、相机和渲染器。这使我们能够合成具有持久空间记忆和一致几何体的新帧。定量指标和定性用户研究都表明，与现有方法相比，在空间记忆、3D一致性和长程稳定性方面都有显著改进，从而能够生成连贯的、演变的3D世界。 我们进一步展示了新颖的功能，包括从单个图像合成多样化的3D环境，以及通过直接在3D空间中支持环境编辑和规范，实现对生成体验的精细、几何感知控制。项目主页：https://francelico.github.io/persist.github.io

Authors: Samuel Garcin, Thomas Walker, Steven McDonagh, Tim Pearce, Hakan Bilen, Tianyu He, Kaixin Wang, Jiang Bian

Pointer-CAD: Unifying B-Rep and Command Sequences via Pointer-based Edges & Faces Selection

简要说明：该论文提出了 Pointer-CAD，一种新颖的基于 LLM 的 CAD 生成框架，它使用基于指针的命令序列来整合 B-rep 几何信息，从而改进了复杂的编辑操作并减少了拓扑错误，优于现有方法。

TLDR: The paper introduces Pointer-CAD, a novel LLM-based CAD generation framework that uses pointer-based command sequences to incorporate B-rep geometric information, improving complex editing and reducing topological errors compared to existing methods.

Abstract: Constructing computer-aided design (CAD) models is labor-intensive but essential for engineering and manufacturing. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have inspired the LLM-based CAD generation by representing CAD as command sequences. But these methods struggle in practical scenarios because command sequence representation does not support entity selection (e.g. faces or edges), limiting its ability to support complex editing operations such as chamfer or fillet. Further, the discretization of a continuous variable during sketch and extrude operations may result in topological errors. To address these limitations, we present Pointer-CAD, a novel LLM-based CAD generation framework that leverages a pointer-based command sequence representation to explicitly incorporate the geometric information of B-rep models into sequential modeling. In particular, Pointer-CAD decomposes CAD model generation into steps, conditioning the generation of each subsequent step on both the textual description and the B-rep generated from previous steps. Whenever an operation requires the selection of a specific geometric entity, the LLM predicts a Pointer that selects the most feature-consistent candidate from the available set. Such a selection operation also reduces the quantization error in the command sequence-based representation. To support the training of Pointer-CAD, we develop a data annotation pipeline that produces expert-level natural language descriptions and apply it to build a dataset of approximately 575K CAD models. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that Pointer-CAD effectively supports the generation of complex geometric structures and reduces segmentation error to an extremely low level, achieving a significant improvement over prior command sequence methods, thereby significantly mitigating the topological inaccuracies introduced by quantization error.

摘要（中文）：构建计算机辅助设计（CAD）模型是一项劳动密集型但对工程和制造至关重要的任务。大型语言模型（LLM）的最新进展启发了基于LLM的CAD生成方法，通过将CAD表示为命令序列。但这些方法在实际场景中表现不佳，因为命令序列表示不支持实体选择（例如面或边），从而限制了其对倒角或圆角等复杂编辑操作的支持能力。此外，草图和拉伸操作过程中连续变量的离散化可能会导致拓扑错误。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了一种新型的基于LLM的CAD生成框架Pointer-CAD，它利用基于指针的命令序列表示，将B-rep模型的几何信息显式地融入到顺序建模中。具体而言，Pointer-CAD将CAD模型生成分解为多个步骤，并将每个后续步骤的生成置于文本描述以及从先前步骤生成的B-rep之上。每当操作需要选择特定的几何实体时，LLM会预测一个指针，该指针从可用集合中选择特征最一致的候选对象。这种选择操作还可以减少基于命令序列表示中的量化误差。为了支持Pointer-CAD的训练，我们开发了一个数据标注流程，该流程生成专家级别的自然语言描述，并将其应用于构建一个包含约57.5万个CAD模型的数据集。大量的实验结果表明，Pointer-CAD有效地支持复杂几何结构的生成，并将分割误差降低到极低的水平，与先前的命令序列方法相比实现了显著的改进，从而大大降低了量化误差引入的拓扑不准确性。

Authors: Dacheng Qi, Chenyu Wang, Jingwei Xu, Tianzhe Chu, Zibo Zhao, Wen Liu, Wenrui Ding, Yi Ma, Shenghua Gao

SSR: A Generic Framework for Text-Aided Map Compression for Localization

简要说明：该论文提出了SSR，一种文本辅助的地图压缩框架，利用大型语言模型和轻量级图像特征来降低机器人定位任务的内存和带宽成本，并且比其他基线方法实现了两倍的压缩效果。

TLDR: This paper introduces SSR, a text-aided map compression framework leveraging large language models and lightweight image features to reduce memory and bandwidth costs for robot localization tasks, achieving 2x better compression than baselines.

Abstract: Mapping is crucial in robotics for localization and downstream decision-making. As robots are deployed in ever-broader settings, the maps they rely on continue to increase in size. However, storing these maps indefinitely (cold storage), transferring them across networks, or sending localization queries to cloud-hosted maps imposes prohibitive memory and bandwidth costs. We propose a text-enhanced compression framework that reduces both memory and bandwidth footprints while retaining high-fidelity localization. The key idea is to treat text as an alternative modality: one that can be losslessly compressed with large language models. We propose leveraging lightweight text descriptions combined with very small image feature vectors, which capture "complementary information" as a compact representation for the mapping task. Building on this, our novel technique, Similarity Space Replication (SSR), learns an adaptive image embedding in one shot that captures only the information "complementary" to the text descriptions. We validate our compression framework on multiple downstream localization tasks, including Visual Place Recognition as well as object-centric Monte Carlo localization in both indoor and outdoor settings. SSR achieves 2 times better compression than competing baselines on state-of-the-art datasets, including TokyoVal, Pittsburgh30k, Replica, and KITTI.

摘要（中文）：摘要： 地图构建在机器人学中对于定位和下游决策至关重要。随着机器人部署在越来越广泛的环境中，它们所依赖的地图规模持续增大。然而，无限期地存储这些地图（冷存储）、跨网络传输它们，或向云托管地图发送定位查询，都会带来令人望而却步的内存和带宽成本。我们提出了一种文本增强的压缩框架，该框架既能减少内存和带宽占用，又能保持高保真定位效果。其核心思想是将文本视为一种替代模式：一种可以用大型语言模型进行无损压缩的模式。我们提出利用轻量级文本描述结合非常小的图像特征向量，它们捕获“互补信息”，作为地图构建任务的紧凑表示。在此基础上，我们新颖的技术“相似性空间复制（SSR）”以一次性方式学习自适应图像嵌入，该嵌入仅捕获文本描述“互补”的信息。我们在多个下游定位任务中验证了我们的压缩框架，包括视觉位置识别以及室内和室外环境中的以对象为中心的蒙特卡洛定位。在包括 TokyoVal、Pittsburgh30k、Replica 和 KITTI 在内的最先进数据集上，SSR 的压缩效果比竞争基线提高了 2 倍。

Authors: Mohammad Omama, Po-han Li, Harsh Goel, Minkyu Choi, Behdad Chalaki, Vaishnav Tadiparthi, Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub, Ehsan Moradi Pari, Sandeep P. Chinchali

ViterbiPlanNet: Injecting Procedural Knowledge via Differentiable Viterbi for Planning in Instructional Videos

简要说明：ViterbiPlanNet 引入了一个可微的维特比层，将程序知识图显式地集成到教学视频的动作规划中，以更少的参数实现了 SOTA 性能。

TLDR: ViterbiPlanNet introduces a differentiable Viterbi layer to explicitly integrate procedural knowledge graphs into action planning for instructional videos, achieving SOTA performance with significantly fewer parameters.

Abstract: Procedural planning aims to predict a sequence of actions that transforms an initial visual state into a desired goal, a fundamental ability for intelligent agents operating in complex environments. Existing approaches typically rely on large-scale models that learn procedural structures implicitly, resulting in limited sample-efficiency and high computational cost. In this work we introduce ViterbiPlanNet, a principled framework that explicitly integrates procedural knowledge into the learning process through a Differentiable Viterbi Layer (DVL). The DVL embeds a Procedural Knowledge Graph (PKG) directly with the Viterbi decoding algorithm, replacing non-differentiable operations with smooth relaxations that enable end-to-end optimization. This design allows the model to learn through graph-based decoding. Experiments on CrossTask, COIN, and NIV demonstrate that ViterbiPlanNet achieves state-of-the-art performance with an order of magnitude fewer parameters than diffusion- and LLM-based planners. Extensive ablations show that performance gains arise from our differentiable structure-aware training rather than post-hoc refinement, resulting in improved sample efficiency and robustness to shorter unseen horizons. We also address testing inconsistencies establishing a unified testing protocol with consistent splits and evaluation metrics. With this new protocol, we run experiments multiple times and report results using bootstrapping to assess statistical significance.

摘要（中文）：程序化规划旨在预测一个行动序列，该序列将初始视觉状态转化为期望的目标，这是智能体在复杂环境中运作的基本能力。现有的方法通常依赖于大规模模型来隐式地学习程序化结构，导致样本效率有限和计算成本高昂。在这项工作中，我们引入了 ViterbiPlanNet，这是一个原则性框架，通过可微分的维特比层 (DVL) 将程序化知识显式地集成到学习过程中。DVL 将程序化知识图 (PKG) 通过维特比解码算法直接嵌入，用平滑松弛替换不可微分的操作，从而实现端到端的优化。这种设计允许模型通过基于图的解码进行学习。在 CrossTask、COIN 和 NIV 上的实验表明，ViterbiPlanNet 实现了最先进的性能，其参数数量比基于扩散和基于 LLM 的规划器少一个数量级。大量的消融实验表明，性能提升来源于我们可微分的、结构感知的训练，而不是事后改进，从而提高了样本效率和对较短未见视野的鲁棒性。我们还解决了测试不一致的问题，建立了一个统一的测试协议，具有一致的划分和评估指标。通过这一新协议，我们多次运行实验，并使用自举法报告结果以评估统计显著性。

Authors: Luigi Seminara, Davide Moltisanti, Antonino Furnari

EmbodiedSplat: Online Feed-Forward Semantic 3DGS for Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Understanding

简要说明：EmbodiedSplat 提出了一种在线、前馈的 3D 高斯溅射 (3DGS) 方法，该方法能够从流式图像中实时重建和语义理解 3D 场景，并利用 CLIP 嵌入实现开放词汇的语义理解。

TLDR: EmbodiedSplat introduces an online, feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) method that reconstructs and semantically understands 3D scenes from streaming images in real-time, leveraging CLIP embeddings for open-vocabulary semantic understanding.

Abstract: Understanding a 3D scene immediately with its exploration is essential for embodied tasks, where an agent must construct and comprehend the 3D scene in an online and nearly real-time manner. In this study, we propose EmbodiedSplat, an online feed-forward 3DGS for open-vocabulary scene understanding that enables simultaneous online 3D reconstruction and 3D semantic understanding from the streaming images. Unlike existing open-vocabulary 3DGS methods which are typically restricted to either offline or per-scene optimization setting, our objectives are two-fold: 1) Reconstructs the semantic-embedded 3DGS of the entire scene from over 300 streaming images in an online manner. 2) Highly generalizable to novel scenes with feed-forward design and supports nearly real-time 3D semantic reconstruction when combined with real-time 2D models. To achieve these objectives, we propose an Online Sparse Coefficients Field with a CLIP Global Codebook where it binds the 2D CLIP embeddings to each 3D Gaussian while minimizing memory consumption and preserving the full semantic generalizability of CLIP. Furthermore, we generate 3D geometric-aware CLIP features by aggregating the partial point cloud of 3DGS through 3D U-Net to compensate the 3D geometric prior to 2D-oriented language embeddings. Extensive experiments on diverse indoor datasets, including ScanNet, ScanNet++, and Replica, demonstrate both the effectiveness and efficiency of our method. Check out our project page in https://0nandon.github.io/EmbodiedSplat/.

摘要（中文）：理解一个3D场景并同时进行探索对于具身任务至关重要，在这些任务中，智能体必须以在线且近乎实时的方式构建和理解3D场景。在本研究中，我们提出EmbodiedSplat，一种用于开放词汇场景理解的在线前馈3D高斯溅射算法(3DGS)，它可以从流式图像中同时进行在线3D重建和3D语义理解。与现有通常局限于离线或单场景优化设置的开放词汇3DGS方法不同，我们的目标是双重的：1）以在线方式从超过300张流式图像中重建整个场景的语义嵌入式3DGS。 2）凭借前馈设计高度泛化到新场景，并在与实时2D模型结合时支持近乎实时的3D语义重建。为了实现这些目标，我们提出了一种具有CLIP全局码本的在线稀疏系数场，该码本将2D CLIP嵌入绑定到每个3D高斯，同时最大限度地减少内存消耗并保留CLIP的完整语义泛化能力。此外，我们通过3D U-Net聚合3DGS的部分点云，来生成3D几何感知CLIP特征，以弥补2D导向的语言嵌入的3D几何先验。在包括ScanNet、ScanNet++和Replica在内的各种室内数据集上的大量实验证明了我们方法的有效性和效率。请查看我们的项目页面：https://0nandon.github.io/EmbodiedSplat/。

Authors: Seungjun Lee, Zihan Wang, Yunsong Wang, Gim Hee Lee

Spatial Causal Prediction in Video

简要说明：该论文介绍了空间因果预测（SCP），一个用于评估视频模型中空间推理能力的基准，重点关注对未见空间状态和因果关系的预测。 结果表明，当前模型与人类的表现之间存在显着差距。

TLDR: The paper introduces Spatial Causal Prediction (SCP), a benchmark for evaluating spatial reasoning capabilities in video models, emphasizing prediction of unseen spatial states and causal relationships. Results indicate significant gaps between current models and human performance.

Abstract: Spatial reasoning, the ability to understand spatial relations, causality, and dynamic evolution, is central to human intelligence and essential for real-world applications such as autonomous driving and robotics. Existing studies, however, primarily assess models on visible spatio-temporal understanding, overlooking their ability to infer unseen past or future spatial states. In this work, we introduce Spatial Causal Prediction (SCP), a new task paradigm that challenges models to reason beyond observation and predict spatial causal outcomes. We further construct SCP-Bench, a benchmark comprising 2,500 QA pairs across 1,181 videos spanning diverse viewpoints, scenes, and causal directions, to support systematic evaluation. Through comprehensive experiments on {23} state-of-the-art models, we reveal substantial gaps between human and model performance, limited temporal extrapolation, and weak causal grounding. We further analyze key factors influencing performance and propose perception-enhancement and reasoning-guided strategies toward advancing spatial causal intelligence. The project page is https://guangstrip.github.io/SCP-Bench.

摘要（中文）：摘要： 空间推理，即理解空间关系、因果关系和动态演变的能力，是人类智能的核心，对自动驾驶和机器人学等现实应用至关重要。然而，现有研究主要评估模型在可见时空理解上的能力，忽略了它们推断未见过去或未来空间状态的能力。在这项工作中，我们引入了空间因果预测（SCP），一种新的任务范式，旨在挑战模型超越观测进行推理并预测空间因果结果。我们进一步构建了SCP-Bench，一个包含2500组问答对的基准，横跨1181个视频，涵盖不同的视角、场景和因果方向，以支持系统性评估。通过对{23}个最先进模型的全面实验，我们揭示了人类和模型表现之间的显著差距，有限的时间外推能力以及薄弱的因果基础。我们进一步分析了影响性能的关键因素，并提出了感知增强和推理引导的策略，旨在提升空间因果智能。项目主页是https://guangstrip.github.io/SCP-Bench。

Authors: Yanguang Zhao, Jie Yang, Shengqiong Wu, Shutong Hu, Hongbo Qiu, Yu Wang, Guijia Zhang, Tan Kai Ze, Hao Fei, Chia-Wen Lin, Mong-Li Lee, Wynne Hsu

DeepScan: A Training-Free Framework for Visually Grounded Reasoning in Large Vision-Language Models

简要说明：该论文介绍了一种名为 DeepScan 的免训练框架，用于大型视觉-语言模型 (LVLM) 中的视觉基础推理。该框架通过分层扫描、重新聚焦和证据增强推理来提高准确性和可解释性。

TLDR: The paper introduces DeepScan, a training-free framework for visually grounded reasoning in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) that improves accuracy and interpretability by employing hierarchical scanning, refocusing, and evidence-enhanced reasoning.

Abstract: Humans can robustly localize visual evidence and provide grounded answers even in noisy environments by identifying critical cues and then relating them to the full context in a bottom-up manner. Inspired by this, we propose DeepScan, a training-free framework that combines Hierarchical Scanning, Refocusing, and Evidence-Enhanced Reasoning for visually grounded reasoning in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs). Unlike existing methods that pursue one-shot localization of complete evidence, Hierarchical Scanning performs local cue exploration and multi-scale evidence extraction to recover evidence in a bottom-up manner, effectively mitigating the impacts of distractive context. Refocusing then optimizes the localized evidence view through collaboration of LVLMs and visual experts. Finally, Evidence-Enhanced Reasoning aggregates multi-granular views via a hybrid evidence memory and yields accurate and interpretable answers. Experimental results demonstrate that DeepScan significantly boosts LVLMs in diverse visual tasks, especially in fine-grained visual understanding. It achieves 90.6% overall accuracy on V* when integrated with Qwen2.5-VL-7B. Moreover, DeepScan provides consistent improvements for LVLMs across various architectures and model scales without additional adaptation cost.

摘要（中文）：人类能够在嘈杂环境中稳健地定位视觉证据并提供基于事实的答案，这得益于他们以自下而上的方式识别关键线索并将其与完整上下文相关联的能力。受此启发，我们提出 DeepScan，这是一个无需训练的框架，它结合了分层扫描、重新聚焦和证据增强推理，用于大型视觉-语言模型（LVLM）中的视觉基础推理。与现有追求完整证据的一次性定位方法不同，分层扫描执行局部线索探索和多尺度证据提取，以自下而上的方式恢复证据，有效减轻干扰性上下文的影响。 重新聚焦随后通过 LVLM 和视觉专家的协作来优化局部证据视图。最后，证据增强推理通过混合证据记忆聚合多粒度视图，并产生准确且可解释的答案。实验结果表明，DeepScan 在各种视觉任务中显著提升了 LVLM 的性能，尤其是在细粒度视觉理解方面。当与 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 集成时，它在 V* 上达到了 90.6% 的总体准确率。此外，DeepScan 为各种架构和模型规模的 LVLM 提供了持续的改进，而无需额外的适应成本。

Authors: Yangfu Li, Hongjian Zhan, Jiawei Chen, Yuning Gong, Qi Liu, Yue Lu

Hazard-Aware Traffic Scene Graph Generation

简要说明：本文提出了一种新的任务，即交通场景图生成，专注于自我车辆与潜在危险之间的危险感知关系，利用交通事故数据和深度线索来提高安全相关性和以自我为中心的推理。

TLDR: This paper introduces a novel task, Traffic Scene Graph Generation, focusing on hazard-aware relationships between the ego vehicle and potential hazards, using traffic accident data and depth cues to improve safety-relevance and ego-centric reasoning.

Abstract: Maintaining situational awareness in complex driving scenarios is challenging. It requires continuously prioritizing attention among extensive scene entities and understanding how prominent hazards might affect the ego vehicle. While existing studies excel at detecting specific semantic categories and visually salient regions, they lack the ability to assess safety-relevance. Meanwhile, the generic spatial predicates either for foreground objects only or for all scene entities modeled by existing scene graphs are inadequate for driving scenarios. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel task, Traffic Scene Graph Generation, which captures traffic-specific relations between prominent hazards and the ego vehicle. We propose a novel framework that explicitly uses traffic accident data and depth cues to supplement visual features and semantic information for reasoning. The output traffic scene graphs provide intuitive guidelines that stress prominent hazards by color-coding their severity and notating their effect mechanism and relative location to the ego vehicle. We create relational annotations on Cityscapes dataset and evaluate our model on 10 tasks from 5 perspectives. The results in comparative experiments and ablation studies demonstrate our capacity in ego-centric reasoning for hazard-aware traffic scene understanding.

摘要（中文）：在复杂的驾驶场景中维持情境感知具有挑战性。它需要持续地在大量的场景实体之间优先分配注意力，并理解显著的危险可能如何影响自车。虽然现有的研究擅长于检测特定的语义类别和视觉显著区域，但它们缺乏评估安全相关性的能力。同时，现有的场景图所建模的通用空间谓词要么只针对前景对象，要么针对所有场景实体，对于驾驶场景来说是不够的。为了弥合这一差距，我们引入了一个新的任务，即交通场景图生成，它捕捉了显著危险和自车之间特定于交通的关系。我们提出了一种新的框架，该框架显式地利用交通事故数据和深度线索来补充视觉特征和语义信息以进行推理。 输出的交通场景图提供了直观的指导，通过颜色编码来强调显著危险的严重程度，并注明其影响机制以及相对于自车的位置。 我们在Cityscapes数据集上创建了关系标注，并从五个角度评估了我们模型在10个任务上的表现。比较实验和消融研究的结果表明了我们在以自我为中心的推理能力，从而实现危险感知的交通场景理解。

Authors: Yaoqi Huang, Julie Stephany Berrio, Mao Shan, Stewart Worrall

Beyond Accuracy: Evaluating Visual Grounding In Multimodal Medical Reasoning

简要说明：本文介绍了一个针对医学VQA的反事实评估框架，揭示了强化学习模型常常利用捷径并生成无根据的视觉声明，尽管实现了高准确率。作者建议使用视觉依赖性评分（VRS）、图像敏感度（IS）和幻觉视觉推理率（HVRR）等指标来更好地评估视觉基础。

TLDR: This paper introduces a counterfactual evaluation framework for medical VQA, revealing that reinforcement learning models often exploit shortcuts and generate ungrounded visual claims despite achieving high accuracy. The authors propose using metrics like Visual Reliance Score (VRS), Image Sensitivity (IS), and Hallucinated Visual Reasoning Rate (HVRR) to better assess visual grounding.

Abstract: Recent work shows that text-only reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) can match or outperform image-text RLVR on multimodal medical VQA benchmarks, suggesting current evaluation protocols may fail to measure causal visual dependence. We introduce a counterfactual evaluation framework using real, blank, and shuffled images across four medical VQA benchmarks: PathVQA, PMC-VQA, SLAKE, and VQA-RAD. Beyond accuracy, we measure Visual Reliance Score (VRS), Image Sensitivity (IS), and introduce Hallucinated Visual Reasoning Rate (HVRR) to detect cases where models generate visual claims despite producing image-invariant answers. Our findings reveal that RLVR improves accuracy while degrading visual grounding: text-only RLVR achieves negative VRS on PathVQA (-0.09), performing better with mismatched images, while image-text RLVR reduces image sensitivity to 39.8% overall despite improving accuracy. On VQA-RAD, both variants achieve 63% accuracy through different mechanisms: text-only RLVR retains 81% performance with blank images, while image-text RLVR shows only 29% image sensitivity. Models generate visual claims in 68-74% of responses, yet 38-43% are ungrounded (HVRR). These findings demonstrate that accuracy-only rewards enable shortcut exploitation, and progress requires grounding-aware evaluation protocols and training objectives that explicitly enforce visual dependence.

摘要（中文）：近期研究表明，采用可验证奖励的纯文本强化学习（RLVR）在多模态医学VQA基准测试中能够达到或超越图像-文本RLVR的性能，这暗示当前的评估协议可能无法衡量因果视觉依赖性。我们引入了一种反事实评估框架，在四个医学VQA基准测试（PathVQA、PMC-VQA、SLAKE和VQA-RAD）中使用真实图像、空白图像和打乱图像。除了准确率之外，我们还测量了视觉依赖度得分（VRS）、图像敏感度（IS），并引入了幻觉视觉推理率（HVRR），以检测模型产生视觉主张但答案与图像无关的情况。我们的发现表明，RLVR提高了准确率，但降低了视觉基础：纯文本RLVR在PathVQA上实现了负VRS（-0.09），在使用不匹配图像时表现更佳，而图像-文本RLVR虽然提高了准确率，但总体图像敏感度却降至39.8%。在VQA-RAD上，两种变体都通过不同的机制实现了63%的准确率：纯文本RLVR在空白图像上保留了81%的性能，而图像-文本RLVR只显示出29%的图像敏感度。模型在68-74%的响应中产生了视觉主张，但其中38-43%是无基础的（HVRR）。这些发现表明，单纯以准确率作为奖励会助长捷径利用，而进步需要考虑基础的评估协议和明确强制视觉依赖性的训练目标。

Authors: Anas Zafar, Leema Krishna Murali, Ashish Vashist

Utonia: Toward One Encoder for All Point Clouds

简要说明：该论文介绍了Utonia，一种自监督点云转换器编码器，它在各种3D点云域上进行训练，展示了改进的迁移学习以及对具身和多模态推理任务的好处。

TLDR: The paper introduces Utonia, a self-supervised point transformer encoder trained across a diverse set of 3D point cloud domains, demonstrating improved transfer learning and benefits for embodied and multimodal reasoning tasks.

Abstract: We dream of a future where point clouds from all domains can come together to shape a single model that benefits them all. Toward this goal, we present Utonia, a first step toward training a single self-supervised point transformer encoder across diverse domains, spanning remote sensing, outdoor LiDAR, indoor RGB-D sequences, object-centric CAD models, and point clouds lifted from RGB-only videos. Despite their distinct sensing geometries, densities, and priors, Utonia learns a consistent representation space that transfers across domains. This unification improves perception capability while revealing intriguing emergent behaviors that arise only when domains are trained jointly. Beyond perception, we observe that Utonia representations can also benefit embodied and multimodal reasoning: conditioning vision-language-action policies on Utonia features improves robotic manipulation, and integrating them into vision-language models yields gains on spatial reasoning. We hope Utonia can serve as a step toward foundation models for sparse 3D data, and support downstream applications in AR/VR, robotics, and autonomous driving.

摘要（中文）：我们憧憬着一个未来，所有领域的点云数据能够汇聚在一起，形成一个对所有领域都有益的单一模型。为了实现这个目标，我们提出了Utonia，这是朝着训练一个跨越多个领域的单一自监督点变换器编码器的第一步，这些领域包括遥感、室外激光雷达、室内RGB-D序列、以物体为中心的CAD模型，以及从仅有RGB视频中提取的点云。 尽管这些领域在感知几何形状、密度和先验知识方面各不相同，但Utonia学习到一个跨领域传递的一致表示空间。这种统一性提高了感知能力，同时也揭示了只有在领域联合训练时才会出现的引人入胜的新兴行为。除了感知之外，我们观察到Utonia表示也可以使具身和多模态推理受益：以Utonia特征为条件的视觉-语言-动作策略可以改善机器人操作，将其集成到视觉-语言模型中可以提高空间推理能力。我们希望Utonia能够成为稀疏3D数据基础模型的一个进步，并支持AR/VR、机器人和自动驾驶领域的下游应用。

Authors: Yujia Zhang, Xiaoyang Wu, Yunhan Yang, Xianzhe Fan, Han Li, Yuechen Zhang, Zehao Huang, Naiyan Wang, Hengshuang Zhao

Memex(RL): Scaling Long-Horizon LLM Agents via Indexed Experience Memory

简要说明：该论文介绍了一种名为 Memex 的索引经验记忆机制，用于解决 LLM 智能体在长周期任务中上下文窗口有限的问题。通过强化学习训练来优化记忆的使用，从而在较小的工作上下文中提高任务成功率。

TLDR: The paper introduces Memex, an indexed experience memory mechanism for LLM agents on long-horizon tasks, trained with reinforcement learning to optimize memory usage under a context budget, leading to improved task success with a smaller working context.

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are fundamentally bottlenecked by finite context windows on long-horizon tasks. As trajectories grow, retaining tool outputs and intermediate reasoning in-context quickly becomes infeasible: the working context becomes prohibitively long, eventually exceeds the context budget, and makes distant evidence harder to use even when it is still present. Existing solutions typically shorten context through truncation or running summaries, but these methods are fundamentally lossy because they compress or discard past evidence itself. We introduce Memex, an indexed experience memory mechanism that instead compresses context without discarding evidence. Memex maintains a compact working context consisting of concise structured summaries and stable indices, while storing full-fidelity underlying interactions in an external experience database under those indices. The agent can then decide when to dereference an index and recover the exact past evidence needed for the current subgoal. We optimize both write and read behaviors with our reinforcement learning framework MemexRL, using reward shaping tailored to indexed memory usage under a context budget, so the agent learns what to summarize, what to archive, how to index it, and when to retrieve it. This yields a substantially less lossy form of long-horizon memory than summary-only approaches. We further provide a theoretical analysis showing the potential of the Memex loop to preserve decision quality with bounded dereferencing while keeping effective in-context computation bounded as history grows. Empirically, on challenging long-horizon tasks, Memex agent trained with MemexRL improves task success while using a significantly smaller working context.

摘要（中文）：大型语言模型 (LLM) 智能体在长时程任务中受到有限上下文窗口的根本限制。随着轨迹的增长，在上下文中保留工具输出和中间推理变得迅速不可行：工作上下文變得过长，最终超过上下文预算，并使得即使仍然存在的远端证据也更难使用。现有的解决方案通常通过截断或运行摘要来缩短上下文，但这些方法本质上是有损的，因为它们压缩或丢弃了过去的证据本身。我们引入了 Memex，一种索引式经验记忆机制，它在不丢弃证据的情况下压缩上下文。Memex 维护一个紧凑的工作上下文，该上下文由简洁的结构化摘要和稳定的索引组成，同时将完整的底层交互存储在这些索引下的外部经验数据库中。然后，智能体可以决定何时解引用一个索引，并恢复完成当前子目标所需的精确的过去证据。我们使用强化学习框架 MemexRL优化读写行为，采用针对上下文预算下索引式记忆使用的奖励塑造，从而使智能体学习总结什么、存档什么、如何索引以及何时检索它。与仅摘要方法相比，这产生了一种实质上损失更少的长时程记忆形式。我们进一步提供了一个理论分析，表明 Memex 循环具有在保持有效上下文计算量随历史增长而受限的同时，通过有界解引用来保持决策质量的潜力。在经验上，在具有挑战性的长时程任务中，使用 MemexRL 训练的 Memex 智能体在显著缩小工作上下文的同时，提高了任务成功率。

Authors: Zhenting Wang, Huancheng Chen, Jiayun Wang, Wei Wei

Gaussian Mixture-Based Inverse Perception Contract for Uncertainty-Aware Robot Navigation

简要说明：本文提出了一种基于高斯混合模型的逆感知合同（GM-IPC），用于机器人导航，与现有的单椭球方法相比，能够实现更准确、更保守的不确定性表示，从而实现更安全、更具适应性的机器人运动。

TLDR: This paper introduces a Gaussian Mixture-based Inverse Perception Contract (GM-IPC) for robot navigation, enabling more accurate and less conservative uncertainty representation compared to existing single-ellipsoidal methods, leading to safer and more adaptive robot motion.

Abstract: Reliable navigation in cluttered environments requires perception outputs that are not only accurate but also equipped with uncertainty sets suitable for safe control. An inverse perception contract (IPC) provides such a connection by mapping perceptual estimates to sets that contain the ground truth with high confidence. Existing IPC formulations, however, instantiate uncertainty as a single ellipsoidal set and rely on deterministic trust scores to guide robot motion. Such a representation cannot capture the multi-modal and irregular structure of fine-grained perception errors, often resulting in over-conservative sets and degraded navigation performance. In this work, we introduce Gaussian Mixture-based Inverse Perception Contract (GM-IPC), which extends IPC to represent uncertainty with unions of ellipsoidal confidence sets derived from Gaussian mixture models. This design moves beyond deterministic single-set abstractions, enabling fine-grained, multi-modal, and non-convex error structures to be captured with formal guarantees. A learning framework is presented that trains GM-IPC to account for probabilistic inclusion, distribution matching, and empty-space penalties, ensuring both validity and compactness of the predicted sets. We further show that the resulting uncertainty characterizations can be leveraged in downstream planning frameworks for real-time safe navigation, enabling less conservative and more adaptive robot motion while preserving safety in a probabilistic manner.

摘要（中文）：在拥挤环境中实现可靠导航需要感知输出不仅精确，而且配备适用于安全控制的不确定性集合。逆感知合约（IPC）通过将感知估计映射到以高置信度包含真实值的集合，从而提供这种连接。然而，现有的IPC公式将不确定性实例化为单个椭球集合，并依赖于确定性的信任评分来引导机器人运动。这种表示无法捕捉细粒度感知误差的多模态和不规则结构，通常导致过于保守的集合和降低的导航性能。在这项工作中，我们引入了基于高斯混合模型的逆感知合约（GM-IPC），它扩展了IPC，利用从高斯混合模型导出的椭球置信集合的并集来表示不确定性。这种设计超越了确定性的单集合抽象，使得能够以正式保证捕捉细粒度、多模态和非凸的误差结构。我们提出了一种学习框架，用于训练GM-IPC以适应概率包含、分布匹配和空空间惩罚，从而确保预测集合的有效性和紧凑性。我们进一步表明，由此产生的不确定性表征可以用于下游规划框架，以实现实时的安全导航，从而在概率上保持安全的同时，实现不那么保守且更具适应性的机器人运动。

Authors: Bingyao Du, Joonkyung Kim, Yiwei Lyu

AMP2026: A Multi-Platform Marine Robotics Dataset for Tracking and Mapping

简要说明：该论文介绍了AMP2026，一个多平台海洋机器人数据集，用于多视角跟踪和海洋环境地图构建，数据采集自空中、水面和水下机器人，包含同步数据和定位信息。

TLDR: The paper introduces AMP2026, a multi-platform marine robotics dataset for multi-view tracking and marine environment mapping, collected from aerial, surface, and submerged robots with synchronized data and localization info.

Abstract: Marine environments present significant challenges for perception and autonomy due to dynamic surfaces, limited visibility, and complex interactions between aerial, surface, and submerged sensing modalities. This paper introduces the Aerial Marine Perception Dataset (AMP2026), a multi-platform marine robotics dataset collected across multiple field deployments designed to support research in two primary areas: multi-view tracking and marine environment mapping. The dataset includes synchronized data from aerial drones, boat-mounted cameras, and submerged robotic platforms, along with associated localization and telemetry information. The goal of this work is to provide a publicly available dataset enabling research in marine perception and multi-robot observation scenarios. This paper describes the data collection methodology, sensor configurations, dataset organization, and intended research tasks supported by the dataset.

摘要（中文）：海洋环境因其动态表面、有限的能见度和空中、水面及水下传感模式之间的复杂交互，对感知和自主性提出了重大挑战。本文介绍海洋空中感知数据集（AMP2026），这是一个多平台海洋机器人数据集，通过多次实地部署收集，旨在支持两个主要领域的研究：多视角跟踪和海洋环境测绘。该数据集包括来自空中无人机、船载摄像机和水下机器人平台的同步数据，以及相关的定位和遥测信息。本工作的目标是提供一个公开可用的数据集，以促进海洋感知和多机器人观测场景的研究。本文描述了数据收集方法、传感器配置、数据集组织以及数据集所支持的预期研究任务。

Authors: Edwin Meriaux, Shuo Wen, David Widhalm, Zhizun Wang, Junming Shi, Mariana Sosa Guzmán, Kalvik Jakkala, Bennett Carley, Elias Sokolova, Yogesh Girdhar, Monika Roznere, Jason O'Kane, Junaed Sattar, Gregory Dudek

Learning Hip Exoskeleton Control Policy via Predictive Neuromusculoskeletal Simulation

简要说明：本文提出了一种基于物理的神经肌肉骨骼模拟框架，用于在模拟环境中训练髋关节外骨骼控制策略，并通过策略蒸馏将其转移到硬件上，无需大量真实数据收集即可实现性能提升。

TLDR: This paper presents a physics-based neuromusculoskeletal simulation framework for training hip exoskeleton control policies in simulation and transferring them to hardware via policy distillation, achieving performance improvements without extensive real-world data collection.

Abstract: Developing exoskeleton controllers that generalize across diverse locomotor conditions typically requires extensive motion-capture data and biomechanical labeling, limiting scalability beyond instrumented laboratory settings. Here, we present a physics-based neuromusculoskeletal learning framework that trains a hip-exoskeleton control policy entirely in simulation, without motion-capture demonstrations, and deploys it on hardware via policy distillation. A reinforcement learning teacher policy is trained using a muscle-synergy action prior over a wide range of walking speeds and slopes through a two-stage curriculum, enabling direct comparison between assisted and no-exoskeleton conditions. In simulation, exoskeleton assistance reduces mean muscle activation by up to 3.4% and mean positive joint power by up to 7.0% on level ground and ramp ascent, with benefits increasing systematically with walking speed. On hardware, the assistance profiles learned in simulation are preserved across matched speed-slope conditions (r: 0.82, RMSE: 0.03 Nm/kg), providing quantitative evidence of sim-to-real transfer without additional hardware tuning. These results demonstrate that physics-based neuromusculoskeletal simulation can serve as a practical and scalable foundation for exoskeleton controller development, substantially reducing experimental burden during the design phase.

摘要（中文）：开发能够在多种运动条件下泛化的外骨骼控制器通常需要大量的动作捕捉数据和生物力学标记，这限制了仪器化实验室之外的可扩展性。在此，我们提出一种基于物理的神经肌肉骨骼学习框架，该框架完全在仿真中训练髋部外骨骼控制策略，无需动作捕捉演示，并通过策略提炼将其部署到硬件上。通过两阶段课程，在广泛的步行速度和坡度范围内，利用肌肉协同作用先验，训练强化学习教师策略，从而可以直接比较有外骨骼辅助和无外骨骼辅助的情况。在仿真中，外骨骼辅助在平地和斜坡攀爬时，可分别减少平均肌肉激活高达3.4%和平均正向关节功率高达7.0%，并且其优势随着步行速度的增加而系统性地增加。在硬件上，仿真中学习到的辅助曲线在匹配的速度-坡度条件下得到保留（r：0.82，RMSE：0.03 Nm/kg），提供了无需额外硬件调整即可实现从仿真到现实转移的定量证据。这些结果表明，基于物理的神经肌肉骨骼仿真可以作为外骨骼控制器开发的一种实用且可扩展的基础，从而大大减少设计阶段的实验负担。

Swimming Under Constraints: A Safe Reinforcement Learning Framework for Quadrupedal Bio-Inspired Propulsion

简要说明：本文介绍了一种用于四足游泳的安全强化学习框架ACPPO-PID，该框架在最大化推力的同时最小化不稳定力，并展示了相比基线的性能提升。

TLDR: This paper introduces ACPPO-PID, a safe reinforcement learning framework for quadrupedal swimming that maximizes thrust while minimizing destabilizing forces, demonstrating improved performance compared to baselines.

Abstract: Bio-inspired aquatic propulsion offers high thrust and maneuverability but is prone to destabilizing forces such as lift fluctuations, which are further amplified by six-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) fluid coupling. We formulate quadrupedal swimming as a constrained optimization problem that maximizes forward thrust while minimizing destabilizing fluctuations. Our proposed framework, Accelerated Constrained Proximal Policy Optimization with a PID-regulated Lagrange multiplier (ACPPO-PID), enforces constraints with a PID-regulated Lagrange multiplier, accelerates learning via conditional asymmetric clipping, and stabilizes updates through cycle-wise geometric aggregation. Initialized with imitation learning and refined through on-hardware towing-tank experiments, ACPPO-PID produces control policies that transfer effectively to quadrupedal free-swimming trials. Results demonstrate improved thrust efficiency, reduced destabilizing forces, and faster convergence compared with state-of-the-art baselines, underscoring the importance of constraint-aware safe RL for robust and generalizable bio-inspired locomotion in complex fluid environments.

摘要（中文）：仿生水下推进能够提供高推力和机动性，但容易受到诸如升力波动等不稳定力的影响，这些波动会被六自由度（6-DoF）流体耦合进一步放大。我们将四足游泳建模为一个约束优化问题，旨在最大化前进推力，同时最小化不稳定波动。我们提出的框架，即基于PID调节拉格朗日乘子的加速约束近端策略优化（ACPPO-PID），通过PID调节的拉格朗日乘子实施约束，通过条件非对称裁剪加速学习，并通过周期性几何聚合稳定更新。通过模仿学习初始化并在硬件拖曳水池实验中进行精炼，ACPPO-PID生成能够有效迁移到四足自由游泳试验中的控制策略。结果表明，与最先进的基线方法相比，推力效率得到提高，不稳定力得到降低，收敛速度更快，突显了约束感知安全强化学习对于在复杂流体环境中实现鲁棒且可泛化的仿生运动的重要性。

Sim2Sea: Sim-to-Real Policy Transfer for Maritime Vessel Navigation in Congested Waters

简要说明：本文提出了Sim2Sea，一个用于自主航海船舶导航策略的sim-to-real迁移框架，其特点是并行模拟器、具有安全机制的双流时空策略和有针对性的领域随机化，成功实现了零样本迁移到真实船舶。

TLDR: This paper proposes Sim2Sea, a framework for sim-to-real transfer of autonomous maritime vessel navigation policies, featuring a parallel simulator, a dual-stream spatiotemporal policy with safety mechanisms, and targeted domain randomization, demonstrating successful zero-shot transfer to a real vessel.

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in congested maritime environments is a critical capability for a wide range of real-world applications. However, it remains an unresolved challenge due to complex vessel interactions and significant environmental uncertainties. Existing methods often fail in practical deployment due to a substantial sim-to-real gap, which stems from imprecise simulation, inadequate situational awareness, and unsafe exploration strategies. To address these, we propose \textbf{Sim2Sea}, a comprehensive framework designed to bridge simulation and real-world execution. Sim2Sea advances in three key aspects. First, we develop a GPU-accelerated parallel simulator for scalable and accurate maritime scenario simulation. Second, we design a dual-stream spatiotemporal policy that handles complex dynamics and multi-modal perception, augmented with a velocity-obstacle-guided action masking mechanism to ensure safe and efficient exploration. Finally, a targeted domain randomization scheme helps bridge the sim-to-real gap. Simulation results demonstrate that our method achieves faster convergence and safer trajectories than established baselines. In addition, our policy trained purely in simulation successfully transfers zero-shot to a 17-ton unmanned vessel operating in real-world congested waters. These results validate the effectiveness of Sim2Sea in achieving reliable sim-to-real transfer for practical autonomous maritime navigation.

摘要（中文）：在拥挤的海事环境中进行自主导航是一项关键能力，对于广泛的现实应用至关重要。然而，由于复杂的船舶交互和显著的环境不确定性，这仍然是一个未解决的挑战。现有的方法通常在实际部署中失败，原因在于存在巨大的仿真到实景差距，这源于不精确的仿真、不足的情境感知以及不安全的探索策略。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了\textbf{Sim2Sea}，这是一个旨在弥合仿真和实际执行之间差距的综合框架。Sim2Sea 在三个关键方面取得了进展。首先，我们开发了一个 GPU 加速的并行模拟器，用于可扩展且精确的海事场景仿真。其次，我们设计了一个双流时空策略，用于处理复杂的动力学和多模态感知，并辅以速度障碍引导的动作掩码机制，以确保安全高效的探索。最后，一个有针对性的领域随机化方案有助于弥合仿真到实景的差距。仿真结果表明，我们的方法比已建立的基线方法实现了更快的收敛速度和更安全的轨迹。此外，我们纯粹在仿真中训练的策略成功地零样本迁移到一艘在真实拥挤水域中运行的 17 吨无人船上。这些结果验证了 Sim2Sea 在实现可靠的仿真到实景迁移以用于实际自主海事导航方面的有效性。

Authors: Xinyu Cui, Xuanfa Jin, Xue Yan, Yongcheng Zeng, Luoyang Sun, Siying Wei, Ruizhi Zhang, Jian Zhao, Haifeng Zhang, Jun Wang

Right in Time: Reactive Reasoning in Regulated Traffic Spaces

简要说明：本文介绍了一种将概率任务设计（ProMis）和反应式电路（RC）相结合的反应式任务设计框架，用于在受监管的交通空间中进行实时、精确的概率推理，与以前的方法相比，显示出显着的加速。

TLDR: This paper introduces a reactive mission design framework combining Probabilistic Mission Design (ProMis) and Reactive Circuits (RC) for real-time, exact probabilistic inference in regulated traffic spaces, showing significant speedups over previous methods.

Abstract: Exact inference in probabilistic First-Order Logic offers a promising yet computationally costly approach for regulating the behavior of autonomous agents in shared traffic spaces. While prior methods have combined logical and probabilistic data into decision-making frameworks, their application is often limited to pre-flight checks due to the complexity of reasoning across vast numbers of possible universes. In this work, we propose a reactive mission design framework that jointly considers uncertain environmental data and declarative, logical traffic regulations. By synthesizing Probabilistic Mission Design (ProMis) with reactive reasoning facilitated by Reactive Circuits (RC), we enable online, exact probabilistic inference over hybrid domains. Our approach leverages the Frequency of Change inherent in heterogeneous data streams to subdivide inference formulas into memoized, isolated tasks, ensuring that only the specific components affected by new sensor data are re-evaluated. In experiments involving both real-world vessel data and simulated drone traffic in dense urban scenarios, we demonstrate that our approach provides orders of magnitude in speedup over ProMis without reactive paradigms. This allows intelligent transportation systems, such as Unmanned Aircraft Systems (UAS), to actively assert safety and legal compliance during operations rather than relying solely on preparation procedures.

摘要（中文）：在概率一阶逻辑中进行精确推理为规范共享交通空间中自主代理的行为提供了一种有前景但计算成本高昂的方法。虽然先前的方法已将逻辑和概率数据结合到决策框架中，但由于跨越大量可能世界的推理复杂性，它们的应用通常仅限于飞行前检查。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种反应式任务设计框架，该框架共同考虑了不确定的环境数据和声明式的逻辑交通规则。通过将概率任务设计（ProMis）与由反应式电路（RC）促进的反应式推理相结合，我们实现了混合领域中的在线、精确概率推理。我们的方法利用异构数据流中固有的变化频率将推理公式细分为可记忆的、隔离的任务，从而确保仅重新评估受新传感器数据影响的特定组件。在涉及真实世界船舶数据和密集城市场景中的模拟无人机交通的实验中，我们证明了我们的方法比没有反应式范式的ProMis提供了数量级的加速。这使得智能交通系统，例如无人驾驶飞行器系统（UAS），能够在运营期间主动维护安全和法律合规性，而不是仅仅依赖于准备程序。

Whole-Body Safe Control of Robotic Systems with Koopman Neural Dynamics

简要说明：本文提出了一种基于Koopman算子学习和安全集算法(SSA)的数据驱动框架，用于安全机器人控制，并在机械臂和四足机器人上展示了精确的跟踪和避障。

TLDR: This paper presents a data-driven framework for safe robot control using Koopman operator learning and the Safe Set Algorithm (SSA), demonstrating accurate tracking and obstacle avoidance on a manipulator and a quadruped.

Abstract: Controlling robots with strongly nonlinear, high-dimensional dynamics remains challenging, as direct nonlinear optimization with safety constraints is often intractable in real time. The Koopman operator offers a way to represent nonlinear systems linearly in a lifted space, enabling the use of efficient linear control. We propose a data-driven framework that learns a Koopman embedding and operator from data, and integrates the resulting linear model with the Safe Set Algorithm (SSA). This allows the tracking and safety constraints to be solved in a single quadratic program (QP), ensuring feasibility and optimality without a separate safety filter. We validate the method on a Kinova Gen3 manipulator and a Go2 quadruped, showing accurate tracking and obstacle avoidance.

摘要（中文）：使用强非线性、高维动力学的机器人控制仍然具有挑战性，因为带有安全约束的直接非线性优化通常难以实时实现。Koopman算子提供了一种在提升空间中线性表示非线性系统的方法，从而能够使用高效的线性控制。我们提出了一种数据驱动的框架，该框架从数据中学习Koopman嵌入和算子，并将生成的线性模型与安全集算法（SSA）集成。这使得跟踪和安全约束可以在单个二次规划（QP）中求解，从而确保可行性和最优性，而无需单独的安全滤波器。我们在Kinova Gen3机械臂和一个Go2四足机器人上验证了该方法，结果表明其具有精确的跟踪和避障能力。

UrbanHuRo: A Two-Layer Human-Robot Collaboration Framework for the Joint Optimization of Heterogeneous Urban Services

简要说明：该论文介绍了UrbanHuRo，一个用于人机协作的双层框架，旨在使用基于MapReduce的K-次模最大化模块和深度次模奖励强化学习算法，联合优化异构的城市服务（众包交付和城市感知），并展示了在感知覆盖范围和快递员收入方面的显著改进。

TLDR: The paper introduces UrbanHuRo, a two-layer framework for human-robot collaboration to jointly optimize heterogeneous urban services (crowdsourced delivery and urban sensing) using a MapReduce-based K-submodular maximization module and a deep submodular reward reinforcement learning algorithm, demonstrating significant improvements in sensing coverage and courier income.

Abstract: In the vision of smart cities, technologies are being developed to enhance the efficiency of urban services and improve residents' quality of life. However, most existing research focuses on optimizing individual services in isolation, without adequately considering reciprocal interactions among heterogeneous urban services that could yield higher efficiency and improved resource utilization. For example, human couriers could collect traffic and air quality data along their delivery routes, while sensing robots could assist with on-demand delivery during peak hours, enhancing both sensing coverage and delivery efficiency. However, the joint optimization of different urban services is challenging due to potentially conflicting objectives and the need for real-time coordination in dynamic environments. In this paper, we propose UrbanHuRo, a two-layer human-robot collaboration framework for joint optimization of heterogeneous urban services, demonstrated through crowdsourced delivery and urban sensing. UrbanHuRo includes two key designs: (i) a scalable distributed MapReduce-based K-submodular maximization module for efficient order dispatch, and (ii) a deep submodular reward reinforcement learning algorithm for sensing route planning. Experimental evaluations on real-world datasets from a food delivery platform demonstrate that UrbanHuRo improves sensing coverage by 29.7% and courier income by 39.2% on average in most settings, while also significantly reducing the number of overdue orders.

摘要（中文）：在智慧城市的愿景下，各种技术不断涌现，旨在提升城市服务的效率并改善居民的生活质量。然而，现有研究大多侧重于孤立地优化单个服务，而未充分考虑异构城市服务之间可能产生更高效率和更优资源利用的互惠互动。例如，人工快递员可以在其配送路线上收集交通和空气质量数据，而传感机器人可以在高峰时段协助按需配送，从而提高传感覆盖率和配送效率。然而，由于潜在的目标冲突以及在动态环境中对实时协调的需求，不同城市服务的联合优化极具挑战。本文提出了一种名为UrbanHuRo的两层人机协作框架，用于异构城市服务的联合优化，并通过众包配送和城市传感进行了演示。UrbanHuRo包括两个关键设计：（i）一个可扩展的基于MapReduce的分布式K-次模最大化模块，用于高效的订单调度，以及（ii）一个深度次模奖励强化学习算法，用于感知路线规划。在食品配送平台提供的真实世界数据集上进行的实验评估表明，在大多数情况下，UrbanHuRo平均将传感覆盖率提高了29.7%，快递员收入提高了39.2%，同时也显著减少了逾期订单的数量。

Authors: Tonmoy Dey, Lin Jiang, Zheng Dong, Guang Wang

Efficient Autonomous Navigation of a Quadruped Robot in Underground Mines on Edge Hardware

简要说明：本文提出了一套用于 Boston Dynamics Spot 机器狗的全自主导航系统，该系统在地下矿井中运行，无需 GPU 或网络连接，在一次环境映射后，在真实环境中 20 次试验中实现了 100% 的成功率。

TLDR: This paper presents a fully autonomous navigation system for a Boston Dynamics Spot in underground mines, running on edge hardware without GPUs or network connectivity, achieving 100% success across 20 trials in a real-world environment after a single mapping pass.

Abstract: Embodied navigation in underground mines faces significant challenges, including narrow passages, uneven terrain, near-total darkness, GPS-denied conditions, and limited communication infrastructure. While recent learning-based approaches rely on GPU-accelerated inference and extensive training data, we present a fully autonomous navigation stack for a Boston Dynamics Spot quadruped robot that runs entirely on a low-power Intel NUC edge computer with no GPU and no network connectivity requirements. The system integrates LiDAR-inertial odometry, scan-matching localization against a prior map, terrain segmentation, and visibility-graph global planning with a velocity-regulated local path follower, achieving real-time perception-to-action at consistent control rates. After a single mapping pass of the environment, the system handles arbitrary goal locations within the known map without any environment-specific training or learned components. We validate the system through repeated field trials using four target locations of varying traversal difficulty in an experimental underground mine, accumulating over 700 m of fully autonomous traverse with a 100% success rate across all 20 trials (5 repetitions x 4 targets) and an overall Success weighted by Path Length (SPL) of 0.73 \pm 0.09.

摘要（中文）：地下矿井中的具身导航面临着严峻挑战，包括狭窄的通道、不平坦的地形、近乎完全的黑暗、GPS受限的环境以及有限的通信基础设施。尽管最近基于学习的方法依赖于GPU加速推理和大量训练数据，但我们提出了一种适用于Boston Dynamics Spot四足机器人的完全自主导航堆栈，该堆栈完全运行在低功耗的Intel NUC边缘计算设备上，无需GPU和网络连接。该系统集成了激光雷达惯性里程计、基于先验地图的扫描匹配定位、地形分割，以及基于可见性图的全局规划，并结合了速度调节的局部路径跟踪器，以一致的控制速率实现实时的感知到行动。经过对环境的单次映射后，该系统能够处理已知地图中的任意目标位置，而无需任何特定于环境的训练或学习组件。我们通过在实验矿井中使用四个不同遍历难度的目标位置进行重复的现场试验来验证该系统，在20次试验（5次重复 x 4个目标）中积累了超过700米的完全自主遍历，成功率达100%，并且总体成功率按路径长度加权 (SPL) 为 0.73 \pm 0.09。

Authors: Yixiang Gao, Kwame Awuah-Offei

Sampling-Based Motion Planning with Scene Graphs Under Perception Constraints

简要说明：本文介绍了一种名为MOPS-PRM的基于采样的运动规划器，该规划器使用场景图表示，将感知约束（多目标监控）融入到高自由度机器人的运动规划中，与基线方法相比，在物体检测和跟踪方面有所改进。

TLDR: The paper introduces MOPS-PRM, a sampling-based motion planner for high-DoF robots that incorporates perception constraints (multi-object monitoring) using a scene graph representation, demonstrating improved object detection and tracking compared to baselines.

Abstract: It will be increasingly common for robots to operate in cluttered human-centered environments such as homes, workplaces, and hospitals, where the robot is often tasked to maintain perception constraints, such as monitoring people or multiple objects, for safety and reliability while executing its task. However, existing perception-aware approaches typically focus on low-degree-of-freedom (DoF) systems or only consider a single object in the context of high-DoF robots. This motivates us to consider the problem of perception-aware motion planning for high-DoF robots that accounts for multi-object monitoring constraints. We employ a scene graph representation of the environment, offering a great potential for incorporating long-horizon task and motion planning thanks to its rich semantic and spatial information. However, it does not capture perception-constrained information, such as the viewpoints the user prefers. To address these challenges, we propose MOPS-PRM, a roadmap-based motion planner, that integrates the perception cost of observing multiple objects or humans directly into motion planning for high-DoF robots. The perception cost is embedded to each object as part of a scene graph, and used to selectively sample configurations for roadmap construction, implicitly enforcing the perception constraints. Our method is extensively validated in both simulated and real-world experiments, achieving more than ~36% improvement in the average number of detected objects and ~17% better track rate against other perception-constrained baselines, with comparable planning times and path lengths.

摘要（中文）：随着机器人在拥挤的以人为中心的环境（如家庭、工作场所和医院）中运行越来越普遍，机器人通常需要维持感知约束，例如为了安全性和可靠性而监控人员或多个物体，同时执行其任务。然而，现有的感知感知方法通常侧重于低自由度（DoF）系统，或者在高自由度机器人的背景下仅考虑单个物体。 这促使我们考虑针对高自由度机器人的感知感知运动规划问题，该问题考虑了多物体监控约束。 我们采用环境的场景图表示，由于其丰富的语义和空间信息，为整合长程任务和运动规划提供了巨大的潜力。 然而，它并未捕获感知约束信息，例如用户偏好的视点。 为了应对这些挑战，我们提出了MOPS-PRM，一种基于路标的运动规划器，它将观察多个物体或人员的感知代价直接整合到高自由度机器人的运动规划中。 感知代价作为场景图的一部分嵌入到每个物体中，并用于选择性地采样配置以构建路标图，从而隐式地执行感知约束。 我们的方法在模拟和真实世界的实验中都得到了广泛的验证，与其他感知约束基线相比，在平均检测到的物体数量方面提高了超过约36％，并且跟踪率提高了约17％，同时规划时间和路径长度相当。
