Authors: Qingxi Meng, Emiliano Flores, Thai Duong, Vaibhav Unhelkar, Lydia E. Kavraki

Overlapping Domain Decomposition for Distributed Pose Graph Optimization

简要说明：该论文介绍了一种名为 ROBO 的分布式姿态图优化方法，用于多机器人系统。该方法使用了重叠域分解，与现有的分布式方法相比，提高了收敛速度，并且对网络延迟具有鲁棒性。

TLDR: The paper introduces ROBO, a distributed pose graph optimization method for multi-robot systems that uses overlapping domain decomposition to improve convergence speed compared to existing distributed approaches while being robust to network delays.

Abstract: We present ROBO (Riemannian Overlapping Block Optimization), a distributed and parallel approach to multi-robot pose graph optimization (PGO) based on the idea of overlapping domain decomposition. ROBO offers a middle ground between centralized and fully distributed solvers, where the amount of pose information shared between robots at each optimization iteration can be set according to the available communication resources. Sharing additional pose information between neighboring robots effectively creates overlapping optimization blocks in the underlying pose graph, which substantially reduces the number of iterations required to converge. Through extensive experiments on benchmark PGO datasets, we demonstrate the applicability and feasibility of ROBO in different initialization scenarios, using various cost functions, and under different communication regimes. We also analyze the tradeoff between the increased communication and local computation required by ROBO's overlapping blocks and the resulting faster convergence. We show that overlaps with an average inter-robot data cost of only 36 Kb per iteration can converge 3.1$\times$ faster in terms of iterations than state-of-the-art distributed PGO approaches. Furthermore, we develop an asynchronous variant of ROBO that is robust to network delays and suitable for real-world robotic applications.

摘要（中文）：我们提出了ROBO（黎曼重叠块优化），一种基于重叠域分解思想的多机器人位姿图优化（PGO）分布式并行方法。ROBO介于集中式和完全分布式求解器之间，允许根据可用的通信资源设置每次优化迭代中机器人之间共享的位姿信息量。在相邻机器人之间共享额外的位姿信息有效地在底层位姿图中创建重叠的优化块，从而大大减少了收敛所需的迭代次数。通过对基准PGO数据集的大量实验，我们证明了ROBO在不同初始化场景、使用各种代价函数以及不同通信机制下的适用性和可行性。我们还分析了ROBO的重叠块所需的增加的通信和本地计算量与由此产生的更快收敛速度之间的权衡。我们表明，平均每次迭代机器人间数据代价仅为36 Kb的重叠相比最先进的分布式PGO方法，迭代收敛速度可以提高3.1倍。此外，我们还开发了ROBO的异步变体，该变体对网络延迟具有鲁棒性，适用于现实世界的机器人应用。

Authors: Aneesa Sonawalla, Yulun Tian, Jonathan P. How

Navigating in Uncertain Environments with Heterogeneous Visibility

简要说明：本文提出了一种启发式算法，用于在具有异构可见性的不确定环境中进行高效导航，从而平衡了路径成本和观察远处边缘的信息增益。 在成本和计算时间方面，它优于最短路径基线和现有方法。

TLDR: This paper introduces a heuristic algorithm for efficient navigation in uncertain environments with heterogeneous visibility, balancing path cost with information gain from observing distant edges. It outperforms shortest-path baselines and existing methods in terms of cost and computation time.

Abstract: Navigating an environment with uncertain connectivity requires a strategic balance between minimizing the cost of traversal and seeking information to resolve map ambiguities. Unlike previous approaches that rely on local sensing, we utilize a framework where nodes possess varying visibility levels, allowing for observation of distant edges from certain vantage points. We propose a novel heuristic algorithm that balances the cost of detouring to high-visibility locations against the gain in information by optimizing the sum of a custom observation reward and the cost of traversal. We introduce a technique to sample the shortest path on numerous realizations of the environment, which we use to define an edge's utility for observation and to quickly estimate the path with the highest reward. Our approach can be easily adapted to a variety of scenarios by tuning a single hyperparameter that determines the importance of observation. We test our method on a variety of uncertain navigation tasks, including a map based on real-world topographical data. The method demonstrates lower mean cost of traversal compared to a shortest path baseline that does not consider observation and has exponentially lower computational overhead compared to an existing method for balancing observation with path cost minimization.

摘要（中文）：在不确定连通性的环境中导航需要在最小化遍历成本和寻找信息以消除地图歧义之间进行策略性平衡。与以往依赖局部感知的方法不同，我们采用一个框架，其中节点具有不同的能见度级别，从而可以在某些有利位置观察到远距离的边。我们提出了一种新颖的启发式算法，通过优化自定义观察奖励和遍历成本的总和，来平衡绕道至高能见度位置的成本和信息增益。我们引入了一种技术，用于对环境的多次实现采样最短路径，利用它来定义边用于观察的效用，并快速估计具有最高奖励的路径。通过调整一个决定观察重要性的超参数，我们的方法可以轻松地适应各种场景。我们在各种不确定的导航任务中测试了我们的方法，包括基于真实地形数据的地图。与不考虑观察的最短路径基线相比，该方法表现出更低的平均遍历成本，并且与现有用于平衡观察和路径成本最小化的方法相比，具有指数级降低的计算开销。

Authors: Jongann Lee, Melkior Ornik

BeamPERL: Parameter-Efficient RL with Verifiable Rewards Specializes Compact LLMs for Structured Beam Mechanics Reasoning

简要说明：该论文探索了使用参数高效的强化学习和可验证的物理奖励来训练一个紧凑的LLM进行梁力学推理，发现模型学习的是解决方案模板而不是内化物理规律，突出了结果层面alignment的局限性。

TLDR: The paper explores using parameter-efficient reinforcement learning with verifiable physics rewards to train a compact LLM on beam mechanics reasoning, finding that the model learns solution templates rather than internalizing physical laws, highlighting limitations of outcome-level alignment.

Abstract: Can reinforcement learning with hard, verifiable rewards teach a compact language model to reason about physics, or does it primarily learn to pattern-match toward correct answers? We study this question by training a 1.5B-parameter reasoning model on beam statics, a classic engineering problem, using parameter-efficient RLVR with binary correctness rewards from symbolic solvers, without teacher-generated reasoning traces. The best BeamPERL checkpoint achieves a 66.7% improvement in Pass@1 over the base model. However, the learned competence is anisotropic: the model generalizes compositionally (more loads) but fails under topological shifts (moved supports) that require the same equilibrium equations. Intermediate checkpoints yield the strongest reasoning, while continued optimization degrades robustness while maintaining reward. These findings reveal a key limitation of outcome-level alignment: reinforcement learning with exact physics rewards induces procedural solution templates rather than internalization of governing equations. The precision of the reward signal - even when analytically exact - does not by itself guarantee transferable physical reasoning. Our results suggest that verifiable rewards may need to be paired with structured reasoning scaffolding to move beyond template matching toward robust scientific reasoning.

摘要（中文）：能否利用具有硬性可验证奖励的强化学习训练一个紧凑的语言模型来推理物理学，或者它主要学习模式匹配以获得正确的答案？我们通过在梁静力学（一个典型的工程问题）上训练一个15亿参数的推理模型来研究这个问题，我们使用了参数高效的RLVR，其奖励来自符号求解器的二元正确性判断，而没有教师生成的推理轨迹。最好的BeamPERL检查点在Pass@1指标上获得了比基础模型高66.7%的提升。然而，所学的能力是各向异性的：该模型在组合性方面泛化良好（更多的载荷），但在需要相同平衡方程的拓扑变换（移动的支撑）下表现不佳。中间检查点产生最强的推理能力，而持续优化则会降低鲁棒性，同时保持奖励。这些发现揭示了结果层面对齐的一个关键限制：具有精确物理奖励的强化学习会诱导过程性的解题模板，而不是对支配方程的内化。奖励信号的精确性——即使在分析上是精确的——本身并不能保证可转移的物理推理。我们的结果表明，可验证的奖励可能需要与结构化的推理支架相结合，才能超越模板匹配，实现稳健的科学推理。

Authors: Tarjei Paule Hage, Markus J. Buehler

GIPO: Gaussian Importance Sampling Policy Optimization

简要说明：该论文介绍了GIPO，一种基于截断重要性采样和高斯信任权重的强化学习策略优化方法。实验表明，与基于裁剪的方法相比，GIPO 具有更高的采样效率和稳定性，尤其是在处理过期数据时。

TLDR: The paper introduces GIPO, a reinforcement learning policy optimization method based on truncated importance sampling with Gaussian trust weights, demonstrating improved sample efficiency and stability compared to clipping-based methods, especially with stale data.

Abstract: Post-training with reinforcement learning (RL) has recently shown strong promise for advancing multimodal agents beyond supervised imitation. However, RL remains limited by poor data efficiency, particularly in settings where interaction data are scarce and quickly become outdated. To address this challenge, GIPO (Gaussian Importance sampling Policy Optimization) is proposed as a policy optimization objective based on truncated importance sampling, replacing hard clipping with a log-ratio-based Gaussian trust weight to softly damp extreme importance ratios while maintaining non-zero gradients. Theoretical analysis shows that GIPO introduces an implicit, tunable constraint on the update magnitude, while concentration bounds guarantee robustness and stability under finite-sample estimation. Experimental results show that GIPO achieves state-of-the-art performance among clipping-based baselines across a wide range of replay buffer sizes, from near on-policy to highly stale data, while exhibiting superior bias--variance trade-off, high training stability and improved sample efficiency.

摘要（中文）：利用强化学习进行的后训练最近在推进多模态智能体超越监督模仿方面展现出巨大潜力。然而，强化学习仍然受限于数据效率低下，尤其是在交互数据稀缺且迅速过时的环境中。为解决这一挑战，我们提出 GIPO（基于高斯重要性采样策略优化），一种基于截断重要性采样的策略优化目标。GIPO 使用基于对数比的高斯置信权重来代替硬裁剪，从而柔性地抑制极端重要性比率，同时保持非零梯度。理论分析表明，GIPO 引入了一个隐式的、可调节的更新幅度约束，且集中度界限保证了在有限样本估计下的鲁棒性和稳定性。实验结果表明，在从近乎同策略到高度陈旧数据的各种回放缓冲区大小下，GIPO 在基于裁剪的基线方法中实现了最先进的性能，同时展现出卓越的偏差-方差权衡、高训练稳定性和改进的样本效率。

Authors: Chengxuan Lu, Zhenquan Zhang, Shukuan Wang, Qunzhi Lin, Baigui Sun, Yang Liu

A Rubric-Supervised Critic from Sparse Real-World Outcomes

简要说明：该论文提出了一种训练评论模型的方法，用于在真实环境中操作且奖励稀疏的编码代理，该方法使用从人机交互轨迹中导出的基于规则的监督。 该评论模型随后提高了 SWE-bench 上的性能，并支持数据管理。

TLDR: This paper introduces a method to train a critic model for coding agents operating in real-world settings with sparse rewards, using rubric-based supervision derived from human-agent interaction traces. The critic model then improves performance on SWE-bench and enables data curation.

Abstract: Academic benchmarks for coding agents tend to reward autonomous task completion, measured by verifiable rewards such as unit-test success. In contrast, real-world coding agents operate with humans in the loop, where success signals are typically noisy, delayed, and sparse. How can we bridge this gap? In this paper, we propose a process to learn a "critic" model from sparse and noisy interaction data, which can then be used both as a reward model for either RL-based training or inference-time scaling. Specifically, we introduce Critic Rubrics, a rubric-based supervision framework with 24 behavioral features that can be derived from human-agent interaction traces alone. Using a semi-supervised objective, we can then jointly predict these rubrics and sparse human feedback (when present). In experiments, we demonstrate that, despite being trained primarily from trace-observable rubrics and sparse real-world outcome proxies, these critics improve best-of-N reranking on SWE-bench (Best@8 +15.9 over Random@8 over the rerankable subset of trajectories), enable early stopping (+17.7 with 83% fewer attempts), and support training-time data curation via critic-selected trajectories.

摘要（中文）：用于编码代理的学术基准往往奖励自主任务完成，并以可验证的奖励来衡量，例如单元测试成功。然而，现实世界的编码代理通常与人类协同工作，其成功信号通常是嘈杂的、延迟的和稀疏的。我们如何弥合这个差距？在本文中，我们提出了一种从稀疏和嘈杂的交互数据中学习“评论家”模型的过程，该模型既可以用作基于强化学习的训练的奖励模型，也可以用于推理时的缩放。具体来说，我们引入了“评论家准则”（Critic Rubrics），这是一个基于准则的监督框架，包含可以仅从人机交互轨迹推导出的24个行为特征。使用半监督目标，我们可以联合预测这些准则和稀疏的人类反馈（如果存在）。在实验中，我们证明了，尽管主要基于轨迹可观察的准则和稀疏的现实世界结果代理进行训练，这些评论家仍然可以提高SWE-bench上的N选优重排序（在可重排序的轨迹子集上，Best@8比Random@8高+15.9），实现提前停止（+17.7，尝试次数减少83%），并通过评论家选择的轨迹来支持训练时的数据管理。

Authors: Xingyao Wang, Valerie Chen, Heng Ji, Graham Neubig

Learning Approximate Nash Equilibria in Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning via Mean-Field Subsampling

简要说明：本文提出了一种通信受限的合作式多智能体强化学习的交替学习框架，证明了其收敛到近似纳什均衡，并具有降低样本复杂度的优势。该方法通过多机器人控制和联邦优化模拟进行了验证。

TLDR: This paper introduces an alternating learning framework for cooperative MARL with communication constraints, proving convergence to an approximate Nash Equilibrium with sample complexity benefits. The approach is validated with multi-robot control and federated optimization simulations.

Abstract: Many large-scale platforms and networked control systems have a centralized decision maker interacting with a massive population of agents under strict observability constraints. Motivated by such applications, we study a cooperative Markov game with a global agent and $n$ homogeneous local agents in a communication-constrained regime, where the global agent only observes a subset of $k$ local agent states per time step. We propose an alternating learning framework $(\texttt{ALTERNATING-MARL})$, where the global agent performs subsampled mean-field $Q$-learning against a fixed local policy, and local agents update by optimizing in an induced MDP. We prove that these approximate best-response dynamics converge to an $\widetilde{O}(1/\sqrt{k})$-approximate Nash Equilibrium, while yielding a separation in the sample complexities between the joint state space and action space. Finally, we validate our results in numerical simulations for multi-robot control and federated optimization.

摘要（中文）：许多大规模平台和网络化控制系统都有一个集中式决策者与大量智能体在严格的可观测性约束下进行交互。受此类应用的启发，我们研究了一个合作马尔可夫博弈，其中包含一个全局智能体和$n$个同质局部智能体，且面临通信受限的环境，即全局智能体在每个时间步仅观察到$k$个局部智能体的状态子集。我们提出了一种交替学习框架$(\texttt{ALTERNATING-MARL})$，其中全局智能体针对固定的局部策略执行子采样的均场$Q$-学习，局部智能体通过在诱导的MDP中进行优化来更新。我们证明了这些近似最佳响应动态收敛到一个$\widetilde{O}(1/\sqrt{k})$-近似纳什均衡，同时在联合状态空间和动作空间的样本复杂度上产生了分离。最后，我们在多机器人控制和联邦优化的数值模拟中验证了我们的结果。

Authors: Emile Anand, Ishani Karmarkar

Agentic Peer-to-Peer Networks: From Content Distribution to Capability and Action Sharing

简要说明：该论文提出了一种用于协作的客户端自主代理（CSAAs）点对点网络架构，重点关注能力和行动共享、发现机制以及分层验证，以解决对抗环境中出现的安全问题。

TLDR: This paper proposes a networking architecture for collaborative Client-Side Autonomous Agents (CSAAs) in a peer-to-peer network, focusing on capability and action sharing, discovery mechanisms, and tiered verification to address security concerns in adversarial environments.

Abstract: The ongoing shift of AI models from centralized cloud APIs to local AI agents on edge devices is enabling \textit{Client-Side Autonomous Agents (CSAAs)} -- persistent personal agents that can plan, access local context, and invoke tools on behalf of users. As these agents begin to collaborate by delegating subtasks directly between clients, they naturally form \emph{Agentic Peer-to-Peer (P2P) Networks}. Unlike classic file-sharing overlays where the exchanged object is static, hash-indexed content (e.g., files in BitTorrent), agentic overlays exchange \emph{capabilities and actions} that are heterogeneous, state-dependent, and potentially unsafe if delegated to untrusted peers. This article outlines the networking foundations needed to make such collaboration practical. We propose a plane-based reference architecture that decouples connectivity/identity, semantic discovery, and execution. Besides, we introduce signed, soft-state capability descriptors to support intent- and constraint-aware discovery. To cope with adversarial settings, we further present a \textit{tiered verification} spectrum: Tier~1 relies on reputation signals, Tier~2 applies lightweight canary challenge-response with fallback selection, and Tier~3 requires evidence packages such as signed tool receipts/traces (and, when applicable, attestation). Using a discrete-event simulator that models registry-based discovery, Sybil-style index poisoning, and capability drift, we show that tiered verification substantially improves end-to-end workflow success while keeping discovery latency near-constant and control-plane overhead modest.

摘要（中文）：摘要： 人工智能模型正从集中式云API向边缘设备上的本地AI代理转变，这孕育了\textit{客户端自主代理(CSAAs)} —— 能够代表用户进行规划、访问本地上下文并调用工具的持久化个人代理。当这些代理开始通过客户端间直接委派子任务进行协作时，它们自然形成\emph{代理式点对点(P2P)网络}。与交换静态、哈希索引内容(例如BitTorrent中的文件)的经典文件共享覆盖网络不同，代理式覆盖网络交换的是异构的、状态相关的，且如果委派给不受信任的对等方可能不安全的\emph{能力和行动}。本文概述了使此类协作成为现实所需的网络基础。我们提出了一个基于平面的参考架构，该架构将连接/身份、语义发现和执行解耦。此外，我们引入了签名的、软状态能力描述符，以支持意图感知和约束感知的发现。为了应对对抗性环境，我们进一步提出了一种\textit{分层验证}方案：第一层依赖于声誉信号，第二层应用轻量级金丝雀挑战-响应与回退选择，第三层需要证据包，例如签名的工具收据/溯源轨迹(以及在适用的情况下，证明)。我们使用一个对基于注册表的发现、女巫攻击式索引投毒和能力漂移进行建模的离散事件模拟器，表明分层验证在保持发现延迟接近恒定和控制平面开销适中的同时，显著提高了端到端工作流程的成功率。

Authors: Taotao Wang, Lizhao You, Jingwen Tong, Chonghe Zhao, Shengli Zhang

MIND: Unified Inquiry and Diagnosis RL with Criteria Grounded Clinical Supports for Psychiatric Consultation

简要说明：该论文提出了MIND，一个用于精神病咨询的强化学习框架，通过结合基于标准的临床支持和基于规则的过程奖励，解决了多轮互动中遇到的挑战，如无根据的断言和询问漂移。

TLDR: The paper presents MIND, a reinforcement learning framework for psychiatric consultation that addresses challenges in multi-turn interactions, unsupported assertions, and inquiry drift by incorporating criteria-grounded clinical supports and rubric-based process rewards.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have advanced medical dialogue systems, yet psychiatric consultation poses substantially higher demands due to subjective ambiguity and comorbidity complexity: an agent must continuously extract psychopathological cues from incomplete and inconsistent patient reports in multi-turn interactions and perform rigorous differential diagnostic reasoning. However, existing methods face two fundamental challenges. First, without criteria-grounded clinical supports, they are prone to unsupported clinical assertions when symptoms are atypical or underspecified. Second, in multi-turn interactions, they struggle to mitigate inquiry drift (off-topic or low-yield questioning) and optimize questioning strategies. To address these challenges, we propose MIND, a unified inquiry--diagnosis reinforcement learning framework for psychiatric consultation. Specifically, we build a Criteria-Grounded Psychiatric Reasoning Bank (PRB) that summarizes dialogue context into clinical retrieval states, retrieves semantically similar reference consultations, and distills reusable criteria-grounded clinical supports to guide criteria-aligned inquiry and reasoning. Building on this foundation, MIND enforces explicit clinical reasoning with rubric-based process rewards to provide fine-grained supervision over intermediate decision steps, and incorporates a value-aware trajectory rectification mechanism to jointly improve information acquisition and diagnostic decision-making across turns. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MIND consistently outperforms strong baselines in diagnostic accuracy, empathetic interaction quality, interpretability, and generalization.

摘要（中文）：大型语言模型（LLMs）推动了医学对话系统的发展，但精神科咨询由于主观模糊性和共病复杂性，提出了更高的要求：智能体必须在多轮互动中，从不完整且不一致的患者报告中持续提取精神病理学线索，并进行严谨的鉴别诊断推理。然而，现有方法面临两个根本性挑战。首先，在缺乏基于标准的临床支持的情况下，当症状非典型或未充分明确时，它们容易做出缺乏支持的临床断言。其次，在多轮互动中，它们难以缓解询问漂移（偏离主题或低效提问）并优化提问策略。为了应对这些挑战，我们提出了MIND，一个用于精神科咨询的统一询问-诊断强化学习框架。具体而言，我们构建了一个基于标准的精神科推理库(PRB)，该库将对话上下文总结为临床检索状态，检索语义上相似的参考咨询，并提炼可重复使用的、基于标准的临床支持，以指导符合标准的询问和推理。在此基础上，MIND利用基于评价标准的流程奖励来强化明确的临床推理，从而为中间决策步骤提供细粒度的监督，并结合了一种价值感知的轨迹修正机制，以共同改进多轮互动中的信息获取和诊断决策。广泛的实验表明，MIND在诊断准确性、共情互动质量、可解释性和泛化能力方面始终优于强大的基线模型。

NeuroSkill(tm): Proactive Real-Time Agentic System Capable of Modeling Human State of Mind

简要说明：该论文介绍NeuroSkill(tm)，一个实时的、主动的智能体系统，它通过从脑机接口设备直接使用基础EXG模型和文本嵌入来建模人类的精神状态，从而能够在认知和情感层面进行互动。该系统据称是开源的并且符合伦理。

TLDR: The paper introduces NeuroSkill(tm), a real-time proactive agentic system modeling human state of mind directly from BCI devices using a foundation EXG model and text embeddings, enabling engagement on cognitive and affective levels. The system is purportedly open-source and ethically aligned.

Abstract: Real-time proactive agentic system, capable of modeling Human State of Mind, using foundation EXG model and text embeddings model, running fully offline on the edge. Unlike all previously known systems, the NeuroSkill(tm) system leverages SKILL.md description of Human's State of Mind via API and CLI provided by the system, directly from the Brain-Computer Interface (BCI) devices, which records Human biophysical and brain signals. Our custom harness - NeuroLoop(tm) - utilizes all of the above to run agentic flow that manages to engage with the Human on multiple cognitive and affective levels of their State of Mind (e.g., empathy), by providing actionable tool calls and protocol execution with explicit or implicit requests from the Human. GPLv3 open-source software with ethically aligned AI100 licensing for the skill markdown.

摘要（中文）：实时主动代理系统，能够建模人类心智状态，采用基础EXG模型和文本嵌入模型，完全离线运行在边缘设备上。与所有已知的系统不同，NeuroSkill(tm)系统利用SKILL.md描述的人类心智状态，通过系统提供的API和CLI，直接从脑机接口(BCI)设备获取，该设备记录人类的生物物理和脑部信号。我们定制的框架——NeuroLoop(tm)——利用以上所有技术运行代理流程，通过提供可操作的工具调用和协议执行，在人类心智状态的多个认知和情感层面（例如，共情）与人类互动，其中包含来自人类的显式或隐式请求。该技能Markdown采用GPLv3开源软件，并遵循伦理对齐的AI100许可。

Authors: Nataliya Kosmyna, Eugene Hauptmann

ArtHOI: Articulated Human-Object Interaction Synthesis by 4D Reconstruction from Video Priors

简要说明：该论文介绍了ArtHOI，一种新的零样本框架，通过从单目视频先验中重建4D场景来合成铰接式人-物交互，与现有方法相比，实现了更高的接触精度和铰接逼真度。

TLDR: This paper introduces ArtHOI, a novel zero-shot framework for synthesizing articulated human-object interactions by reconstructing 4D scenes from monocular video priors, achieving improved contact accuracy and articulation fidelity compared to existing methods.

Abstract: Synthesizing physically plausible articulated human-object interactions (HOI) without 3D/4D supervision remains a fundamental challenge. While recent zero-shot approaches leverage video diffusion models to synthesize human-object interactions, they are largely confined to rigid-object manipulation and lack explicit 4D geometric reasoning. To bridge this gap, we formulate articulated HOI synthesis as a 4D reconstruction problem from monocular video priors: given only a video generated by a diffusion model, we reconstruct a full 4D articulated scene without any 3D supervision. This reconstruction-based approach treats the generated 2D video as supervision for an inverse rendering problem, recovering geometrically consistent and physically plausible 4D scenes that naturally respect contact, articulation, and temporal coherence. We introduce ArtHOI, the first zero-shot framework for articulated human-object interaction synthesis via 4D reconstruction from video priors. Our key designs are: 1) Flow-based part segmentation: leveraging optical flow as a geometric cue to disentangle dynamic from static regions in monocular video; 2) Decoupled reconstruction pipeline: joint optimization of human motion and object articulation is unstable under monocular ambiguity, so we first recover object articulation, then synthesize human motion conditioned on the reconstructed object states. ArtHOI bridges video-based generation and geometry-aware reconstruction, producing interactions that are both semantically aligned and physically grounded. Across diverse articulated scenes (e.g., opening fridges, cabinets, microwaves), ArtHOI significantly outperforms prior methods in contact accuracy, penetration reduction, and articulation fidelity, extending zero-shot interaction synthesis beyond rigid manipulation through reconstruction-informed synthesis.

摘要（中文）：在没有3D/4D监督的情况下合成具有物理合理性的铰接人-物交互(HOI)仍然是一项根本挑战。虽然最近的零样本方法利用视频扩散模型来合成人-物交互，但它们主要局限于刚性物体操作，并且缺乏显式的4D几何推理。为了弥合这一差距，我们将铰接HOI合成构建为从单目视频先验中的4D重建问题：仅给定由扩散模型生成的视频，我们在没有任何3D监督的情况下重建完整的4D铰接场景。这种基于重建的方法将生成的2D视频视为反向渲染问题的监督，从而恢复几何一致且物理合理的4D场景，这些场景自然地尊重接触、铰接和时间连贯性。我们推出了ArtHOI，这是第一个通过从视频先验进行4D重建来合成铰接人-物交互的零样本框架。我们的关键设计是：1) 基于光流的部件分割：利用光流作为几何线索来解耦单目视频中的动态区域和静态区域；2) 解耦重建流程：在单目模糊的情况下，人类运动和物体铰接的联合优化是不稳定的，因此我们首先恢复物体铰接，然后合成以重建的物体状态为条件的人类运动。ArtHOI桥接了基于视频的生成和几何感知的重建，产生了语义对齐且物理基础扎实的交互。在各种铰接场景（例如，打开冰箱、橱柜、微波炉）中，ArtHOI在接触精度、穿透减少和铰接保真度方面显著优于现有方法，通过重建感知合成将零样本交互合成扩展到刚性操作之外。

Authors: Zihao Huang, Tianqi Liu, Zhaoxi Chen, Shaocong Xu, Saining Zhang, Lixing Xiao, Zhiguo Cao, Wei Li, Hao Zhao, Ziwei Liu

Real Eyes Realize Faster: Gaze Stability and Pupil Novelty for Efficient Egocentric Learning

简要说明：该论文提出了一种利用注视稳定性和瞳孔扩张来高效选择以自我为中心的视频帧的方法，结果表明，一小部分精选的帧可以匹配完整视频在活动和场景识别等任务中的性能。

TLDR: This paper presents a method for efficient egocentric video frame selection using gaze stability and pupil dilation, showing that a small subset of curated frames can match the performance of the full video for tasks like activity and scene recognition.

Abstract: Always-on egocentric cameras are increasingly used as demonstrations for embodied robotics, imitation learning, and assistive AR, but the resulting video streams are dominated by redundant and low-quality frames. Under the storage and battery constraints of wearable devices, choosing which frames to keep is as important as how to learn from them. We observe that modern eye-tracking headsets provide a continuous, training-free side channel that decomposes into two complementary axes: gaze fixation captures visual stability (quality), while pupil response captures arousal-linked moments (novelty). We operationalize this insight as a Dual-Criterion Frame Curator that first gates frames by gaze quality and then ranks the survivors by pupil-derived novelty. On the Visual Experience Dataset (VEDB), curated frames at 10% budget match the classification performance of the full stream, and naive signal fusion consistently destroys both contributions. The benefit is task-dependent: pupil ranking improves activity recognition, while gaze-only selection already dominates for scene recognition, confirming that the two signals serve genuinely different roles. Our method requires no model inference and operates at capture time, offering a path toward efficient, always-on egocentric data curation.

摘要（中文）：始终开启的自我中心相机越来越多地被用作具身机器人、模仿学习和辅助增强现实的演示，但由此产生的视频流充斥着冗余和低质量的帧。在可穿戴设备的存储和电池约束下，选择保留哪些帧与如何从这些帧中学习同样重要。我们观察到，现代眼动追踪头显提供了一个连续的、无需训练的辅助通道，可以分解为两个互补的轴：视线注视捕捉视觉稳定性（质量），而瞳孔反应捕捉与唤醒相关的时刻（新颖性）。我们将这一洞察操作化为一个双重标准帧选择器，它首先通过视线质量来筛选帧，然后按瞳孔导出的新颖性对幸存者进行排序。在视觉体验数据集（VEDB）上，以10%预算策展的帧与完整流的分类性能相匹配，而简单的信号融合始终会破坏这两方面的贡献。这种好处取决于任务：瞳孔排序提高了活动识别率，而仅视线选择已经在场景识别方面占据主导地位，证实了这两个信号发挥着真正不同的作用。我们的方法不需要模型推断，并且在捕获时运行，为高效、始终开启的自我中心数据策展提供了一条途径。

Authors: Ajan Subramanian, Sumukh Bettadapura, Rohan Sathish

Yolo-Key-6D: Single Stage Monocular 6D Pose Estimation with Keypoint Enhancements

简要说明：该论文介绍了一种快速准确的单阶段单目6D姿态估计方法Yolo-Key-6D，该方法通过关键点回归头和连续9D旋转表示来增强YOLO，并在标准基准测试中实现了有竞争力的实时结果。

TLDR: The paper introduces Yolo-Key-6D, a fast and accurate single-stage monocular 6D pose estimation method which enhances YOLO with a keypoint regression head and a continuous 9D rotation representation, achieving competitive results on standard benchmarks in real-time.

Abstract: Estimating the 6D pose of objects from a single RGB image is a critical task for robotics and extended reality applications. However, state-of-the-art multi stage methods often suffer from high latency, making them unsuitable for real time use. In this paper, we present Yolo-Key-6D, a novel single stage, end-to-end framework for monocular 6D pose estimation designed for both speed and accuracy. Our approach enhances a YOLO based architecture by integrating an auxiliary head that regresses the 2D projections of an object's 3D bounding box corners. This keypoint detection task significantly improves the network's understanding of 3D geometry. For stable end-to-end training, we directly regress rotation using a continuous 9D representation projected to SO(3) via singular value decomposition. On the LINEMOD and LINEMOD-Occluded benchmarks, YOLO-Key-6D achieves competitive accuracy scores of 96.24% and 69.41%, respectively, with the ADD(-S) 0.1d metric, while proving itself to operate in real time. Our results demonstrate that a carefully designed single stage method can provide a practical and effective balance of performance and efficiency for real world deployment.

摘要（中文）：从单张RGB图像中估计物体的6D姿态是机器人和扩展现实应用中的一项关键任务。然而，目前最先进的多阶段方法通常存在高延迟问题，使其不适用于实时应用。在本文中，我们提出Yolo-Key-6D，一种新颖的单阶段、端到端单目6D姿态估计框架，旨在兼顾速度和精度。我们的方法通过集成一个辅助头来增强基于YOLO的架构，该辅助头回归物体3D边界框角点的2D投影。这种关键点检测任务显著提高了网络对3D几何的理解。为了实现稳定的端到端训练，我们使用连续的9D表示直接回归旋转，并通过奇异值分解投影到SO(3)。在LINEMOD和LINEMOD-Occluded基准数据集上，YOLO-Key-6D在使用ADD(-S) 0.1d指标时分别实现了96.24%和69.41%具有竞争力的精度分数，同时证明其能够实时运行。我们的结果表明，精心设计的单阶段方法可以在实际部署中为性能和效率提供实用有效的平衡。

Authors: Kemal Alperen Çetiner, Hazım Kemal Ekenel

LiDAR Prompted Spatio-Temporal Multi-View Stereo for Autonomous Driving

简要说明：DriveMVS 是一项新颖的多视图立体框架，它使用激光雷达提示来提高自动驾驶深度估计中的度量精度、时间稳定性和跨域迁移能力。

TLDR: DriveMVS is a novel multi-view stereo framework that uses LiDAR prompts to improve metric accuracy, temporal stability, and cross-domain transfer in autonomous driving depth estimation.

Abstract: Accurate metric depth is critical for autonomous driving perception and simulation, yet current approaches struggle to achieve high metric accuracy, multi-view and temporal consistency, and cross-domain generalization. To address these challenges, we present DriveMVS, a novel multi-view stereo framework that reconciles these competing objectives through two key insights: (1) Sparse but metrically accurate LiDAR observations can serve as geometric prompts to anchor depth estimation in absolute scale, and (2) deep fusion of diverse cues is essential for resolving ambiguities and enhancing robustness, while a spatio-temporal decoder ensures consistency across frames. Built upon these principles, DriveMVS embeds the LiDAR prompt in two ways: as a hard geometric prior that anchors the cost volume, and as soft feature-wise guidance fused by a triple-cue combiner. Regarding temporal consistency, DriveMVS employs a spatio-temporal decoder that jointly leverages geometric cues from the MVS cost volume and temporal context from neighboring frames. Experiments show that DriveMVS achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple benchmarks, excelling in metric accuracy, temporal stability, and zero-shot cross-domain transfer, demonstrating its practical value for scalable, reliable autonomous driving systems.

摘要（中文）：精确的度量深度对于自动驾驶感知和仿真至关重要，然而，目前的方法难以实现高精度的度量精度、多视角和时间一致性，以及跨域泛化能力。为了应对这些挑战，我们提出DriveMVS，这是一种新型的多视角立体视觉框架，通过两个关键见解来协调这些相互竞争的目标： (1) 稀疏但具有度量精度的激光雷达观测可以作为几何提示，将深度估计锚定在绝对尺度上，以及(2) 多样化线索的深度融合对于解决模糊性和增强鲁棒性至关重要，而时空解码器可确保跨帧之间的一致性。基于这些原则，DriveMVS通过两种方式嵌入激光雷达提示：作为锚定代价体的硬几何先验，以及由三重线索组合器融合的软特征化指导。在时间一致性方面，DriveMVS采用了一种时空解码器，该解码器联合利用来自MVS代价体的几何线索和来自相邻帧的时间上下文。实验表明，DriveMVS在多个基准测试中实现了最先进的性能，在度量精度、时间稳定性和零样本跨域迁移方面表现出色，证明了其对于可扩展、可靠的自动驾驶系统的实际价值。

Authors: Qihao Sun, Jiarun Liu, Ziqian Ni, Jianyun Xu, Tao Xie, Lijun Zhao, Ruifeng Li, Sheng Yang

RAGTrack: Language-aware RGBT Tracking with Retrieval-Augmented Generation

简要说明：该论文介绍了RAGTrack，一个用于鲁棒的RGB-Thermal目标跟踪的检索增强生成框架。它通过结合由MLLM生成的文本描述，解决了如依赖初始帧和模态差距等问题，并达到了最先进的性能。

TLDR: The paper introduces RAGTrack, a Retrieval-Augmented Generation framework for robust RGB-Thermal object tracking, addressing issues like reliance on initial frames and modality gaps by incorporating textual descriptions generated by MLLMs and achieving state-of-the-art results.

Abstract: RGB-Thermal (RGBT) tracking aims to achieve robust object localization across diverse environmental conditions by fusing visible and thermal infrared modalities. However, existing RGBT trackers rely solely on initial-frame visual information for target modeling, failing to adapt to appearance variations due to the absence of language guidance. Furthermore, current methods suffer from redundant search regions and heterogeneous modality gaps, causing background distraction. To address these issues, we first introduce textual descriptions into RGBT tracking benchmarks. This is accomplished through a pipeline that leverages Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to automatically produce texual annotations. Afterwards, we propose RAGTrack, a novel Retrieval-Augmented Generation framework for robust RGBT tracking. To this end, we introduce a Multi-modal Transformer Encoder (MTE) for unified visual-language modeling. Then, we design an Adaptive Token Fusion (ATF) to select target-relevant tokens and perform channel exchanges based on cross-modal correlations, mitigating search redundancies and modality gaps. Finally, we propose a Context-aware Reasoning Module (CRM) to maintain a dynamic knowledge base and employ a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to enable temporal linguistic reasoning for robust target modeling. Extensive experiments on four RGBT benchmarks demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance across various challenging scenarios. The source code is available https://github.com/IdolLab/RAGTrack.

摘要（中文）：RGB-热成像 (RGBT) 跟踪旨在通过融合可见光和热红外模态，在各种环境条件下实现鲁棒的目标定位。然而，现有的 RGBT 跟踪器仅依赖于初始帧的视觉信息进行目标建模，由于缺乏语言引导，无法适应外观变化。此外，当前方法存在冗余搜索区域和异构模态差距的问题，导致背景干扰。为了解决这些问题，我们首先将文本描述引入 RGBT 跟踪基准。这是通过一个利用多模态大型语言模型 (MLLM) 自动生成文本标注的流水线实现的。随后，我们提出 RAGTrack，一种用于鲁棒 RGBT 跟踪的新型检索增强生成框架。为此，我们引入一个多模态 Transformer 编码器 (MTE) 用于统一的视觉-语言建模。然后，我们设计了一个自适应 Token 融合 (ATF) 模块，以选择目标相关的 Token 并基于跨模态相关性执行通道交换，从而减轻搜索冗余和模态差距。最后，我们提出了一个上下文感知推理模块 (CRM) 以维护一个动态知识库，并采用检索增强生成 (RAG) 来实现时间语言推理，从而实现鲁棒的目标建模。在四个 RGBT 基准上的大量实验表明，我们的框架在各种具有挑战性的场景中实现了最先进的性能。源代码可在 https://github.com/IdolLab/RAGTrack 获取。

Authors: Hao Li, Yuhao Wang, Wenning Hao, Pingping Zhang, Dong Wang, Huchuan Lu

MIBURI: Towards Expressive Interactive Gesture Synthesis

简要说明：MIBURI是一个新颖的框架，用于生成与实时语音同步的富有表现力的全身手势和面部表情，它使用身体部位感知的手势编解码器和基于因果LLM的架构。

TLDR: MIBURI is a novel framework for generating expressive full-body gestures and facial expressions synchronized with real-time speech, using body-part aware gesture codecs and a causal LLM-based architecture.

Abstract: Embodied Conversational Agents (ECAs) aim to emulate human face-to-face interaction through speech, gestures, and facial expressions. Current large language model (LLM)-based conversational agents lack embodiment and the expressive gestures essential for natural interaction. Existing solutions for ECAs often produce rigid, low-diversity motions, that are unsuitable for human-like interaction. Alternatively, generative methods for co-speech gesture synthesis yield natural body gestures but depend on future speech context and require long run-times. To bridge this gap, we present MIBURI, the first online, causal framework for generating expressive full-body gestures and facial expressions synchronized with real-time spoken dialogue. We employ body-part aware gesture codecs that encode hierarchical motion details into multi-level discrete tokens. These tokens are then autoregressively generated by a two-dimensional causal framework conditioned on LLM-based speech-text embeddings, modeling both temporal dynamics and part-level motion hierarchy in real time. Further, we introduce auxiliary objectives to encourage expressive and diverse gestures while preventing convergence to static poses. Comparative evaluations demonstrate that our causal and real-time approach produces natural and contextually aligned gestures against recent baselines. We urge the reader to explore demo videos on https://vcai.mpi-inf.mpg.de/projects/MIBURI/.

摘要（中文）：具身对话代理（ECA）旨在通过语音、手势和面部表情来模拟人与人之间的面对面互动。目前基于大型语言模型（LLM）的对话代理缺乏具身性和自然互动所必需的富有表现力的手势。现有的ECA解决方案通常产生僵硬、低多样性的动作，不适合类人互动。另一种方法是用生成式方法进行伴随语音的手势合成，它可以生成自然的身体手势，但依赖于未来的语音上下文，并且需要较长的运行时间。为了弥合这一差距，我们提出了 MIBURI，这是第一个在线、因果框架，用于生成与实时口语对话同步的富有表现力的全身手势和面部表情。我们采用身体部位感知的姿势编解码器，将分层运动细节编码为多级离散令牌。然后，这些令牌由基于LLM的语音-文本嵌入条件下的二维因果框架自回归地生成，从而实时地建模了时间动态和部分级别的运动层次结构。此外，我们引入了辅助目标，以鼓励富有表现力和多样化的手势，同时防止收敛到静态姿势。对比评估表明，与最新的基线方法相比，我们的因果和实时方法产生了自然且上下文对齐的手势。我们恳请读者访问 https://vcai.mpi-inf.mpg.de/projects/MIBURI/ 观看演示视频。

Authors: M. Hamza Mughal, Rishabh Dabral, Vera Demberg, Christian Theobalt

LoGeR: Long-Context Geometric Reconstruction with Hybrid Memory

简要说明：LoGeR 提出了一种新的混合记忆架构，用于扩展前馈 3D 重建模型以处理极长的视频序列（数千帧），无需后优化，并在现有基准和新的长序列数据集上实现了显着的性能提升。

TLDR: LoGeR introduces a novel hybrid memory architecture for scaling feedforward 3D reconstruction models to handle extremely long video sequences (thousands of frames) without post-optimization, achieving significant performance gains on existing benchmarks and a new long-sequence dataset.

Abstract: Feedforward geometric foundation models achieve strong short-window reconstruction, yet scaling them to minutes-long videos is bottlenecked by quadratic attention complexity or limited effective memory in recurrent designs. We present LoGeR (Long-context Geometric Reconstruction), a novel architecture that scales dense 3D reconstruction to extremely long sequences without post-optimization. LoGeR processes video streams in chunks, leveraging strong bidirectional priors for high-fidelity intra-chunk reasoning. To manage the critical challenge of coherence across chunk boundaries, we propose a learning-based hybrid memory module. This dual-component system combines a parametric Test-Time Training (TTT) memory to anchor the global coordinate frame and prevent scale drift, alongside a non-parametric Sliding Window Attention (SWA) mechanism to preserve uncompressed context for high-precision adjacent alignment. Remarkably, this memory architecture enables LoGeR to be trained on sequences of 128 frames, and generalize up to thousands of frames during inference. Evaluated across standard benchmarks and a newly repurposed VBR dataset with sequences of up to 19k frames, LoGeR substantially outperforms prior state-of-the-art feedforward methods--reducing ATE on KITTI by over 74%--and achieves robust, globally consistent reconstruction over unprecedented horizons.

摘要（中文）：前馈几何基础模型在短窗口重建中表现出色，然而将其拓展到数分钟长的视频受限于二次注意力复杂度或循环设计中有限的有效记忆。我们提出了LoGeR (长上下文几何重建)，一种新型架构，可在无需后优化的情况下将密集3D重建扩展到极长的序列。LoGeR以块为单位处理视频流，利用强大的双向先验进行高保真的块内推理。为了应对跨块边界一致性的关键挑战，我们提出了一种基于学习的混合记忆模块。该双组件系统结合了一个参数化的测试时训练(TTT)记忆，用于锚定全局坐标框架并防止尺度漂移，以及一个非参数化的滑动窗口注意力(SWA)机制，用于保留未压缩的上下文以实现高精度的相邻对齐。值得注意的是，这种记忆架构使得LoGeR能够在128帧的序列上进行训练，并在推理期间推广到数千帧。在标准基准测试和一个新改建的VBR数据集（序列长度高达1.9万帧）上进行评估，LoGeR显著优于先前的最先进前馈方法——在KITTI上将ATE降低了超过74%——并在前所未有的时间范围内实现了稳健、全局一致的重建。

Authors: Junyi Zhang, Charles Herrmann, Junhwa Hur, Chen Sun, Ming-Hsuan Yang, Forrester Cole, Trevor Darrell, Deqing Sun

DuoMo: Dual Motion Diffusion for World-Space Human Reconstruction

简要说明：DuoMo 使用双重扩散模型从噪声视频中重建世界坐标系下的人体运动，通过将运动学习分解为相机空间和世界空间扩散模型，实现了最先进的结果。

TLDR: DuoMo uses a dual-diffusion model to reconstruct human motion in world-space coordinates from noisy video, achieving state-of-the-art results by factoring motion learning into camera-space and world-space diffusion models.

Abstract: We present DuoMo, a generative method that recovers human motion in world-space coordinates from unconstrained videos with noisy or incomplete observations. Reconstructing such motion requires solving a fundamental trade-off: generalizing from diverse and noisy video inputs while maintaining global motion consistency. Our approach addresses this problem by factorizing motion learning into two diffusion models. The camera-space model first estimates motion from videos in camera coordinates. The world-space model then lifts this initial estimate into world coordinates and refines it to be globally consistent. Together, the two models can reconstruct motion across diverse scenes and trajectories, even from highly noisy or incomplete observations. Moreover, our formulation is general, generating the motion of mesh vertices directly and bypassing parametric models. DuoMo achieves state-of-the-art performance. On EMDB, our method obtains a 16% reduction in world-space reconstruction error while maintaining low foot skating. On RICH, it obtains a 30% reduction in world-space error. Project page: https://yufu-wang.github.io/duomo/

摘要（中文）：我们提出了DuoMo，一种生成方法，用于从具有噪声或不完整观测的无约束视频中恢复世界坐标系下的人体运动。重建此类运动需要解决一个根本性的权衡：从多样化和嘈杂的视频输入中泛化，同时保持全局运动一致性。我们的方法通过将运动学习分解为两个扩散模型来解决这个问题。摄像机空间模型首先从摄像机坐标中的视频估计运动。然后，世界空间模型将此初始估计提升到世界坐标系中，并对其进行优化以实现全局一致性。这两个模型协同工作，即使从高度嘈杂或不完整的观测中，也能重建跨越不同场景和轨迹的运动。此外，我们的公式具有通用性，可直接生成网格顶点的运动，绕过参数模型。DuoMo取得了最先进的性能。在EMDB上，我们的方法在保持低足部滑动的条件下，将世界坐标系重建误差降低了16%。在RICH上，它将世界坐标系误差降低了30%。项目页面：https://yufu-wang.github.io/duomo/

Authors: Yufu Wang, Evonne Ng, Soyong Shin, Rawal Khirodkar, Yuan Dong, Zhaoen Su, Jinhyung Park, Kris Kitani, Alexander Richard, Fabian Prada, Michael Zollhofer

Continuous Modal Logical Neural Networks: Modal Reasoning via Stochastic Accessibility

简要说明：该论文介绍了连续模态逻辑神经网络 (CMLNNs)，它利用神经 SDE 进行模态推理；同时介绍了逻辑信息神经网络 (LINNs)，它将逻辑公式融入训练损失中，并在多机器人系统、动力系统和安全机器人技术中展示了应用。

TLDR: The paper introduces Continuous Modal Logical Neural Networks (CMLNNs) leveraging Neural SDEs for modal reasoning and Logic-Informed Neural Networks (LINNs) integrating logical formulas into training losses, demonstrating applications in multi-robot systems, dynamical systems, and safe robotics.

Abstract: We propose Fluid Logic, a paradigm in which modal logical reasoning, temporal, epistemic, doxastic, deontic, is lifted from discrete Kripke structures to continuous manifolds via Neural Stochastic Differential Equations (Neural SDEs). Each type of modal operator is backed by a dedicated Neural SDE, and nested formulas compose these SDEs in a single differentiable graph. A key instantiation is Logic-Informed Neural Networks (LINNs): analogous to Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), LINNs embed modal logical formulas such as ($\Box$ bounded) and ($\Diamond$ visits\_lobe) directly into the training loss, guiding neural networks to produce solutions that are structurally consistent with prescribed logical properties, without requiring knowledge of the governing equations. The resulting framework, Continuous Modal Logical Neural Networks (CMLNNs), yields several key properties: (i) stochastic diffusion prevents quantifier collapse ($\Box$ and $\Diamond$ differ), unlike deterministic ODEs; (ii) modal operators are entropic risk measures, sound with respect to risk-based semantics with explicit Monte Carlo concentration guarantees; (iii)SDE-induced accessibility provides structural correspondence with classical modal axioms; (iv) parameterizing accessibility through dynamics reduces memory from quadratic in world count to linear in parameters. Three case studies demonstrate that Fluid Logic and LINNs can guide neural networks to produce consistent solutions across diverse domains: epistemic/doxastic logic (multi-robot hallucination detection), temporal logic (recovering the Lorenz attractor geometry from logical constraints alone), and deontic logic (learning safe confinement dynamics from a logical specification).

摘要（中文）：我们提出流体逻辑，这是一种将模态逻辑推理，包括时序、认知、信念、义务逻辑，从离散Kripke结构提升到连续流形的范式，其实现通过神经随机微分方程（Neural SDEs）。每种类型的模态算子都由一个专用的神经SDE支持，嵌套公式将这些SDE组合成一个可微的图。一个关键的实例化是逻辑知情神经网络（LINNs）：类似于物理知情神经网络（PINNs），LINNs将模态逻辑公式，例如（$\Box$ 有界）和（$\Diamond$ 访问_波瓣），直接嵌入到训练损失中，引导神经网络产生与预定的逻辑属性在结构上一致的解，而无需了解控制方程。 由此产生的框架，连续模态逻辑神经网络（CMLNNs），产生了以下几个关键特性：（i）随机扩散防止量词坍缩（$\Box$和$\Diamond$不同），这与确定性ODE不同；（ii）模态算子是熵风险度量，对于基于风险的语义是可靠的，并具有显式的蒙特卡洛集中保证；（iii）SDE诱导的可达性提供了与经典模态公理的结构对应性；（iv）通过动力学参数化可达性将内存从世界计数的二次方降低到参数的线性。 三个案例研究表明，流体逻辑和LINNs可以引导神经网络在不同的领域产生一致的解：认知/信念逻辑（多机器人幻觉检测），时序逻辑（仅从逻辑约束中恢复洛伦兹吸引子的几何形状）和义务逻辑（从逻辑规范中学习安全约束动力学）。

Authors: Antonin Sulc

On the Learnability of Offline Model-Based Optimization: A Ranking Perspective

简要说明：该论文将离线模型优化重构为一个排序问题，而非回归问题，提出了一个基于排序的目标函数和一个分布感知的排序方法，该方法优于现有方法，同时也揭示了离线MBO的内在局限性。

TLDR: This paper reframes offline model-based optimization as a ranking problem rather than a regression problem, introducing a ranking-based objective and a distribution-aware ranking method that outperforms existing approaches, while also revealing inherent limitations of offline MBO.

Abstract: Offline model-based optimization (MBO) seeks to discover high-performing designs using only a fixed dataset of past evaluations. Most existing methods rely on learning a surrogate model via regression and implicitly assume that good predictive accuracy leads to good optimization performance. In this work, we challenge this assumption and study offline MBO from a learnability perspective. We argue that offline optimization is fundamentally a problem of ranking high-quality designs rather than accurate value prediction. Specifically, we introduce an optimization-oriented risk based on ranking between near-optimal and suboptimal designs, and develop a unified theoretical framework that connects surrogate learning to final optimization. We prove the theoretical advantages of ranking over regression, and identify distributional mismatch between the training data and near-optimal designs as the dominant error. Inspired by this, we design a distribution-aware ranking method to reduce this mismatch. Empirical results across various tasks show that our approach outperforms twenty existing methods, validating our theoretical findings. Additionally, both theoretical and empirical results reveal intrinsic limitations in offline MBO, showing a regime in which no offline method can avoid over-optimistic extrapolation.

摘要（中文）：离线模型优化(MBO)旨在仅使用过去的固定评估数据集来发现高性能的设计方案。现有的大多数方法依赖于通过回归学习一个代理模型，并隐式地假设良好的预测精度能够带来良好的优化性能。在本文中，我们挑战了这个假设，并从可学习性的角度研究离线MBO。我们认为，离线优化本质上是一个对高质量设计方案进行排序的问题，而不是精确的价值预测。具体来说，我们引入了一个面向优化的风险，该风险基于近优方案和次优方案之间的排序，并开发了一个统一的理论框架，将代理学习与最终优化联系起来。我们证明了排序相对于回归在理论上的优势，并将训练数据和近优方案之间的分布不匹配确定为主要误差来源。受此启发，我们设计了一种分布感知的排序方法来减少这种不匹配。在各种任务上的实验结果表明，我们的方法优于现有的二十种方法，验证了我们的理论发现。此外，理论和实验结果都揭示了离线MBO的内在局限性，表明存在一种任何离线方法都无法避免过度乐观外推的机制。

Authors: Shen-Huan Lyu, Rong-Xi Tan, Ke Xue, Yi-Xiao He, Yu Huang, Qingfu Zhang, Chao Qian

Freezing of Gait Prediction using Proactive Agent that Learns from Selected Experience and DDQN Algorithm

简要说明：本文提出了一种基于强化学习的方法，使用DDQN和优先级经验回放来预测帕金森病患者的冻结步态（FOG），以便进行主动干预，实现了超过7秒的预测范围。

TLDR: This paper presents a reinforcement learning-based approach using DDQN and prioritized experience replay to predict freezing of gait (FOG) in Parkinson's Disease for proactive intervention, achieving prediction horizons of over 7 seconds.

Abstract: Freezing of Gait (FOG) is a debilitating motor symptom commonly experienced by individuals with Parkinson's Disease (PD) which often leads to falls and reduced mobility. Timely and accurate prediction of FOG episodes is essential for enabling proactive interventions through assistive technologies. This study presents a reinforcement learning-based framework designed to identify optimal pre-FOG onset points, thereby extending the prediction horizon for anticipatory cueing systems. The model implements a Double Deep Q-Network (DDQN) architecture enhanced with Prioritized Experience Replay (PER) allowing the agent to focus learning on high-impact experiences and refine its policy. Trained over 9000 episodes with a reward shaping strategy that promotes cautious decision-making, the agent demonstrated robust performance in both subject-dependent and subject-independent evaluations. The model achieved a prediction horizon of up to 8.72 seconds prior to FOG onset in subject-independent scenarios and 7.89 seconds in subject-dependent settings. These results highlight the model's potential for integration into wearable assistive devices, offering timely and personalized interventions to mitigate FOG in PD patients.

摘要（中文）：步态冻结 (FOG) 是帕金森病 (PD) 患者常见的使人衰弱的运动症状，经常导致跌倒和活动能力下降。及时准确地预测 FOG 发作对于通过辅助技术实现主动干预至关重要。本研究提出了一种基于强化学习的框架，旨在识别最佳的 FOG 发作前起始点，从而延长预期提示系统的预测范围。该模型采用双层深度 Q 网络 (DDQN) 架构，并增强了优先级经验回放 (PER)，使智能体能够专注于高影响力经验的学习并改进其策略。经过 9000 多个回合的训练，采用奖励塑造策略来促进谨慎决策，该智能体在受试者相关和受试者无关的评估中均表现出稳健的性能。该模型在受试者无关情景下实现了最长 8.72 秒的 FOG 发作前预测范围，在受试者相关情景下实现了 7.89 秒。这些结果突显了该模型集成到可穿戴辅助设备中的潜力，为帕金森病患者提供及时的个性化干预以减轻 FOG。

Adaptive Sensing of Continuous Physical Systems for Machine Learning

简要说明：本文介绍了一个自适应学习如何感知物理动力系统的框架，以优化预测性能，并在混沌系统上显示出更高的准确性。

TLDR: This paper introduces a framework for adaptively learning how to sense physical dynamical systems to optimize prediction performance, showing improved accuracy on chaotic systems.

Abstract: Physical dynamical systems can be viewed as natural information processors: their systems preserve, transform, and disperse input information. This perspective motivates learning not only from data generated by such systems, but also how to measure them in a way that extracts the most useful information for a given task. We propose a general computing framework for adaptive information extraction from dynamical systems, in which a trainable attention module learns both where to probe the system state and how to combine these measurements to optimize prediction performance. As a concrete instantiation, we implement this idea using a spatiotemporal field governed by a partial differential equation as the underlying dynamics, though the framework applies equally to any system whose state can be sampled. Our results show that adaptive spatial sensing significantly improves prediction accuracy on canonical chaotic benchmarks. This work provides a perspective on attention-enhanced reservoir computing as a special case of a broader paradigm: neural networks as trainable measurement devices for extracting information from physical dynamical systems.

摘要（中文）：物理动力系统可以被视为天然的信息处理器：它们保存、转换和分散输入信息。 这种观点推动我们不仅要学习这些系统生成的数据，还要学习如何以一种为给定任务提取最有用信息的方式测量它们。 我们提出了一种通用的计算框架，用于从动力系统中自适应地提取信息，其中一个可训练的注意力模块学习探测系统状态的位置以及如何组合这些测量值以优化预测性能。 作为一个具体的实例，我们使用由偏微分方程控制的时空场作为底层动力学来实现这个想法， 尽管该框架同样适用于任何状态可以被采样的系统。 我们的结果表明，自适应空间传感显著提高了规范混沌基准上的预测精度。 这项工作提供了一种关于注意力增强的储层计算的视角，并将其视为更广泛范例的一个特例：神经网络作为可训练的测量设备，用于从物理动力系统中提取信息。

Authors: Felix Köster, Atsushi Uchida

Hybrid Belief Reinforcement Learning for Efficient Coordinated Spatial Exploration

简要说明：本文提出了一种混合置信强化学习（HBRL）框架，用于使用多个自主代理进行高效的协同空间探索，结合了基于模型和无模型的强化学习方法，并在不同阶段之间进行知识迁移。

TLDR: This paper proposes a hybrid belief-reinforcement learning (HBRL) framework for efficient coordinated spatial exploration using multiple autonomous agents, combining model-based and model-free RL approaches with knowledge transfer between phases.

Abstract: Coordinating multiple autonomous agents to explore and serve spatially heterogeneous demand requires jointly learning unknown spatial patterns and planning trajectories that maximize task performance. Pure model-based approaches provide structured uncertainty estimates but lack adaptive policy learning, while deep reinforcement learning often suffers from poor sample efficiency when spatial priors are absent. This paper presents a hybrid belief-reinforcement learning (HBRL) framework to address this gap. In the first phase, agents construct spatial beliefs using a Log-Gaussian Cox Process (LGCP) and execute information-driven trajectories guided by a Pathwise Mutual Information (PathMI) planner with multi-step lookahead. In the second phase, trajectory control is transferred to a Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) agent, warm-started through dual-channel knowledge transfer: belief state initialization supplies spatial uncertainty, and replay buffer seeding provides demonstration trajectories generated during LGCP exploration. A variance-normalized overlap penalty enables coordinated coverage through shared belief state, permitting cooperative sensing in high-uncertainty regions while discouraging redundant coverage in well-explored areas. The framework is evaluated on a multi-UAV wireless service provisioning task. Results show 10.8% higher cumulative reward and 38% faster convergence over baselines, with ablation studies confirming that dual-channel transfer outperforms either channel alone.

摘要（中文）：协调多个自主体探索并服务于空间异质性需求，需要在联合学习未知空间模式的同时，规划最大化任务表现的轨迹。纯粹基于模型的方法能提供结构化的不确定性估计，但缺乏自适应策略学习能力；而深度强化学习在缺乏空间先验知识时，常常面临样本效率低下的问题。本文提出了一种混合信念-强化学习（HBRL）框架来弥补这一差距。在第一阶段，智能体使用对数高斯考克斯过程（LGCP）构建空间信念，并执行由路径互信息（PathMI）规划器指导的、具备多步前瞻的信息驱动轨迹。在第二阶段，轨迹控制权转移给软演员-评论家（SAC）智能体，通过双通道知识迁移进行预热：信念状态初始化提供空间不确定性，重放缓冲区播种提供在LGCP探索期间生成的演示轨迹。一种方差归一化重叠惩罚能够通过共享信念状态实现协同覆盖，从而允许在高不确定性区域进行合作感知，同时抑制在已充分探索区域的冗余覆盖。该框架在一个多无人机无线服务提供任务上进行了评估。结果表明，相比基线方法，累积奖励提高了10.8%，收敛速度提高了38%，消融研究证实了双通道迁移优于单独使用任一通道。

Authors: Danish Rizvi, David Boyle

Q-Measure-Learning for Continuous State RL: Efficient Implementation and Convergence

简要说明：本文介绍了一种新的强化学习算法Q-Measure-Learning，用于连续状态空间，该算法通过核积分有效地估计Q函数，并证明了其收敛性。他们在库存控制任务中展示了算法的性能。

TLDR: This paper introduces Q-Measure-Learning, a novel reinforcement learning algorithm for continuous state spaces that efficiently estimates the Q-function via kernel integration and proves its convergence. They show its performance on an inventory control task.

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning in infinite-horizon discounted Markov decision processes with continuous state spaces, where data are generated online from a single trajectory under a Markovian behavior policy. To avoid maintaining an infinite-dimensional, function-valued estimate, we propose the novel Q-Measure-Learning, which learns a signed empirical measure supported on visited state-action pairs and reconstructs an action-value estimate via kernel integration. The method jointly estimates the stationary distribution of the behavior chain and the Q-measure through coupled stochastic approximation, leading to an efficient weight-based implementation with $O(n)$ memory and $O(n)$ computation cost per iteration. Under uniform ergodicity of the behavior chain, we prove almost sure sup-norm convergence of the induced Q-function to the fixed point of a kernel-smoothed Bellman operator. We also bound the approximation error between this limit and the optimal $Q^*$ as a function of the kernel bandwidth. To assess the performance of our proposed algorithm, we conduct RL experiments in a two-item inventory control setting.

摘要（中文）：我们研究具有连续状态空间的无限期折扣马尔可夫决策过程中的强化学习，其中数据在线生成自马尔科夫行为策略下的单个轨迹。为避免维护无限维度的函数值估计，我们提出了一种新的Q-测度学习方法，该方法学习一个有符号的经验测度，该测度支持于访问过的状态-动作对，并通过核积分重建动作价值估计。该方法通过耦合随机逼近联合估计行为链的平稳分布和Q-测度，从而实现一种高效的基于权重的实现，每次迭代具有$O(n)$的内存和$O(n)$的计算成本。在行为链的均匀遍历性下，我们证明了诱导的Q-函数几乎确定地以超范数收敛于核平滑的贝尔曼算子的不动点。我们还根据核带宽限制了该极限与最优$Q^*$之间的逼近误差。为了评估我们提出的算法的性能，我们在一个两项目库存控制环境中进行了强化学习实验。

Authors: Shengbo Wang

Minimax Optimal Strategy for Delayed Observations in Online Reinforcement Learning

简要说明：本文提出了一种用于具有延迟状态观测的MDP的极小极大最优强化学习算法，实现了接近最优的后悔界，并将该问题构建在一个更通用的MDP框架内。

TLDR: This paper proposes a minimax optimal reinforcement learning algorithm for MDPs with delayed state observations, achieving a near-optimal regret bound and formulating the problem within a more general MDP framework.

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning with delayed state observation, where the agent observes the current state after some random number of time steps. We propose an algorithm that combines the augmentation method and the upper confidence bound approach. For tabular Markov decision processes (MDPs), we derive a regret bound of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(H \sqrt{D_{\max} SAK})$, where $S$ and $A$ are the cardinalities of the state and action spaces, $H$ is the time horizon, $K$ is the number of episodes, and $D_{\max}$ is the maximum length of the delay. We also provide a matching lower bound up to logarithmic factors, showing the optimality of our approach. Our analytical framework formulates this problem as a special case of a broader class of MDPs, where their transition dynamics decompose into a known component and an unknown but structured component. We establish general results for this abstract setting, which may be of independent interest.

摘要（中文）：我们研究了具有延迟状态观测的强化学习，其中智能体在经过若干随机时间步后才能观测到当前状态。我们提出了一种结合了增广方法和上限置信区间方法的算法。对于表格型马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），我们推导出一个遗憾界$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(H \sqrt{D_{\max} SAK})$，其中$S$和$A$分别是状态空间和动作空间的基数，$H$是时间范围，$K$是幕数，$D_{\max}$是最大延迟长度。我们还提供了一个匹配的下界，仅相差对数因子，表明了我们方法的优越性。我们的分析框架将这个问题公式化为更广泛的一类MDP的特例，其中它们的转移动态可以分解为一个已知成分和一个未知但结构化的成分。我们为这个抽象设置建立了通用结果，这些结果可能具有独立的意义。

Authors: Harin Lee, Kevin Jamieson

Perception-Aware Time-Optimal Planning for Quadrotor Waypoint Flight

简要说明：本文提出了一种四旋翼飞行器的时间最优轨迹规划框架，该框架整合了感知约束，提高了闭环飞行成功率，并实现了高速自主导航。

TLDR: This paper presents a time-optimal trajectory planning framework for quadrotors that integrates perception constraints, improving closed-loop flight success rates and enabling high-speed autonomous navigation.

Abstract: Agile quadrotor flight pushes the limits of control, actuation, and onboard perception. While time-optimal trajectory planning has been extensively studied, existing approaches typically neglect the tight coupling between vehicle dynamics, environmental geometry, and the visual requirements of onboard state estimation. As a result, trajectories that are dynamically feasible may fail in closed-loop execution due to degraded visual quality. This paper introduces a unified time-optimal trajectory optimization framework for vision-based quadrotors that explicitly incorporates perception constraints alongside full nonlinear dynamics, rotor actuation limits, aerodynamic effects, camera field-of-view constraints, and convex geometric gate representations. The proposed formulation solves minimum-time lap trajectories for arbitrary racetracks with diverse gate shapes and orientations, while remaining numerically robust and computationally efficient. We derive an information-theoretic position uncertainty metric to quantify visual state-estimation quality and integrate it into the planner through three perception objectives: position uncertainty minimization, sequential field-of-view constraints, and look-ahead alignment. This enables systematic exploration of the trade-offs between speed and perceptual reliability. To accurately track the resulting perception-aware trajectories, we develop a model predictive contouring tracking controller that separates lateral and progress errors. Experiments demonstrate real-world flight speeds up to 9.8 m/s with 0.07 m average tracking error, and closed-loop success rates improved from 55% to 100% on a challenging Split-S course. The proposed system provides a scalable benchmark for studying the fundamental limits of perception-aware, time-optimal autonomous flight.

摘要（中文）：敏捷四旋翼飞行不断挑战控制、驱动和机载感知的极限。虽然时间最优轨迹规划已被广泛研究，但现有方法通常忽略了车辆动力学、环境几何结构以及机载状态估计的视觉需求之间的紧密耦合。因此，动力学上可行的轨迹可能会由于视觉质量下降而在闭环执行中失败。本文提出了一种统一的基于视觉的四旋翼飞行器时间最优轨迹优化框架，该框架明确地结合了感知约束以及完整的非线性动力学、旋翼驱动限制、空气动力学效应、相机视场约束和凸几何门表示。 所提出的公式解决了具有不同门形状和方向的任意赛道的最短时间圈轨迹，同时保持了数值鲁棒性和计算效率。我们推导出一个信息论的位置不确定性度量来量化视觉状态估计质量，并通过三个感知目标将其整合到规划器中：位置不确定性最小化、顺序视场约束和前瞻对齐。这使得能够系统地探索速度和感知可靠性之间的权衡。 为了准确跟踪由此产生的感知感知轨迹，我们开发了一种模型预测轮廓跟踪控制器，该控制器分离了横向误差和前进误差。实验表明，在具有挑战性的 Split-S 赛道上，实际飞行速度高达 9.8 米/秒，平均跟踪误差为 0.07 米，闭环成功率从 55% 提高到 100%。所提出的系统为研究感知感知、时间最优自主飞行的基本限制提供了一个可扩展的基准。

GSeg3D: A High-Precision Grid-Based Algorithm for Safety-Critical Ground Segmentation in LiDAR Point Clouds

简要说明：本文提出了一种基于网格的激光雷达点云地面分割算法（GSeg3D），旨在为安全攸关的自动驾驶和机器人应用提供高精度，因现有方法不足以满足需求。

TLDR: This paper introduces a grid-based ground segmentation algorithm (GSeg3D) for LiDAR point clouds, aiming for high precision in safety-critical autonomous driving and robotics applications where existing methods are insufficient.

Abstract: Ground segmentation in point cloud data is the process of separating ground points from non-ground points. This task is fundamental for perception in autonomous driving and robotics, where safety and reliable operation depend on the precise detection of obstacles and navigable surfaces. Existing methods often fall short of the high precision required in safety-critical environments, leading to false detections that can compromise decision-making. In this work, we present a ground segmentation approach designed to deliver consistently high precision, supporting the stringent requirements of autonomous vehicles and robotic systems operating in real-world, safety-critical scenarios.

摘要（中文）：点云数据中的地面分割是从非地面点中分离出地面点的过程。 这项任务对于自动驾驶和机器人领域的感知至关重要，在这些领域，安全且可靠的运行取决于对障碍物和可导航表面的精确检测。 现有方法通常无法满足安全关键环境中所需的高精度，导致可能危及决策的错误检测。 在这项工作中，我们提出了一种旨在提供始终如一的高精度的地面分割方法，以支持在真实世界、安全关键场景中运行的自动驾驶汽车和机器人系统的严格要求。

Authors: Muhammad Haider Khan Lodhi, Christoph Hertzberg

Map-Agnostic And Interactive Safety-Critical Scenario Generation via Multi-Objective Tree Search

简要说明：本文提出了一种与地图无关的多目标蒙特卡洛树搜索框架，用于生成逼真且多样的安全关键交通场景，以测试自动驾驶汽车的压力，展示了更高的碰撞率和场景复杂性。

TLDR: This paper presents a map-agnostic multi-objective Monte Carlo Tree Search framework for generating realistic and diverse safety-critical traffic scenarios to stress test autonomous vehicles, demonstrating improved collision rates and scenario complexity.

Abstract: Generating safety-critical scenarios is essential for validating the robustness of autonomous driving systems, yet existing methods often struggle to produce collisions that are both realistic and diverse while ensuring explicit interaction logic among traffic participants. This paper presents a novel framework for traffic-flow level safety-critical scenario generation via multi-objective Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS). We reframe trajectory feasibility and naturalistic behavior as optimization objectives within a unified evaluation function, enabling the discovery of diverse collision events without compromising realism. A hybrid Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) and Lower Confidence Bound (LCB) search strategy is introduced to balance exploratory efficiency with risk-averse decision-making. Furthermore, our method is map-agnostic and supports interactive scenario generation with each vehicle individually powered by SUMO's microscopic traffic models, enabling realistic agent behaviors in arbitrary geographic locations imported from OpenStreetMap. We validate our approach across four high-risk accident zones in Hong Kong's complex urban environments. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework achieves an 85\% collision failure rate while generating trajectories with superior feasibility and comfort metrics. The resulting scenarios exhibit greater complexity, as evidenced by increased vehicle mileage and CO\(_2\) emissions. Our work provides a principled solution for stress testing autonomous vehicles through the generation of realistic yet infrequent corner cases at traffic-flow level.

摘要（中文）：生成安全关键场景对于验证自动驾驶系统的鲁棒性至关重要，然而，现有方法通常难以生成既真实又多样化的碰撞，同时确保交通参与者之间存在明确的互动逻辑。本文提出了一种基于多目标蒙特卡罗树搜索(MCTS)的交通流层级安全关键场景生成的新型框架。我们将轨迹可行性和自然行为重新定义为统一评估函数中的优化目标，从而能够在不损害真实性的前提下发现各种碰撞事件。我们引入了一种混合的上置信上限(UCB)和下置信下限(LCB)搜索策略，以平衡探索效率与风险规避型决策。此外，我们的方法与地图无关，并支持交互式场景生成，每个车辆均由SUMO的微观交通模型驱动，从而能够在从OpenStreetMap导入的任意地理位置实现真实的智能体行为。我们在香港复杂城市环境中的四个高风险事故区域验证了我们的方法。实验结果表明，我们的框架实现了85%的碰撞失效率，同时生成具有卓越可行性和舒适度指标的轨迹。结果场景表现出更高的复杂性，车辆行驶里程和CO\(_2\)排放量的增加证明了这一点。我们的工作为通过生成交通流层面上现实但罕见的边缘案例来对自动驾驶汽车进行压力测试提供了一种有原则的解决方案。

Authors: Wenyun Li, Zejian Deng, Chen Sun

Dual-Interaction-Aware Cooperative Control Strategy for Alleviating Mixed Traffic Congestion

简要说明：本文介绍了一种双重交互感知协同控制（DIACC）策略，该策略利用多智能体强化学习（MARL）来改善混合 CAV-HDV 环境中的交通流量，实验结果表明该策略提高了交通效率和适应性。

TLDR: This paper introduces a Dual-Interaction-Aware Cooperative Control (DIACC) strategy using MARL to improve traffic flow in mixed CAV-HDV environments, demonstrating improved traffic efficiency and adaptability.

Abstract: As Intelligent Transportation System (ITS) develops, Connected and Automated Vehicles (CAVs) are expected to significantly reduce traffic congestion through cooperative strategies, such as in bottleneck areas. However, the uncertainty and diversity in the behaviors of Human-Driven Vehicles (HDVs) in mixed traffic environments present major challenges for CAV cooperation. This paper proposes a Dual-Interaction-Aware Cooperative Control (DIACC) strategy that enhances both local and global interaction perception within the Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework for Connected and Automated Vehicles (CAVs) in mixed traffic bottleneck scenarios. The DIACC strategy consists of three key innovations: 1) A Decentralized Interaction-Adaptive Decision-Making (D-IADM) module that enhances actor's local interaction perception by distinguishing CAV-CAV cooperative interactions from CAV-HDV observational interactions. 2) A Centralized Interaction-Enhanced Critic (C-IEC) that improves critic's global traffic understanding through interaction-aware value estimation, providing more accurate guidance for policy updates. 3) A reward design that employs softmin aggregation with temperature annealing to prioritize interaction-intensive scenarios in mixed traffic. Additionally, a lightweight Proactive Safety-based Action Refinement (PSAR) module applies rule-based corrections to accelerate training convergence. Experimental results demonstrate that DIACC significantly improves traffic efficiency and adaptability compared to rule-based and benchmark MARL models.

摘要（中文）：随着智能交通系统（ITS）的发展，联网和自动驾驶车辆（CAVs）有望通过合作策略，例如在瓶颈区域，显著减少交通拥堵。然而，在混合交通环境中，人工驾驶车辆（HDVs）行为的不确定性和多样性给CAV的合作带来了重大挑战。本文提出了一种双重交互感知协同控制（DIACC）策略，该策略增强了在混合交通瓶颈场景中联网和自动驾驶车辆（CAVs）的多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架内的局部和全局交互感知。DIACC策略包含三个关键创新点：1) 一个去中心化交互自适应决策（D-IADM）模块，通过区分CAV-CAV合作交互与CAV-HDV观察交互，增强actor的局部交互感知。2) 一个中心化交互增强评论家（C-IEC），通过交互感知的价值估计，提高评论家对全局交通的理解，为策略更新提供更准确的指导。3) 一种奖励设计，采用带有温度退火的Softmin聚合，优先考虑混合交通中交互密集的场景。此外，一个轻量级基于主动安全的行动精炼（PSAR）模块应用基于规则的校正来加速训练收敛。实验结果表明，与基于规则和基准MARL模型相比，DIACC显著提高了交通效率和适应性。

Authors: Zhengxuan Liu, Yuxin Cai, Yijing Wang, Xiangkun He, Chen Lv, Zhiqiang Zuo

Touch2Insert: Zero-Shot Peg Insertion by Touching Intersections of Peg and Hole

简要说明：该论文介绍了一种基于触觉的零样本定位销钉插入框架Touch2Insert。该框架利用高分辨率触觉图像重建横截面几何形状，并估计孔的相对姿态，并在模拟和真实世界实验中取得了可喜的成果。

TLDR: The paper introduces Touch2Insert, a tactile-based framework for zero-shot peg insertion using high-resolution tactile images to reconstruct cross-sectional geometry and estimate the relative pose of the hole, achieving promising results in both simulation and real-world experiments.

Abstract: Reliable insertion of industrial connectors remains a central challenge in robotics, requiring sub-millimeter precision under uncertainty and often without full visual access. Vision-based approaches struggle with occlusion and limited generalization, while learning-based policies frequently fail to transfer to unseen geometries. To address these limitations, we leverage tactile sensing, which captures local surface geometry at the point of contact and thus provides reliable information even under occlusion and across novel connector shapes. Building on this capability, we present \emph{Touch2Insert}, a tactile-based framework for arbitrary peg insertion. Our method reconstructs cross-sectional geometry from high-resolution tactile images and estimates the relative pose of the hole with respect to the peg in a zero-shot manner. By aligning reconstructed shapes through registration, the framework enables insertion from a single contact without task-specific training. To evaluate its performance, we conducted experiments with three diverse connectors in both simulation and real-robot settings. The results indicate that Touch2Insert achieved sub-millimeter pose estimation accuracy for all connectors in simulation, and attained an average success rate of 86.7\% on the real robot, thereby confirming the robustness and generalizability of tactile sensing for real-world robotic connector insertion.

摘要（中文）：工业连接器的可靠插入仍然是机器人领域的一项核心挑战，需要在不确定性条件下（通常在无完整视觉访问的情况下）实现亚毫米级的精度。基于视觉的方法难以应对遮挡和有限的泛化能力，而基于学习的策略常常无法迁移到未见过的几何形状。为了解决这些局限性，我们利用触觉传感，它可以捕捉接触点处的局部表面几何信息，从而在遮挡情况下以及面对新型连接器形状时，也能提供可靠的信息。在此基础上，我们提出了 \emph{Touch2Insert}，一个基于触觉的框架，用于任意销钉插入。我们的方法从高分辨率触觉图像重建横截面几何形状，并以零样本方式估计孔相对于销钉的相对位姿。通过配准对重建的形状进行对齐，该框架能够在无需任务特定训练的情况下，从单次接触实现插入。为了评估其性能，我们在仿真和真实机器人环境中，使用三种不同的连接器进行了实验。结果表明，对于仿真中的所有连接器，Touch2Insert都实现了亚毫米级的位姿估计精度，并且在真实机器人上获得了平均 86.7% 的成功率，从而证实了触觉传感在真实世界机器人连接器插入中的鲁棒性和泛化性。

BLOCK: An Open-Source Bi-Stage MLLM Character-to-Skin Pipeline for Minecraft

简要说明：该论文介绍了BLOCK，一个开源的pipeline，使用MLLM和一个微调的FLUX.2模型，从角色概念生成Minecraft皮肤，并采用渐进式LoRA课程来提高稳定性和效率。

TLDR: The paper introduces BLOCK, an open-source pipeline using MLLMs and a fine-tuned FLUX.2 model to generate Minecraft skins from character concepts, employing a progressive LoRA curriculum for improved stability and efficiency.

Abstract: We present \textbf{BLOCK}, an open-source bi-stage character-to-skin pipeline that generates pixel-perfect Minecraft skins from arbitrary character concepts. BLOCK decomposes the problem into (i) a \textbf{3D preview synthesis stage} driven by a large multimodal model (MLLM) with a carefully designed prompt-and-reference template, producing a consistent dual-panel (front/back) oblique-view Minecraft-style preview; and (ii) a \textbf{skin decoding stage} based on a fine-tuned FLUX.2 model that translates the preview into a skin atlas image. We further propose \textbf{EvolveLoRA}, a progressive LoRA curriculum (text-to-image $\rightarrow$ image-to-image $\rightarrow$ preview-to-skin) that initializes each phase from the previous adapter to improve stability and efficiency. BLOCK is released with all prompt templates and fine-tuned weights to support reproducible character-to-skin generation.

摘要（中文）：我们提出了 \textbf{BLOCK}，一个开源的双阶段角色到皮肤生成流程，能够从任意的角色概念生成像素完美的 Minecraft 皮肤。BLOCK 将问题分解为两个阶段：（i）\textbf{3D 预览合成阶段}，由一个大型多模态模型 (MLLM) 驱动，并采用精心设计的提示和参考模板，生成一致的双面板（正面/背面）倾斜视角 Minecraft 风格预览；以及（ii）一个基于微调 FLUX.2 模型的 \textbf{皮肤解码阶段}，将预览图像转换为皮肤图集图像。我们进一步提出了 \textbf{EvolveLoRA}，一种渐进式 LoRA 课程（文本到图像 $\rightarrow$ 图像到图像 $\rightarrow$ 预览到皮肤），它从先前的适配器初始化每个阶段，以提高稳定性和效率。BLOCK 附带所有提示模板和微调权重发布，以支持可复现的角色到皮肤生成。

NOVA3R: Non-pixel-aligned Visual Transformer for Amodal 3D Reconstruction

简要说明：NOVA3R 采用非像素对齐的视觉transformer，结合场景令牌机制和基于扩散的解码器，从无姿态图像中进行非模态3D重建，性能优于现有方法。

TLDR: NOVA3R uses a non-pixel-aligned visual transformer with a scene-token mechanism and diffusion-based decoder for amodal 3D reconstruction from unposed images, outperforming state-of-the-art methods.

Abstract: We present NOVA3R, an effective approach for non-pixel-aligned 3D reconstruction from a set of unposed images in a feed-forward manner. Unlike pixel-aligned methods that tie geometry to per-ray predictions, our formulation learns a global, view-agnostic scene representation that decouples reconstruction from pixel alignment. This addresses two key limitations in pixel-aligned 3D: (1) it recovers both visible and invisible points with a complete scene representation, and (2) it produces physically plausible geometry with fewer duplicated structures in overlapping regions. To achieve this, we introduce a scene-token mechanism that aggregates information across unposed images and a diffusion-based 3D decoder that reconstructs complete, non-pixel-aligned point clouds. Extensive experiments on both scene-level and object-level datasets demonstrate that NOVA3R outperforms state-of-the-art methods in terms of reconstruction accuracy and completeness.

摘要（中文）：我们提出了一种名为NOVA3R的有效方法，用于从一组未定位姿态的图像中，以前馈方式进行非像素对齐的3D重建。与将几何体与每条光线预测绑定的像素对齐方法不同，我们的方法学习了一种全局的、与视角无关的场景表示，从而将重建与像素对齐解耦。这解决了像素对齐3D中的两个关键限制：（1）它利用完整的场景表示恢复可见和不可见点，以及（2）它以更少的重叠区域中的重复结构产生物理上合理的几何体。为了实现这一点，我们引入了一种场景令牌机制，用于聚合未定位姿态图像中的信息，以及一个基于扩散的3D解码器，用于重建完整的、非像素对齐的点云。在场景级和物体级数据集上的大量实验表明，NOVA3R在重建精度和完整性方面均优于最先进的方法。

Authors: Weirong Chen, Chuanxia Zheng, Ganlin Zhang, Andrea Vedaldi, Daniel Cremers

DAGE: Dual-Stream Architecture for Efficient and Fine-Grained Geometry Estimation

简要说明：本文介绍了一种双流Transformer架构DAGE，用于从多视图/视频数据中高效且精细地估计几何形状。它通过使用低分辨率和高分辨率流来分离全局一致性和精细细节，从而实现了最先进的结果。

TLDR: This paper introduces DAGE, a dual-stream transformer architecture for efficient and fine-grained geometry estimation from multi-view/video data, achieving state-of-the-art results by disentangling global coherence from fine detail using low- and high-resolution streams.

Abstract: Estimating accurate, view-consistent geometry and camera poses from uncalibrated multi-view/video inputs remains challenging - especially at high spatial resolutions and over long sequences. We present DAGE, a dual-stream transformer whose main novelty is to disentangle global coherence from fine detail. A low-resolution stream operates on aggressively downsampled frames with alternating frame/global attention to build a view-consistent representation and estimate cameras efficiently, while a high-resolution stream processes the original images per-frame to preserve sharp boundaries and small structures. A lightweight adapter fuses these streams via cross-attention, injecting global context without disturbing the pretrained single-frame pathway. This design scales resolution and clip length independently, supports inputs up to 2K, and maintains practical inference cost. DAGE delivers sharp depth/pointmaps, strong cross-view consistency, and accurate poses, establishing new state-of-the-art results for video geometry estimation and multi-view reconstruction.

摘要（中文）：从无标定的多视角/视频输入中估计准确且视角一致的几何体和相机位姿仍然具有挑战性，尤其是在高空间分辨率和长时间序列下。我们提出了一种双流Transformer模型DAGE，其主要创新之处在于将全局一致性与精细细节解耦。一个低分辨率流处理大幅度下采样的帧，并采用交替帧/全局注意力机制，以构建视角一致的表示并高效地估计相机；而一个高分辨率流逐帧处理原始图像，以保留清晰的边界和小结构。一个轻量级适配器通过交叉注意力融合这些流，注入全局上下文，而不会干扰预训练的单帧路径。这种设计可以独立地缩放分辨率和剪辑长度，支持高达2K的输入，并保持实际的推理成本。DAGE能够提供锐利的深度图/点云图、强大的交叉视角一致性和准确的位姿，为视频几何估计和多视角重建建立了新的最先进水平。

Authors: Tuan Duc Ngo, Jiahui Huang, Seoung Wug Oh, Kevin Blackburn-Matzen, Evangelos Kalogerakis, Chuang Gan, Joon-Young Lee

Glass Segmentation with Fusion of Learned and General Visual Features

简要说明：本文提出了一种用于玻璃分割的新型架构，该架构使用双骨干网络方法，结合来自冻结的 DINOv3 模型的通用视觉特征和来自训练过的 Swin 模型的任务特定特征，并在多个数据集上取得了最先进的结果。

TLDR: This paper presents a novel architecture for glass segmentation using a dual-backbone approach, combining general visual features from a frozen DINOv3 model and task-specific features from a trained Swin model, achieving state-of-the-art results on several datasets.

Abstract: Glass surface segmentation from RGB images is a challenging task, since glass as a transparent material distinctly lacks visual characteristics. However, glass segmentation is critical for scene understanding and robotics, as transparent glass surfaces must be identified as solid material. This paper presents a novel architecture for glass segmentation, deploying a dual-backbone producing general visual features as well as task-specific learned visual features. General visual features are produced by a frozen DINOv3 vision foundation model, and the task-specific features are generated with a Swin model trained in a supervised manner. Resulting multi-scale feature representations are downsampled with residual Squeeze-and-Excitation Channel Reduction, and fed into a Mask2Former Decoder, producing the final segmentation masks. The architecture was evaluated on four commonly used glass segmentation datasets, achieving state-of-the-art results on several accuracy metrics. The model also has a competitive inference speed compared to the previous state-of-the-art method, and surpasses it when using a lighter DINOv3 backbone variant. The implementation source code and model weights are available at: https://github.com/ojalar/lgnet

摘要（中文）：从RGB图像中分割玻璃表面是一项具有挑战性的任务，因为玻璃作为一种透明材料，明显缺乏视觉特征。然而，玻璃分割对于场景理解和机器人技术至关重要，因为透明的玻璃表面必须被识别为固体材料。本文提出了一种用于玻璃分割的新型架构，它部署了一个双骨干网络，产生通用的视觉特征以及特定任务的学习视觉特征。通用视觉特征由冻结的DINOv3视觉基础模型生成，而特定任务的特征则由以监督方式训练的Swin模型生成。由此产生的多尺度特征表示通过残差挤压-激励通道缩减进行下采样，并馈入Mask2Former解码器，生成最终的分割掩码。该架构在四个常用的玻璃分割数据集上进行了评估，在多个精度指标上取得了最先进的结果。与先前最先进的方法相比，该模型还具有具有竞争力的推理速度，并且在使用更轻量级的DINOv3骨干网络变体时，性能超过了它。该实现的源代码和模型权重可在以下网址获得：https://github.com/ojalar/lgnet

Authors: Risto Ojala, Tristan Ellison, Mo Chen

Spectrum Shortage for Radio Sensing? Leveraging Ambient 5G Signals for Human Activity Detection

简要说明：本文介绍了一种名为环境无线电感知（ARS）的新型集成感知与通信（ISAC）方法，该方法利用环境5G信号，通过自混合射频架构和由视觉模型监督的跨模态学习框架进行人体活动识别。

TLDR: This paper introduces Ambient Radio Sensing (ARS), a novel ISAC approach that utilizes ambient 5G signals for human activity recognition using a self-mixing RF architecture and a cross-modal learning framework supervised by a vision model.

Abstract: Radio sensing in the sub-10 GHz spectrum offers unique advantages over traditional vision-based systems, including the ability to see through occlusions and preserve user privacy. However, the limited availability of spectrum in this range presents significant challenges for deploying largescale radio sensing applications. In this paper, we introduce Ambient Radio Sensing (ARS), a novel Integrated Sensing and Communications (ISAC) approach that addresses spectrum scarcity by repurposing over-the-air radio signals from existing wireless systems (e.g., 5G and Wi-Fi) for sensing applications, without interfering with their primary communication functions. ARS operates as a standalone device that passively receives communication signals, amplifies them to illuminate surrounding objects, and captures the reflected signals using a self-mixing RF architecture to extract baseband features. This hardware innovation enables robust Doppler and angular feature extraction from ambient OFDM signals. To support downstream applications, we propose a cross-modal learning framework focusing on human activity recognition, featuring a streamlined training process that leverages an off-the-shelf vision model to supervise radio model training. We have developed a prototype of ARS and validated its effectiveness through extensive experiments using ambient 5G signals, demonstrating accurate human skeleton estimation and body mask segmentation applications.

摘要（中文）：在10 GHz以下频段的无线电感知相比于传统的基于视觉的系统具有独特的优势，包括能够穿透遮挡并保护用户隐私。然而，该频段内频谱的有限可用性为大规模无线电感知应用的部署带来了重大挑战。在本文中，我们提出了一种新颖的集成感知与通信 (ISAC) 方法——环境无线电感知 (ARS)，该方法通过将现有无线系统（例如，5G 和 Wi-Fi）的空中无线电信号重新用于感知应用来解决频谱稀缺问题，同时不干扰其主要通信功能。ARS作为一个独立的设备运行，被动接收通信信号，放大这些信号以照亮周围的物体，并使用自混频射频架构捕获反射信号以提取基带特征。这项硬件创新使得能够从环境 OFDM 信号中提取鲁棒的多普勒和角度特征。为了支持下游应用，我们提出了一个专注于人体活动识别的跨模态学习框架，该框架具有简化的训练过程，利用现成的视觉模型来监督无线电模型训练。我们开发了一个 ARS 原型，并通过使用环境 5G 信号进行的大量实验验证了其有效性，实验结果展示了在人体骨骼估计和身体掩模分割应用中的准确性。

Authors: Kunzhe Song, Maxime Zingraff, Huacheng Zeng

Principled Learning-to-Communicate with Quasi-Classical Information Structures

简要说明：该论文使用去中心化随机控制的信息结构（ISs）正式化了多智能体强化学习中的学习交流（LTC）， 重点关注准经典（QC）LTC，并提供了可证明的算法和复杂度界限。 它还探讨了QC IS和与策略无关的基于共同信息的信念之间的关系。

TLDR: This paper formalizes learning-to-communicate (LTC) in multi-agent reinforcement learning using information structures (ISs) from decentralized stochastic control, focusing on quasi-classical (QC) LTCs with provable algorithms and complexity bounds. It also explores the relationship between QC IS and strategy-independent common-information-based beliefs.

Abstract: Learning-to-communicate (LTC) in partially observable environments has received increasing attention in deep multi-agent reinforcement learning, where the control and communication strategies are jointly learned. Meanwhile, the impact of communication on decision-making has been extensively studied in control theory. In this paper, we seek to formalize and better understand LTC by bridging these two lines of work, through the lens of information structures (ISs). To this end, we formalize LTC in decentralized partially observable Markov decision processes (Dec-POMDPs) under the common-information-based framework from decentralized stochastic control, and classify LTC problems based on the ISs before (additional) information sharing. We first show that non-classical LTCs are computationally intractable in general, and thus focus on quasi-classical (QC) LTCs. We then propose a series of conditions for QC LTCs, under which LTCs preserve the QC IS after information sharing, whereas violating which can cause computational hardness in general. Further, we develop provable planning and learning algorithms for QC LTCs, and establish quasi-polynomial time and sample complexities for several QC LTC examples that satisfy the above conditions. Along the way, we also establish results on the relationship between (strictly) QC IS and the condition of having strategy-independent common-information-based beliefs (SI-CIBs), as well as on solving Dec-POMDPs without computationally intractable oracles but beyond those with SI-CIBs, which may be of independent interest.

摘要（中文）：在深度多智能体强化学习中，部分可观测环境下的学习通信（LTC）问题受到了越来越多的关注，其中控制和通信策略是联合学习的。与此同时，通信对决策的影响在控制理论中得到了广泛的研究。在本文中，我们试图通过信息结构（ISs）的视角，将这两条研究路线联系起来，从而形式化并更好地理解LTC。为此，我们基于分散式随机控制的共同信息框架，将LTC形式化为分散式部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（Dec-POMDPs），并根据（额外）信息共享前的信息结构对LTC问题进行分类。我们首先证明，非经典LTC问题在一般情况下是计算上难解的，因此我们专注于准经典（QC）LTC问题。然后，我们为QC LTC问题提出了一系列条件，在这些条件下，LTC在信息共享后仍能保持QC IS，而违反这些条件通常会导致计算上的困难。此外，我们为QC LTC问题开发了可证明的规划和学习算法，并为满足上述条件的几个QC LTC示例建立了拟多项式时间和样本复杂度。在此过程中，我们还建立了关于（严格）QC IS和具有策略无关的基于共同信息的信念（SI-CIBs）的条件之间的关系，以及关于解决Dec-POMDPs问题的结果，这些问题无需计算上难解的预言机，但超出了具有SI-CIBs的问题范围，这可能具有独立的意义。

Authors: Xiangyu Liu, Haoyi You, Kaiqing Zhang

[Re] FairDICE: A Gap Between Theory And Practice

简要说明：本文是对 FairDICE 的一项复现研究，FairDICE 是一种多目标离线强化学习算法，旨在寻找目标之间的公平折衷方案。该研究发现原始实现中存在一个关键错误，导致其性能降低至行为克隆。

TLDR: This paper is a replication study of FairDICE, a multi-objective offline RL algorithm designed to find fair compromises between objectives. The study found a critical error in the original implementation that reduced its performance to behavior cloning.

Abstract: Offline Reinforcement Learning (RL) is an emerging field of RL in which policies are learned solely from demonstrations. Within offline RL, some environments involve balancing multiple objectives, but existing multi-objective offline RL algorithms do not provide an efficient way to find a fair compromise. FairDICE (see arXiv:2506.08062v2) seeks to fill this gap by adapting OptiDICE (an offline RL algorithm) to automatically learn weights for multiple objectives to e.g.\ incentivise fairness among objectives. As this would be a valuable contribution, this replication study examines the replicability of claims made regarding FairDICE. We find that many theoretical claims hold, but an error in the code reduces FairDICE to standard behaviour cloning in continuous environments, and many important hyperparameters were originally underspecified. After rectifying this, we show in experiments extending the original paper that FairDICE can scale to complex environments and high-dimensional rewards, though it can be reliant on (online) hyperparameter tuning. We conclude that FairDICE is a theoretically interesting method, but the experimental justification requires significant revision.

摘要（中文）：离线强化学习 (RL) 是一种新兴的 RL 领域，其中策略完全从演示数据中学习。在离线 RL 中，一些环境涉及平衡多个目标，但现有的多目标离线 RL 算法无法提供一种有效的方法来找到公平的妥协。FairDICE (参见 arXiv:2506.08062v2) 旨在通过调整 OptiDICE (一种离线 RL 算法) 来自动学习多个目标的权重，例如激励目标之间的公平性，从而填补这一空白。由于这将是一个有价值的贡献，这项复现研究考察了关于 FairDICE 的论断的可复现性。我们发现许多理论论断成立，但代码中的一个错误将 FairDICE 在连续环境中简化为标准行为克隆，并且许多重要的超参数最初未得到充分指定。在纠正这个问题后，我们在扩展原始论文的实验中表明，FairDICE 可以扩展到复杂的环境和高维奖励，尽管它可能依赖于（在线）超参数调整。我们得出结论，FairDICE 是一种理论上有趣的方法，但实验验证需要进行重大修改。
